# The One with Ross's Wedding: Part 1
> *罗斯婚礼上的那一个：第一部分*

The One With Ross’s Wedding Parts I and II
> 有罗斯婚礼第一部分和第二部分的那个

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="0.0" duration="1.296" /> **Part I Written by:** Michael Borkow
> Michael Borkow

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1.296" duration="3.768" /> **Part II Teleplay by:** Shana Goldberg-Meehan &amp; Scott Silveri
> 沙娜·戈德伯格·米汉和斯科特·西尔维尼

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="5.064" duration="2.904" /> **Part II Story by:** Jill Condon &amp; Amy Toomin
> 吉尔·康登和艾米·图敏

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="7.968" duration="1.128" /> **Part I Transcribed by:** Eric Aasen
> 埃里克·艾森

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="9.096" duration="1.896" /> **Part II Transcribed by:** Aaron D. Miller
> 亚伦·D·米勒
<GrammarTip points="分词,署名惯用语">

- **Written by / Teleplay by / Story by / Transcribed by**：过去分词 + by 引出施事者，是 (Part I was) written by... 的省略——片头署名惯用分词短语（[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)）
- 分工：written by=编剧；teleplay by=电视剧本执笔；story by=故事大纲；transcribed by=听写转录；**&amp;** = and 的符号形式

</GrammarTip>

---

🎬 *Chandler and Joey's, Joey and Chandler are getting ready for the flight to London and Monica comes running in.*
> 🎬 *钱德勒和乔伊的，乔伊和钱德勒正准备飞往伦敦的航班，莫妮卡跑了进来。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：现在式表时刻表 / could 表推测 / used to do / 介词结尾的疑问句

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="10.992" duration="17.328" /> **Monica Geller:** Guys, hurry up! <Note>The flight leaves in four hours</Note>! <Note>It could take time to get a taxi</Note>! There could be traffic! The plane could leave early! <Note>When we get to London, there could be a line at customs</Note>! Come on!! (She runs back to her apartment.)
> 伙计们，快点！飞机四小时后起飞！坐出租车可能需要时间！可能会有交通堵塞！飞机可以早点起飞！当我们到达伦敦时，海关可能会排起长队！来吧（她跑回公寓。）
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,语气,副词从句">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">The flight</span> <span class="v">leaves</span> <span class="a">in four hours</span></p>

- **The flight leaves in four hours**：航班时刻是"既定事实"，用**现在式**表将来（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **could take / could be / could leave**：情态动词 could + 原形表"可能"的推测，一连三个 could 排出焦虑感（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **It could take time to get a taxi**：it 作虚主语，真正主语是后面的**不定式**（台：不定词）to get a taxi——"做某事要花……"句型 It takes (time) to do（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）
- **When we get to London, there could be a line**：时间副词从句用**现在式 get 代未来**；there could be = there be 存在句加进情态动词（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="28.32" duration="4.848" /> **Chandler Bing:** <Note>Six-hour trip</Note> to London. That’s <Note>a lot of Monica</Note>.
> 六小时的伦敦之旅。莫妮卡的话太多了。
<GrammarTip points="名词词组,幽默">

- **Six-hour trip**：数词 + 单数名词用连字符连成复合形容词——six-**hour** 不加 s（比较：a six hours' trip ❌）（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）
- **That’s a lot of Monica**：a lot of + 名词表"大量"；把 Monica 当不可数名词用，调侃"一路上全是莫妮卡的唠叨"——人名临时不可数化的幽默（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

[Cut to the girls’ apartment, Monica is putting things into her purse as Phoebe and Rachel watch.]
> [切到女孩公寓，菲比和瑞秋看着莫妮卡把东西放进钱包。]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="33.168" duration="6.84" /> **Monica Geller:** <Note>Passport, check</Note>! (As she puts away each item, she says check.) Camera, check! Traveller’s cheques, check!
> 护照，检查！（当她收起每件物品时，她说检查。）相机，检查！旅行支票，支票！
<GrammarTip points="省略">

- **Passport, check!**：清点口号是紧缩省略句 = (The) passport (is) checked! 名词 + check"已核对"，一项一项报平安；traveller’s cheques 旅行支票（英式拼写 cheque）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="40.008" duration="2.4" /> **Rachel Green:** <Note>Who are you saying "check" too</Note>?
> 你说的“支票”也是谁？
<GrammarTip points="原文勘误,介词">

- ⚠️ 原文句尾 **too** 应为 **to**（同音误写）：say sth **to** sb"对某人说某话"
- **Who are you saying "check" to?**：疑问句中**介词**（台：介系词）to 留在句尾、宾语 who 提前——口语语序；正式写法是 To whom are you saying "check"?（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)/[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="42.408" duration="11.568" /> **Monica Geller:** <Note>Myself</Note>. Y’know for remembering to pack a thing. Yeah, <Note>you do a good thing, you get a check</Note>! (pause) My mom does it, <Note>I never realized it was weird</Note>.
> 我自己。你知道，记得收拾东西。是的，你做了一件好事，你得到了一张支票！（停顿）是我妈妈做的，我从没意识到这很奇怪。
<GrammarTip points="省略,副词从句,名词从句">

- **Myself**：独词答句 = (I say it to) myself 的省略；反身代词单独作答很口语（[Ch.16](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter16.md)）
- **you do a good thing, you get a check**：两个分句逗号相连 = **If** you do a good thing, you get a check——口语里 if 常省掉，用陈述语序表条件（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- **I never realized (that) it was weird**：宾语从句省略 that；realized 是过去式，从句 was 跟着用过去式（时态呼应）（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="53.976" duration="6.936" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Yeah, my mom <Note>used to put her head in the oven</Note>. Well, actually, <Note>she only did it the one time</Note>. But it was pretty weird.
> 是的，我妈妈过去常把头放在烤箱里。事实上，她只做了一次。但这很奇怪。
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,副词">

- **used to put her head in the oven**：used to + 原形"过去常常（现在不了）"；菲比先说得像惯常行为，再自己拆台（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **she only did it the one time**：did it 是代动词，替代 put her head in the oven；**the one time**"就那么一次"，only 限定范围——黑色幽默全靠这个限定（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）
- **pretty weird**：pretty 作程度副词"相当"，修饰形容词（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="60.912" duration="1.44" /> **Ross Geller:** (entering) Hey!
> （进来）嘿！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="62.352" duration="1.488" /> **Monica Geller:** Hey!
> 嘿！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="63.84" duration="3.144" /> **Ross Geller:** Hey! <Note>Are you ready yet</Note>?
> 嘿！你准备好了吗？
<GrammarTip points="副词">

- **Are you ready yet?**：yet"已经……（了吗）"用于疑问句和否定句句尾；be ready 是系表结构表状态，不必用完成式（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="66.984" duration="3.456" /> **Monica Geller:** Yep! <Note>You got the tickets</Note>?
> 是的！你拿到票了吗？
<GrammarTip points="疑问句">

- **You got the tickets?**：陈述语序 + 升调表疑问的口语句 = Have you got the tickets?；got 在此 = have got"拿到/持有"的口语缩略（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="70.44" duration="3.984" /> **Ross Geller:** Oh! <Note>Got ‘em right here</Note>, (Pats his coat pocket) check!
> 哦！就在这里，（拍拍他的外套口袋）支票！
<GrammarTip points="省略,弱读">

- **Got ‘em right here**：= (I've) got them right here——句首 I've 省略；**‘em** 是 them 的弱读缩略形式，剧本用左单引号标示被吞掉的 th（[Ch.16](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter16.md)）

</GrammarTip>

[Cut to the guys’ apartment.]
> [转到男生公寓。]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="74.424" duration="4.224" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** It’s all London, baby! <Note>Here we go</Note>. (He takes a picture of a less than enthused Chandler and starts towards the girls’ apartment.)
> 都是伦敦，宝贝！我们开始吧。（他给一个不那么热情的钱德勒拍了一张照片，然后朝女孩的公寓走去。）
<GrammarTip points="倒装">

- **Here we go**：地点副词 here 置句首，主语是代词时**半倒装**（Here we go，不是 Here go we）——"我们出发喽"的固定口号（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）
- **It’s all London**：= It's all about London / London is all (that matters)，"接下来全是伦敦"的兴奋宣告

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="78.648" duration="2.184" /> **Chandler Bing:** You got your passport?
> 你拿到护照了吗？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="80.832" duration="5.496" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Yeah, <Note>in my third drawer on my dresser</Note>. <Note>You don’t want to lose that</Note>.
> 是的，在我梳妆台的第三个抽屉里。你不想失去它。
<GrammarTip points="介词,惯用语">

- **in my third drawer on my dresser**：介词层次——**in** the drawer（抽屉里头）/ **on** the dresser（台面上）；third 序数词前有限定词 my 故不加 the（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）
- **You don’t want to lose that**：口语忠告句型 = "你可别把它弄丢了"（字面"你不想弄丢它"），其实是在提醒对方别犯傻——果然护照真没带（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

*(Chandler glares at him. At first Joey doesn’t know why, it takes him a little bit to figure it out.)*
> *（钱德勒瞪着他。起初乔伊不知道为什么，他花了一点时间才弄明白。）*

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="86.328" duration="1.368" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Ohh!! (Runs to his room.)
> 哦！！（跑向他的房间。）

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="87.696" duration="1.824" /> **Chandler Bing:** <Note>There it is</Note>.
> 在那里。
<GrammarTip points="倒装,惯用语">

- **There it is**：there 置句首的感叹句式，代词主语故不倒装成 *There is it*；此处是"（我等的那反应）终于来了"的得意口吻，也可表"找到了/果然如此"（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）

</GrammarTip>

Opening Credits
> 期初学分

---

🎬 *Monica and Rachel's, continued from earlier, Monica is telling Phoebe where everything is.*
> 🎬 *莫妮卡和瑞秋家，从早些时候开始继续，莫妮卡告诉菲比一切都在哪里。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：if 条件句现在式 / be gonna 将来 / Why don't you... 建议句型 / 双宾语

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="89.52" duration="8.784" /> **Monica Geller:** Okay, <Note>if you need the vacuum</Note>, it’s in my closet <Note>on the left-hand side</Note>. Ah, the garbage bags are <Note>next to the refrigerator</Note>…
> 好的，如果你需要吸尘器，它在我衣橱的左手边。啊，垃圾袋在冰箱旁边…
<GrammarTip points="副词从句,介词">

- **if you need the vacuum**：if 条件从句用**现在式代未来**（主句 it's... 也是现在式表"位置事实"）（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- **on the left-hand side / next to the refrigerator**：方位介词 on（在……边上）与复合介词 next to（紧挨）；left-hand side"左手边"是复合名词（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="98.304" duration="4.968" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Okay, okay, but <Note>Rachel’s gonna be here</Note> too, <Note>can’t I just ask her this stuff</Note>?
> 好吧，好吧，但瑞秋也会在这里，我能不能问问她这件事？
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,疑问句,双宾语">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">can’t ask</span> <span class="o">her</span> <span class="o">this stuff</span></p>

- **Rachel’s gonna be here**：gonna = going to，be gonna + 原形表最近将来（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **can’t I just ask her this stuff?**：否定疑问句表"难道不行吗"的商量口吻；**ask her this stuff** 是 S+V+O+O 双宾语（人 + 物）（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="103.272" duration="2.928" /> **Monica Geller:** Yeah, okay, <Note>give that a try</Note>!
> 是啊，好吧，试试看！
<GrammarTip points="惯用语,双宾语">

- **give that a try**：习语 give it/that a try"试试看"——give + 间接宾语 that + 直接宾语 a try 的双宾语结构；莫妮卡赌瑞秋答不上来，语气带挑衅（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="106.2" duration="3.288" /> **Chandler Bing:** (entering, with Joey) All right! <Note>Let’s do it</Note>!
> （进来，和乔伊一起）好吧！让我们做吧！
<GrammarTip points="祈使句">

- **Let’s do it!**：Let's + 原形"咱们（一起）做吧"，表提议的祈使句型；let's 包含听话人，区别于 Let me（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="109.488" duration="1.656" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Woo-hoo!!
> 呜呼！！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="111.144" duration="2.232" /> **Ross Geller:** Yeah, cheerie-o!
> 是的，干杯！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="113.376" duration="1.992" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** London baby!
> 伦敦宝贝！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="115.368" duration="3.216" /> **Chandler Bing:** Okay, ‘cause <Note>that’s not gonna get annoying</Note>.
> 好吧，因为这不会让人讨厌的。
<GrammarTip points="副词从句,系动词,反讽">

- **‘cause (that’s not gonna...)**：'cause = because 的口语缩略，句首引导原因从句——"（好吧，你尽管喊）因为这才不会烦人呢"，实为反讽（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- **get annoying**：get 作**系动词**（台：连缀动词）"变得"，后接形容词 annoying 作补语（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="118.584" duration="1.992" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** (louder) London baby!!
> （大声点）伦敦宝贝！！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="120.576" duration="3.888" /> **Chandler Bing:** Hey, y’know what? <Note>I was wrong</Note>.
> 嘿，你知道吗？我错了。
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,话语标记">

- **y’know what?**：引起下文的话语标记，= Do you know what? 的缩略
- **I was wrong**：用过去式"我（刚才）错了"——刚下的判断马上收回，英语习惯用过去式指刚发生的事（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="124.464" duration="4.68" /> **Ross Geller:** Well, <Note>we’re all here</Note>! <Note>I guess we should get going</Note>!
> 好吧，我们都在这里！我想我们该走了！
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,惯用语">

- **we’re all here**：all 作主语 we 的同位语，"我们全都到了"（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）
- **I guess (that) we should get going**：guess 后接宾语从句省略 that；**get going** = 动身、出发（get + 现在分词的固定搭配）（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="129.144" duration="5.496" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Ohhh, <Note>I wanna come over there and give you a hug</Note> and <Note>wish you luck on your wedding</Note>, but I don’t—can’t get up.
> 哦，我想过来给你一个拥抱，祝你婚礼好运，但我起不来。
<GrammarTip points="不定词,双宾语">

- **wanna come... and give... and wish...**：wanna = want to，一个 to 管三个并列不定式 come / give / wish（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)）
- **wish you luck on your wedding**：wish + 人 + 物双宾语"祝你……"（wish you luck / wish you a happy birthday 同类）（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）
- **I don’t—can’t get up**：说到一半自我修正，破折号表话语中断重来

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="134.64" duration="2.832" /> **Ross Geller:** Oh, I’ll-I’ll come hug you.
> 哦，我会来拥抱你。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="137.472" duration="3.144" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Great! Yeah, <Note>could you bring me the newspaper</Note>?
> 太好了！是的，你能把报纸给我吗？
<GrammarTip points="语气,双宾语">

- **Could you bring me the newspaper?**：could 比 can 更委婉的请求句型；**bring me the newspaper** 双宾语 = bring the newspaper **to** me（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="140.616" duration="1.368" /> **Ross Geller:** Yeah. (He does so and Phoebe hugs him.)
> 是的。（他这样做了，菲比拥抱了他。）

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="141.984" duration="1.68" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Oh, have a great wedding!
> 哦，祝你的婚礼愉快！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="143.664" duration="1.608" /> **Ross Geller:** Thanks.
> 谢谢。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="145.272" duration="2.4" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Oh, hey, Chandler I wanna hug you too!
> 哦，嘿，钱德勒，我也想拥抱你！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="147.672" duration="1.44" /> **Chandler Bing:** Hey! (Trots over)
> 嘿！（小跑过去）

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="149.112" duration="5.328" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Oh, and great! <Note>You might as well bring me my book</Note>, it’s on the counter in your apartment.
> 哦，太好了！你最好把我的书带来，它在你公寓的柜台上。
<GrammarTip points="语气">

- **might as well + 原形**："不妨、干脆"——反正都要跑一趟，顺手把书也拿来；as well 后不加 to（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **bring me my book**：又是双宾语；菲比得寸进尺的喜剧节奏全靠这几个礼貌句型堆出来（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="154.44" duration="1.368" /> **Chandler Bing:** Oh. (Goes and gets Phoebe’s book as Rachel comes in from her room.)
> 哦。（当瑞秋从她的房间进来时，她去拿菲比的书。）

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="155.808" duration="2.4" /> **Ross Geller:** (to Rachel) So, <Note>we’re off</Note>.
> （对瑞秋）那么，我们走了。
<GrammarTip points="介词,惯用语">

- **we’re off**：be off = 出发、离开——off 是副词小品词作补语；"So, we're off." 是告辞开场的固定说法（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="158.208" duration="1.68" /> **Rachel Green:** Have fun!
> 玩得开心！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="159.888" duration="4.824" /> **Ross Geller:** Thanks! (They hug.) Ugh, <Note>I can’t believe you’re not gonna be there</Note>!
> 谢谢！（他们拥抱）啊，真不敢相信你不在那里！
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,否定前移">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">can’t believe</span> <span class="o">(that) you’re not gonna be there</span></p>

- **I can’t believe (that)...**：believe 后接宾语从句，that 常省；从句的否定 you're **not** gonna be there 保留在从句内——"不敢相信你不去"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="164.712" duration="2.04" /> **Rachel Green:** Oh, I know.
> 哦，我知道。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="166.752" duration="3.84" /> **Ross Geller:** So-so come! <Note>Why don’t you come</Note>?
> 来吧！你为什么不来？
<GrammarTip points="语气,疑问句">

- **Why don’t you come?**：Why don't you + 原形是**建议句型**"你何不一起来？"——不是质问原因，而是热情邀请（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="170.592" duration="1.464" /> **Rachel Green:** What?!
> 什么？！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="172.056" duration="7.272" /> **Ross Geller:** To London! <Note>Come to London</Note>. Please? <Note>It’ll mean so much to me</Note>.
> 去伦敦！来伦敦吧。请这对我意义重大。
<GrammarTip points="祈使句,介词">

- **Come to London**：祈使句省略主语 you；前一句 To London! 是介词短语独用表感叹（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **It’ll mean so much to me**：mean (a lot / so much) to sb"对某人意义重大"；will 将来式表"（如果来了）将会"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="179.328" duration="3.528" /> **Rachel Green:** Yeah, well, <Note>I gotta work</Note>, I’m sorry.
> 是啊，我得工作了，对不起。
<GrammarTip points="语气">

- **I gotta work**：gotta = have got to = have to"不得不"——三层口语缩略：I've gotta work → I gotta work（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="182.856" duration="3.168" /> **Ross Geller:** Why-why <Note>can’t you take a couple of days off</Note>?
> 你为什么不能休息几天？
<GrammarTip points="疑问句,短语动词">

- **Why can’t you...?**：否定疑问句追问"为什么不能……"，比 Why don't you 多一层不解/纠缠
- **take a couple of days off**：take + 时段 + off"请……假"；a couple of + 复数名词"两三个"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="186.024" duration="6.48" /> **Rachel Green:** Because, I can’t! Ross, <Note>I told you</Note>, no. I can’t.
> 因为，我不能！罗斯，我告诉过你，不行。
<GrammarTip points="副词从句,动词时态">

- **Because, I can’t!**：because 独词起句回答 Why 问句——口语里常见，书面应避免半截从句（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- **I told you**：过去式"我（之前）告诉过你"，强调动作已完成（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="192.504" duration="2.016" /> **Ross Geller:** This is my wedding.
> 这是我的婚礼。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="194.52" duration="9.24" /> **Monica Geller:** All right, y’know what? <Note>We really are late</Note>! Let’s go! Let’s go! Let’s go!!
> 好吧，你知道吗？我们真的迟到了！走吧！走吧！走吧！！
<GrammarTip points="强调,祈使句">

- **We really are late**：be 动词前加 really 强调"真的"；are 重读表坚持（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）
- **Let’s go! ×3**：祈使句三连重复表催促，英语口语常用重复加强语气（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="203.76" duration="4.872" /> **Ross Geller:** Fine. <Note>You’ll-you’ll watch it on video</Note> <Note>when we get back</Note>.
> 好的。我们回来的时候，你会在录像中看到的。
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,副词从句">

- **You’ll watch it on video when we get back**：主句将来式 will watch，时间从句用**现在式 get 代未来**——"主将从现"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="208.632" duration="7.632" /> **Chandler Bing:** (entering, with Phoebe’s book.) <Note>Here you go</Note> Phoebe! Here you go Phobo! Phewbedo! Phaybobo.
> （拿着菲比的书进来。）给你菲比！给你，菲波！菲贝多！法博。
<GrammarTip points="倒装,幽默">

- **Here you go**："给你"——here 置句首、代词主语不倒装的交接惯用语（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）
- **Phobo / Phewbedo / Phaybobo**：故意把 Phoebe 的名字越念越离谱，名字游戏式幽默，无词义

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="216.264" duration="0.936" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** (laughs) Thank you.
> （笑）谢谢你。

(Chandler kneels down with his arms spread waiting for his hug.
> （钱德勒张开双臂跪下，等待他的拥抱。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="217.2" duration="0.936" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Thank you. (She pats him on his head.)
> 谢谢。（她拍了拍他的头。）

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="218.136" duration="4.632" /> **Ross Geller:** All right, let’s go! Bye, Pheebs!
> 好吧，我们走吧！再见，菲比！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="222.768" duration="2.208" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Bye, Pheebs!
> 再见，菲比！

*(They all start out, Rachel gives each one a kiss, and says "bye." In the hall, Joey says…)*
> *（他们都出发了，瑞秋给每个人一个吻，然后说“再见”。在大厅里，乔伊说……）*

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="224.976" duration="1.992" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** London baby!! (And Rachel slowly closes the door, sadly.)
> 伦敦宝贝！！（瑞秋伤心地慢慢关上门。）

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="226.968" duration="3.696" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Oh, do you need a hug? <Note>You don’t have to bring me anything</Note>!
> 哦，你需要拥抱吗？你不必给我带任何东西！
<GrammarTip points="语气">

- **You don’t have to bring me anything**：**don't have to** = 不必（≠ must not"禁止"）；anything 用于否定句，"什么都不必带"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

*(And with that, television history is made as, for the first time ever, an entire show moves it’s entire production to an entirely different country to make a single episode. We get shots of Buckingham Palace, London Bridge, Big Ben, and the London Marriott as Joey and Chandler exit.)*
> *（这样一来，电视史就有了新的记录，因为有史以来第一次，整个节目将其整个制作转移到一个完全不同的国家来制作一集。我们拍摄了白金汉宫、伦敦桥、大本钟以及乔伊和钱德勒离开时的伦敦万豪酒店。）*

---

🎬 *Street in front of the London Marriott, Joey and Chandler exit. Joey is carrying a video camera and is shooting Chandler.*
> 🎬 *伦敦万豪酒店前的街道，乔伊和钱德勒出口。乔伊拿着摄像机正在拍摄钱德勒。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：祈使句 / 现在进行式 / 附加疑问句 / make + 宾语 + 原形补语

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="230.664" duration="3.168" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Come on! <Note>Do something</Note>!
> 快点！做点什么！
<GrammarTip points="祈使句,名词词组">

- **Do something!**：祈使句省略主语 you；**something** 复合不定代词，形容词修饰它要后置（something interesting）（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="233.832" duration="2.616" /> **Chandler Bing:** I am, <Note>I’m ignoring you</Note>.
> 是的，我不理你。
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,省略">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">’m ignoring</span> <span class="o">you</span></p>

- **I’m ignoring you**：现在进行式"正在不理你"——把"无视"说成一个正在进行的大动作，冷幽默（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **I am**：= I am doing something 的省略应答，只留助动词 be（[Ch.16](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter16.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="236.448" duration="8.304" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Okay, here! (Gives him the camera.) I wanna be the on camera guy. All right, <Note>first stop, Westminster Abbey</Note>. (Joey folds out his "pop-up" map of London. All of the major landmarks pop-up like in a pop-up book.)
> 好的，在这里！（把相机给他。）我想当一名摄影师。好的，第一站，威斯敏斯特大教堂。（乔伊折叠他的“弹出式”伦敦地图。所有主要地标都像弹出式书本一样弹出。）
<GrammarTip points="同位语">

- **first stop, Westminster Abbey**：Westminster Abbey 作 first stop 的同位语，逗号相连"第一站，（也就是）威斯敏斯特大教堂"（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）
- **the on camera guy**：on camera 介词短语直接作定语"镜头前的"，口播行业说法

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="244.752" duration="2.64" /> **Chandler Bing:** Oh, <Note>what the hell is that</Note>?
> 哦，那到底是什么？
<GrammarTip points="疑问句,语气">

- **what the hell is that?**：疑问词 what + 插入语 **the hell** 加强语气"到底/究竟"；同类加强词：the heck / on earth（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="247.392" duration="14.232" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** It’s London, baby! All right, the hotel’s here. (Points to the map.) Wait. No, we wanna go… No. I know. (Sets the map down.) <Note>I’m gonna have to go into the map</Note>. (So Joey literally steps into the map.)
> 是伦敦，宝贝！好了，酒店就在这里。（指着地图）等等。不，我们想去……不。我知道。（放下地图）我必须进入地图。（所以乔伊真的进入地图。）
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,语气">

- **I’m gonna have to go into the map**：be gonna（将来）+ have to（不得不）+ go into（短语动词）三层叠加——"我得钻进地图里去了"；literally 照字面执行，笑点所在（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="261.624" duration="4.968" /> **Chandler Bing:** Okay, <Note>if you see a little version of me in there</Note>? <Note>Kill it</Note>!
> 好吧，如果你在那里看到我的小版本？杀了它！
<GrammarTip points="副词从句,祈使句">

- **if you see a little version of me in there?**：if 条件从句现在式代未来；句尾用问号是把条件"随口一提"，主句 Kill it! 才是重点（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- **Kill it!**：祈使句；**a little version of me**"迷你版的我"——version of + 名词（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="266.592" duration="3.36" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** I got it! (Picks up the map and starts walking.) Here we go.
> 我明白了！（拿起地图开始走。）我们走吧。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="269.952" duration="6.312" /> **Chandler Bing:** Okay. Listen-listen, <Note>we’re not gonna have to walk this way the entire time</Note> <Note>are we</Note>?
> 好吧。听着，我们不必一直走这条路，是吗？
<GrammarTip points="附加疑问句">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">we</span> <span class="v">’re not gonna have to walk</span> <span class="a">this way the entire time</span></p>

- **..., are we?**：**附加疑问句**——主句否定（not gonna），问句用肯定 are we；"前否后肯"，语调上扬表确认（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）
- **this way the entire time**：两个副词短语连用"一直走这条路"；the entire time 名词短语作时间状语（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="276.264" duration="3.888" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Shhh! (Pause) Man, <Note>you made me lose it</Note>! (He goes into the map again.)
> 嘘！（停顿）老兄，你把它弄丢了！（他又进入地图。）
<GrammarTip points="使役动词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">you</span> <span class="v">made</span> <span class="o">me</span> <span class="c">lose it</span></p>

- **make + 宾语 + 原形 lose**：使役动词 make 的**宾语补足语**（台：补语）用原形、不加 to——"害我（方向感）又丢了"（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md) S+V+O+C）

</GrammarTip>

---

🎬 *Another street, somewhere along the River Themes, Ross, Emily, and Monica are walking to where they’re gonna get married. Emily is relating the troubles with the caterer.*
> 🎬 *另一条街，在Themes河沿岸的某个地方，罗斯、艾米莉和莫妮卡正走向他们要结婚的地方。艾米莉正在讲述与宴会承办人的麻烦。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：过去将来 was going to / 否定疑问句修辞 / 最高级 + 关系从句 / 比较级

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="280.152" duration="14.016" /> **Emily:** …and that was all before 10 o’clock. <Note>The caterer rang and said it was going to be Chicken Kiev</Note> instead of Chicken Tarragon. And then the florist <Note>phoned to say there aren’t any tulips</Note>. Oh, and the chilliest has carpel-tunel syndrome. We’re not gonna be…
> ……那是10点之前的事了。宴会承办人打来电话说是基辅鸡而不是龙蒿鸡。然后花店打电话说没有郁金香。哦，最冷的有心皮调音综合症。我们不会…
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,不定词,原文勘误">

- **rang and said (that) it was going to be...**：两个并列过去式；said 后宾语从句用 **was going to**——从过去看"将要"，过去将来（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **instead of + 名词**："而不是"；phoned **to say**... 不定式表目的（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)/[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）
- **there aren’t any tulips**：any 用于否定句，"一朵郁金香都没有"（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）
- ⚠️ 原文 **the chilliest has carpel-tunel syndrome** 是听写走样：应为 **the cellist has carpal-tunnel syndrome**"大提琴手得了腕管综合症"——婚礼乐手也倒下了

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="294.168" duration="4.536" /> **Ross Geller:** Whoa-whoa-whoa! Emily, (Gives her the time-out signal.) honey, okay?
> 哇哇！艾米丽，（给她暂停信号。）亲爱的，好吗？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="298.704" duration="1.296" /> **Emily:** Well, <Note>up yours</Note> too!
> 好吧，你也来吧！
<GrammarTip points="惯用语">

- **up yours (too)**：粗俗回敬语"你也一样/去你的"——Emily 把 Ross 的 time-out 手势误读成侮辱手势，才甩出这句；语用误判造成的笑点（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="300.0" duration="6.288" /> **Ross Geller:** What?! No! No! <Note>That’s-that’s time-out</Note>!
> 什么？！不！不！那-那是暂停！
<GrammarTip points="名词词组">

- **That’s time-out**：time-out 体育/亲子用语"暂停"（T 形手势），此处作名词补语；Ross 急着澄清手势含义（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="306.288" duration="0.936" /> **Emily:** Sorry.
> 对不起。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="307.224" duration="8.64" /> **Ross Geller:** Sweetie, <Note>you gotta relax</Note>. <Note>Everything’s gonna be great</Note>, okay? Come on. Come on.
> 亲爱的，你要放松。一切都会好起来的，好吗？来吧，来吧。
<GrammarTip points="语气,主谓一致">

- **you gotta relax**：gotta = have got to"得"；**Everything’s gonna be great**：复合不定代词 everything 永远配单数动词——**主谓一致**（台：主语动词一致性）（[Ch.11](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter11.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="315.864" duration="1.896" /> **Monica Geller:** Chicken Kiev?
> 基辅鸡？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="317.76" duration="4.704" /> **Ross Geller:** Um-hmm! <Note>Doesn’t that sound delicious</Note> at the last minute?
> 嗯哼！在最后一刻听起来不是很好吃吗？
<GrammarTip points="疑问句,系动词,反讽">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">that</span> <span class="v">Doesn’t sound</span> <span class="c">delicious</span></p>

- **Doesn’t that sound delicious?**：否定疑问句表修辞强调"难道不香吗"；**sound** 在此是系动词（台：连缀动词）"听起来"，后接形容词 delicious 作补语——Ross 实为反讽：临婚礼前换菜哪有好吃的（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **at the last minute**：固定介词短语"在最后一刻"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="322.464" duration="13.176" /> **Monica Geller:** Yeah, y’know, but something like salmon <Note>which would be so much more elegant than the chicken</Note>. And, <Note>you wouldn’t have to worry about the salmonella</Note>. (Ross pushes her.) So, <Note>I can’t wait to see this place you’re getting married</Note>!
> 是的，你知道，但是像鲑鱼这样的东西会比鸡肉优雅得多。而且，你不用担心沙门氏菌。（罗斯推了推她。）所以，我迫不及待地想看看你结婚的地方！
<GrammarTip points="关系从句,形容词比较,不定词">

- **salmon which would be so much more elegant than the chicken**：**定语从句**（台：关系从句）which... 修饰 salmon；比较级 more elegant **than**，程度用 **so much** 加强（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)/[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)）
- **wouldn’t have to worry about the salmonella**：wouldn't have to"就不必"（委婉设想）；worry about + 名词（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **can’t wait to see**：迫不及待做某事 can't wait to do（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）
- **this place (that) you’re getting married (at)**：定语从句修饰 place，关系词作介词 at 的宾语故省略——口语常把介词一起吞掉（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="335.64" duration="8.208" /> **Ross Geller:** Yeah. Yeah. Yes, this place is beautiful. <Note>Emily’s parents got married here</Note>.
> 是的。是的。是的，这个地方很漂亮。艾米丽的父母在这里结婚了。
<GrammarTip points="分词,惯用语">

- **got married (here)**：get + 过去分词 married 表"结婚"这一状态变化——get married 是固定搭配（[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="343.848" duration="7.848" /> **Emily:** I still can’t believe <Note>they’re tearing it down</Note>. It really is <Note>the most lovely building you’ll ever see</Note>. I mean it’s over… (She stops suddenly, when she sees that demolition has already started.) Oh my God!
> 我还是不敢相信他们要拆掉它。这真是你见过的最可爱的建筑。我的意思是它已经结束了…（当她看到拆除已经开始时，她突然停了下来。）哦，我的天啊！
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,关系从句,形容词比较">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="343.848" duration="7.848" />

**原句**：It really is the most lovely building you'll ever see.

**层次划分**：

- 主干：<p class="svoc"><span class="s">It</span> <span class="v">is</span> <span class="c">the most lovely building</span></p> 主系表结构，really 副词加强（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）
- **(that) you'll ever see**：定语从句修饰 building，关系代词 that 作 see 的宾语故省略；**最高级 + ever** 的固定搭配"你这辈子见过的最……"，从句用 will 看"今后一生"（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)/[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)）
- **they’re tearing it down**：现在进行式表"已定的计划"——他们就要拆它了（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

**翻译**：这真的是你一辈子能见到的最可爱的建筑了。

**语法要点**：最高级 + ever 从句（Ch.7）；关系代词作宾语可省（Ch.14）；进行式表已定计划（Ch.3）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="351.696" duration="1.776" /> **Monica Geller:** It’s nice.
> 很好。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="353.472" duration="1.368" /> **Emily:** Oh. Oh. (She starts running towards the building.)
> 哦。哦。（她开始朝大楼跑去。）

[Cut to inside.]
> [切到内部。]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="354.84" duration="4.2" /> **Emily:** (running in, with Ross) Oh. Oh my God. <Note>How can this be happening</Note>? What are we going to do?
> （和罗斯一起跑进来）哦，天哪，怎么会这样？我们该怎么办？
<GrammarTip points="疑问句,动词时态">

- **How can this be happening?**：疑问词 how + 情态动词 can + 进行式 be happening——"这怎么可能正在发生？"，进行式让"发生"有了眼前直播感（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **What are we going to do?**：be going to 表"打算/将要"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="359.04" duration="4.344" /> **Ross Geller:** It’s all right! Everything’s gonna be all right.
> 没事的！一切都会好起来的。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="363.384" duration="1.512" /> **Emily:** How’s it gonna be all right?!
> 怎么会没事？！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="364.896" duration="2.376" /> **Ross Geller:** Uh-huh, I see that.
> 嗯，我明白了。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="367.272" duration="4.728" /> **Monica Geller:** (entering) Okay, I talked to the guy with the shovel and <Note>I found out what happened</Note>.
> （进来）好吧，我和那个拿铲子的人谈过了，我知道发生了什么事。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,介词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">found out</span> <span class="o">what happened</span></p>

- **found out what happened**：短语动词 find out + **what 引导的宾语从句**；what 在从句中作主语，语序不倒装（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **the guy with the shovel**：介词短语 with the shovel 作后置定语"拿铲子的那位"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="372.0" duration="2.784" /> **Ross Geller:** What? What?
> 什么？什么？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="374.784" duration="2.856" /> **Monica Geller:** <Note>They torn it down</Note> a few days early.
> 他们提前几天把它拆了。
<GrammarTip points="原文勘误,动词时态">

- ⚠️ 原文 **They torn it down**：torn 是过去分词，不能单用——规范说法应为 **They tore it down**（过去式）或 They've torn it down（现在完成式）；剧本如实记下了口语中的过去式/分词混用（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **a few days early**：名词短语作时间状语"提前了几天"；tear down 拆毁（短语动词）（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

---

🎬 *Westminster Abbey, Joey and Chandler have successfully navigated the streets of London and are approaching the Abbey.*
> 🎬 *威斯敏斯特大教堂，乔伊和钱德勒已经成功地在伦敦的街道上航行，并正在接近教堂。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：最高级 + 现在完成式 / think of + 动名词 / 介词前置的疑问省略句

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="377.64" duration="12.576" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** All right! Westminster Abbey! <Note>Hands down</Note>, <Note>best Abbey I’ve ever seen</Note>. Hey! (Pushes Chandler in front of the camera.) Okay. <Note>What do you think of the Abbey</Note>, Chandler?
> 好的！威斯敏斯特大教堂！把手放下，我见过的最好的大教堂。嘿！（把钱德勒推到镜头前）好的。你觉得这座大教堂怎么样，钱德勒？
<GrammarTip points="惯用语,形容词比较,动词时态">

- **Hands down**：习语"毫无疑问地、轻松取胜"（源自赛马领先到可以放手），此处作评注性状语（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）
- **best Abbey (that) I’ve ever seen**：最高级 + 定语从句（台：关系从句），that 作 seen 的宾语故省略；从句用**现在完成式** I've seen——"到目前为止见过的"（[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)/[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **What do you think of...?**：征求意见固定句型 = How do you like...?（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="390.216" duration="7.512" /> **Chandler Bing:** I think it’s great. It’s great. Y’know, <Note>they’re thinking of changing the name</Note> of this place.
> 我觉得很好。很好。你知道，他们正在考虑改变这个地方的名字。
<GrammarTip points="动名词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">they</span> <span class="v">’re thinking of</span> <span class="o">changing the name</span></p>

- **think of + 动名词 changing**："考虑做某事"用 think of **doing**；of 是介词，后接动名词作宾语（[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md)）
- 现在进行式 they're thinking 表"正在酝酿中"——Chandler 在编段子（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="397.728" duration="3.024" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Really? <Note>To what</Note>?
> 真的吗？为了什么？
<GrammarTip points="疑问句,介词">

- **To what?**：介词**前置**的疑问省略句 = To what (are they thinking of changing the name)?——承接上句 change the name **to**...；口语更常说 What...to?（句尾介词），Joey 这问法反而显得正式（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)/[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="400.752" duration="2.352" /> **Chandler Bing:** <Note>To Put the Camera Away</Note>!!!
> 把相机收起来！！！
<GrammarTip points="祈使句,短语动词">

- **(To) Put the Camera Away**：答句沿用对方的 To...?，把一个祈使句当"新名字"抛回去；put away = 收起来（短语动词，宾语为名词时可分可合：put the camera away = put away the camera）（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="403.104" duration="3.12" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Man, you are <Note>Westminster Crabby</Note>. (He starts chasing Chandler towards the Abbey.)
> 老兄，你是威斯敏斯特暴躁的人。（他开始朝修道院追钱德勒。）
<GrammarTip points="幽默,构词">

- **Westminster Crabby**：谐音梗——Crabby（暴躁的）押韵 Abbey；crab"爱发牢骚的人"+ 形容词后缀 -y 构成 crabby（[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)）

</GrammarTip>

---

🎬 *Monica and Rachel's, Phoebe is trying to move off of the couch as Rachel enters.*
> 🎬 *莫妮卡和瑞秋家，瑞秋进来时菲比正试图离开沙发。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：should have done 懊悔句 / 关系从句嵌套 / 强调助动词 do / as...as 同级比较

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="406.224" duration="1.152" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Oh. Oh.
> 哦，哦。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="407.376" duration="5.256" /> **Rachel Green:** Oh, honey! <Note>Don’t get up</Note>! What do you need?
> 哦，亲爱的！别起来！你需要什么？
<GrammarTip points="祈使句">

- **Don’t get up!**：否定祈使句 Don't + 原形——"别起来"；get up 短语动词（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="412.632" duration="1.968" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Oh, no. Oh, nothing.
> 哦，不。哦，没什么。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="414.6" duration="7.2" /> **Rachel Green:** Come on! <Note>I am here to take care of you</Note>! What do you need? Anything.
> 来吧！我是来照顾你的！你需要什么？什么都行。
<GrammarTip points="不定词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">am</span> <span class="a">here</span> <span class="a">to take care of you</span></p>

- **I am here to take care of you**：不定式（台：不定词）to take care of you 作目的状语——"我来就是为了照顾你"；take care of = 照顾（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="421.8" duration="1.488" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Okay, I have a wedgie.
> 好的，我有一只威奇。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="423.288" duration="2.592" /> **Rachel Green:** Okay, <Note>that is all you</Note>.
> 好吧，你就这些。
<GrammarTip points="惯用语">

- **that is all you**：口语"那只能靠你自己了/这我帮不上"——all + 人称代词，字面"那全是你（的事）"；wedgie 指内裤勒进屁股的窘境，Rachel 拒绝代劳

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="425.88" duration="2.16" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** So-so, what do you want for lunch?
> 那么，你午餐想吃什么？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="428.04" duration="4.344" /> **Rachel Green:** Oh, I don’t know. <Note>I guess we have to eat</Note>.
> 哦，我不知道。我想我们得吃饭了。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">guess</span> <span class="o">(that) we have to eat</span></p>

- **I guess (that) we have to eat**：guess 后接宾语从句，that 省略；have to + 原形"不得不"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="432.384" duration="2.376" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Yeah, I do. What’s the matter?
> 是的，我知道。怎么了？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="434.76" duration="8.976" /> **Rachel Green:** I’m just <Note>bummed about the way I left things with Ross</Note>. <Note>I shouldn’t have lied to him about having to work</Note>. <Note>He seemed so mad at me</Note>.
> 我只是对我把东西留给罗斯的方式感到不安。我不该就工作的事对他撒谎。他似乎对我很生气。
<GrammarTip points="关系从句,语气,动名词">

- **the way (that) I left things with Ross**：定语从句（台：关系从句）修饰 the way，关系词省略；leave things with sb"把局面留给某人"（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)）
- **shouldn’t have lied**：**should have + 过去分词**表对过去的懊悔"（当时）本不该撒谎"——情态动词 + 完成式（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **about having to work**：介词 about 后接**动名词**短语 having to work"关于必须上班（这事）"（[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md)）
- **He seemed so mad at me**：seem 系动词 + 形容词 mad 作补语；be mad at sb"生某人的气"（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="443.736" duration="5.208" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Eh, <Note>don’t be so hard on yourself</Note>. <Note>If someone I was still in love with was getting married</Note>…
> 嗯，别对自己太苛刻了。如果我还爱着的人要结婚了…
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,关系从句,副词从句">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="443.736" duration="5.208" />

**原句**：If someone I was still in love with was getting married…

**层次划分**：

- 从句主干：<p class="svoc"><span class="a">If</span> <span class="s">someone</span> <span class="v">was getting married</span></p> if 条件副词从句，主句还没说完就被 Rachel 打断（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- **(who) I was still in love with**：定语从句修饰 someone，关系代词 who 作介词 **with** 的宾语故省略——介词留在句尾是口语语序；**be in love with sb**"爱着某人"（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **don’t be so hard on yourself**：否定祈使句；be hard on oneself"对某人苛刻"，yourself 反身代词（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

**翻译**：如果是我还爱着的人要和别人结婚了（我也会这么难受）……

**语法要点**：关系代词作介词宾语可省、介词留句尾（Ch.14/10）；if 条件从句现在/过去式表假设（Ch.13）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="448.944" duration="2.04" /> **Rachel Green:** (interrupting) <Note>Still in love with</Note>?!
> （打断）还爱你吗？！
<GrammarTip points="省略,介词">

- **Still in love with?!**：把对方从句的尾巴（介词 with 收尾）原样拎出来反问——介词短语独立成句，质问语气全靠升调（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)/[Ch.16](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter16.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="450.984" duration="0.552" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Yeah!
> 是啊！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="451.536" duration="2.424" /> **Rachel Green:** <Note>I’m not in love with Ross</Note>!
> 我不爱罗斯！
<GrammarTip points="惯用语">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">’m not</span> <span class="c">in love with Ross</span></p>

- **be in love with sb**：固定系表短语"爱着某人"——in love 介词短语作补语；否定直接在 be 后加 not（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="453.96" duration="3.936" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Oh. No. No. Good! Yeah, <Note>me neither</Note>.
> 哦。不。不。很好！是的，我也是。
<GrammarTip points="省略,惯用语">

- **me neither**："我也不（爱）"——= I'm not either 的口语紧缩；口语用宾格 me 而非 I，neither 表"也不"（[Ch.16](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter16.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="457.896" duration="39.696" /> **Rachel Green:** Phoebe, I’m going to Ross’s wedding <Note>because he is my ex-boyfriend</Note> and that would be really uncomfortable. <Note>Not because, I’m still in love with him</Note>! I mean, hey, y’know, <Note>I like Ross as much as the next guy</Note>, y’know? Clearly I have feelings for him, but <Note>feelings don’t mean love</Note>! I mean, I still have loving feelings for Ross. Yeah! But, I have, I have continuing feelings of love, but <Note>that doesn’t mean that-that I’m still in love with him</Note>. Y’know? I-I have sexual feelings for him, but <Note>I do love him</Note>—Ohh! Oh my God! Oh my—why didn’t you tell me?!!
> 菲比，我要去罗斯的婚礼，因为他是我的前男友，那会很不舒服。不是因为，我仍然爱着他！我是说，嘿，你知道，我和下一个男人一样喜欢罗斯，你知道吗？显然我对他有感觉，但感觉并不意味着爱！我是说，我对罗斯仍然有爱的感觉。是的！但是，我有，我有持续的爱的感觉，但这并不意味着我仍然爱着他。你知道吗？我对他有性的感觉，但我确实爱他哦！哦，我的天！哦，我的天！哦，你为什么不告诉我？！！
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,副词从句,形容词比较,强调">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="457.896" duration="39.696" />

**原句**：I like Ross as much as the next guy... but that doesn't mean that I'm still in love with him... but I do love him.

**层次划分**：

- **because he is my ex-boyfriend / Not because I’m still in love with him**：原因副词从句的正反对举——先说 because...，再用 Not because... 否认，辩白式结构（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- **I like Ross as much as the next guy**：<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">like</span> <span class="o">Ross</span> <span class="a">as much as the next guy</span></p> as...as 同级比较"和随便哪个人一样喜欢"；the next guy 惯用语"旁人/别人"（[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)）
- **feelings don’t mean love**：复数主语 feelings 配 don't——主谓一致；mean + 名词"意味着"（[Ch.11](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter11.md)）
- **that doesn’t mean (that) I’m still in love with him**：mean 后接宾语从句，第二个 that 省略；瑞秋嘴瓢说出的 that-that 是第一个 that（引导词）的重复（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **I **do** love him**：助动词 **do** 强调谓语"我确实爱他"——前面一路否认，到这里破防，全剧经典瞬间（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

**翻译**：我喜欢罗斯就跟喜欢别人一样……但这不代表我还爱着他……可我确实爱他——天哪！

**语法要点**：because / not because 对举（Ch.13）；as much as（Ch.7）；mean + 宾语从句（Ch.12）；强调助动词 do（Ch.9）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="497.592" duration="1.368" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** <Note>We thought you knew</Note>!
> 我们以为你知道！
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,动词时态">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">We</span> <span class="v">thought</span> <span class="o">(that) you knew</span></p>

- **We thought (that) you knew**：宾语从句省略 that；主句过去式 thought，从句用 knew 呼应（时态一致）（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="498.96" duration="1.344" /> **Rachel Green:** We?!
> 我们？！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="500.304" duration="3.384" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Yeah, we all know! We talk about it all the time!
> 是啊，我们都知道！我们一直在谈论这个！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="503.688" duration="3.456" /> **Rachel Green:** You all know? Does Ross know?
> 你们都知道吗？罗斯知道吗？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="507.144" duration="2.304" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Oh no, Ross doesn’t know anything.
> 哦，不，罗斯什么都不知道。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="509.448" duration="3.144" /> **Rachel Green:** Oh, <Note>I can not believe you didn’t tell me</Note>!
> 哦，我真不敢相信你竟然不告诉我！
<GrammarTip points="名词从句">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">can not believe</span> <span class="o">(that) you didn’t tell me</span></p>

- **I can not believe (that) you didn’t tell me**：believe 接宾语从句；can not 分写比 can't 语气更重（一字一顿）；从句用过去式 didn't tell 指"（一直）没告诉我"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="512.592" duration="7.728" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Well, because we thought you knew!! It’s so obvious! God, <Note>that would be like telling Monica</Note>, "Hey, you like things clean."
> 嗯，因为我们以为你知道！！太明显了！天啊，那就像告诉莫妮卡，“嘿，你喜欢干净的东西。”
<GrammarTip points="动名词,基本句型">

- **that would be like telling Monica**：介词 like + **动名词** telling"就像去告诉莫妮卡一样"；would be 委婉比喻（[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **you like things clean**：<p class="svoc"><span class="s">you</span> <span class="v">like</span> <span class="o">things</span> <span class="c">clean</span></p> like + 宾语 + 形容词**宾语补足语**（台：补语）"喜欢东西干干净净"（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md) S+V+O+C）

</GrammarTip>

---

🎬 *A park in London, Joey and Chandler walk up to a souvenir stand.*
> 🎬 *伦敦的一个公园，乔伊和钱德勒走向一个纪念品摊。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：介词结尾的疑问句 / how come 不倒装 / insist on + 动名词 / 使役动词 make

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="520.32" duration="1.488" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Hey!
> 嘿！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="521.808" duration="4.728" /> **The Vendor:** So, <Note>what are you guys in the market for</Note>? We’ve got uh, scarves, tulip post cards...
> 那个么，你们来市场干什么？我们有围巾、郁金香明信片。。。
<GrammarTip points="疑问句,介词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="o">What</span> <span class="v">are</span> <span class="s">you guys</span> <span class="a">in the market for</span></p>

- **What are you guys in the market for?**：be in the market for"正想买……"；疑问词 what 是介词 **for** 的宾语提前，for 留在句尾——口语语序（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)/[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）
- **We’ve got...**：= We have got"我们有"，口语 have got 表现在有（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="526.536" duration="8.28" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** <Note>Check this out</Note>? Huh? (Joey has this big tall hat with a British flag on the front of it.) Yeah. <Note>That’s the stuff</Note>. What do you think?
> 看看这个？哈？（乔伊有一顶大而高的帽子，前面有一面英国国旗。）是的。就是这些东西。你觉得怎么样？
<GrammarTip points="短语动词,名词词组">

- **Check this out**：check out"看看"——可分短语动词，代词宾语 this 必须夹中间（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **That’s the stuff**："这才是好货"——stuff 不可数名词"东西/货色"（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="534.816" duration="4.872" /> **Chandler Bing:** Well, <Note>I don’t have to buy that, "I’m with stupid" T-shirt anymore</Note>.
> 嗯，我再也不用买那件“我和傻瓜在一起”的t恤了。
<GrammarTip points="副词,定语">

- **not...anymore**："再也不……"，anymore 置句尾（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）
- **that, "I’m with stupid" T-shirt**：同位语解说——整句话 "I'm with stupid" 直接作 T-shirt 的定语（印在 T 恤上的标语）；潜台词：这顶帽子自带同样效果（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="539.688" duration="3.864" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Well, I like it. Here you go. (He pays for the hat.)
> 嗯，我喜欢。给你。（他付帽子的钱。）

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="543.552" duration="7.152" /> **Chandler Bing:** All right, look, <Note>you’re not really gonna buy that are you</Note>? <Note>Don’t you think you’ve embarrassed me enough for one day</Note>?
> 好吧，听着，你不是真的要买吗？你不觉得你让我难堪了一天吗？
<GrammarTip points="附加疑问句,动词时态">

- **you’re not really gonna buy that, are you?**：附加疑问句——主句否定 not，问句肯定 are you，"前否后肯"（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）
- **Don’t you think (that) you’ve embarrassed me enough...?**：否定疑问句表"难道你不觉得"；从句用**现在完成式** you've embarrassed——"到今天为止已经让我难堪够了"；enough 副词后置修饰 embarrass（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="550.704" duration="2.424" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Oh, <Note>I embarrass you</Note>?
> 哦，我让你难堪了？
<GrammarTip points="疑问句">

- **I embarrass you?**：陈述语序 + 升调的"回响疑问句"——把对方的话变成问句表惊讶/不服（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="553.128" duration="3.456" /> **Chandler Bing:** <Note>How can I answer that when I’m pretending I don’t know you</Note>?
> 当我假装不认识你的时候，我怎么回答？
<GrammarTip points="副词从句,名词从句">

<p class="svoc"><span class="a">How</span> <span class="v">can</span> <span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">answer</span> <span class="o">that</span></p>

- **when I’m pretending...**：when 时间从句用现在进行式"正假装着"（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **pretending (that) I don’t know you**：pretend + 宾语从句省 that；从句否定 don't know——"假装不认识你"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="556.584" duration="4.608" /> **The Vendor:** He’s just jealous. <Note>You’ll fit right in</Note>; all Londoners wear them!
> 他只是嫉妒。你会很合身的，所有的伦敦人都穿！
<GrammarTip points="短语动词,副词">

- **fit right in**：fit in"融入"；right 副词加强"恰到好处地"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)/[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）
- **all Londoners wear them**：all + 复数名词"所有的伦敦人（都戴）"——小贩睁眼说瞎话（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="561.192" duration="4.56" /> **Chandler Bing:** Oh really? Then <Note>how come no one here is wearing them</Note>?
> 哦，真的吗？那为什么这里没有人穿呢？
<GrammarTip points="疑问句,主谓一致">

<p class="svoc"><span class="a">how come</span> <span class="s">no one here</span> <span class="v">is wearing</span> <span class="o">them</span></p>

- **how come + 陈述语序**：how come = why，但后面**不倒装**（不说 how come is no one wearing）——固定用法（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）
- **no one here is wearing**：no one 表单数，动词用 is；here 副词后置修饰 no one"这里的人"（[Ch.11](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter11.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="565.752" duration="1.296" /> **The Vendor:** (looks around) They’re all tourists.
> （环顾四周）他们都是游客。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="567.048" duration="8.52" /> **Chandler Bing:** All right, look, <Note>if you insist on wearing that</Note>, in public, y’know, <Note>you’re gonna spend the rest of the afternoon all by yourself</Note>.
> 好吧，听着，如果你坚持穿这个，在公共场合，你知道，你会一个人度过下午的剩余时间。
<GrammarTip points="动名词,副词从句">

- **if you insist on wearing that**：if 条件从句现在式；**insist on + 动名词** wearing"坚持做某事"（on 是介词）（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md)）
- **spend the rest of the afternoon (by yourself)**：spend + 时段"度过"；**all by yourself** = 独自一人（by + 反身代词的固定强调式）（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="575.568" duration="6.912" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Oh yeah? (Puts the hat on.) If you’re gonna <Note>make me choose between you and the hat</Note>? <Note>I choose the hat</Note>.
> 哦，是吗？（戴上帽子）如果你想让我在你和帽子之间选择的话？我选择帽子。
<GrammarTip points="使役动词,介词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">you</span> <span class="v">’re gonna make</span> <span class="o">me</span> <span class="c">choose...</span></p>

- **make me choose**：使役动词 make + 宾语 + **原形**补语 choose（不加 to）（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）
- **choose between you and the hat**：choose between A and B"在……之间选"；between 后接并列宾格（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)/[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="582.48" duration="1.128" /> **The Vendor:** Good choice.
> 不错的选择。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="583.608" duration="1.584" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Thanks.
> 谢谢。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="585.192" duration="8.904" /> **Chandler Bing:** Okay, wait. All right, <Note>that’s it</Note>, okay, I’m out of here. <Note>I am not going to be embarrassed anymore</Note>! (He trips over a box, falls into a flower stand and walks away trying to be cool.)
> 好吧，等等。好吧，就这样，好吧，我要离开这里。我不会再感到尴尬了！（他被一个盒子绊倒，掉进花架，然后走开，试图保持冷静。）
<GrammarTip points="惯用语,分词">

- **that’s it**：固定感叹"够了/到此为止"；I'm out of here = 我走了（小品词 out）（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **not going to be embarrassed anymore**：be going to + **be + 过去分词 embarrassed** 被动式"再也不要（被人）弄丢脸"；embarrassed 此处已形容词化表感受（[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

---

🎬 *The hotel, Ross’s room, Emily is entering.*
> 🎬 *酒店，罗斯的房间，艾米丽正在进入。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：seem to do / suggest + 从句用原形 / 强调句 It is...that / 动名词复合结构

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="594.096" duration="0.84" /> **Emily:** Hello?
> 喂？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="594.936" duration="8.592" /> **Ross Geller:** Hey! <Note>I just spoke to your dad</Note>, and you know what? <Note>He seems to think we’ll be able to find a new place</Note> for the wedding.
> 嘿！我刚和你爸爸谈过，你知道吗？他似乎认为我们能为婚礼找到一个新地方。
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,不定词,名词从句">

- **I just spoke to your dad**：just + 过去式"刚刚……"（美式英语习惯；英式多配现在完成式 I've just spoken）（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **He seems to think...**：seem + 不定式 to think"似乎认为"；think 后接宾语从句省 that（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **we’ll be able to find**：will 不能接 can，"将来能够"要说 will **be able to**（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="603.528" duration="1.128" /> **Emily:** <Note>We don’t have to</Note>.
> 我们不必。
<GrammarTip points="语气,省略">

- **We don’t have to (find a new place)**：don't have to = 不必；后半截 find a new place 承前省略，只留 to 都不必留（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.16](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter16.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="604.656" duration="3.648" /> **Ross Geller:** Whoa-whoa-what? You found a place?
> 哇哇什么？你找到地方了？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="608.304" duration="8.616" /> **Emily:** No. But, <Note>Monica and I were talking</Note>, and-and <Note>I was so upset about the hall being knocked down</Note>, and <Note>she suggested that we put the wedding off</Note> for a bit.
> 没有。但是，莫妮卡和我在聊天，我对大厅被撞倒感到非常不安，她建议我们把婚礼推迟一点。
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,动名词,语气">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="608.304" duration="8.616" />

**原句**：I was so upset about the hall being knocked down, and she suggested that we put the wedding off for a bit.

**层次划分**：

- 主干①：<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">was</span> <span class="c">so upset</span></p> so... 修饰 upset（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）
- **about the hall being knocked down**：介词 about + **动名词复合结构**——the hall 是动名词的逻辑主语，being knocked down 是动名词的**被动进行式**（being + 过去分词）"礼堂被拆这件事"（[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md)/[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)）
- 主干②：<p class="svoc"><span class="s">she</span> <span class="v">suggested</span> <span class="o">that we put the wedding off</span></p>
- **suggested that we (should) put...**：suggest 接宾语从句，从句动词用**原形**（= should 省略）——表建议的虚拟语气；同类动词：recommend / insist / demand（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **put off** = 推迟；**for a bit** = 一会儿（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

**翻译**：我对礼堂被拆这事特别难过，她建议我们把婚礼推迟一阵子。

**语法要点**：动名词复合结构 + 被动（Ch.5/6）；suggest that + 主语 + 原形（Ch.9）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="616.92" duration="1.848" /> **Ross Geller:** <Note>She said what</Note>?
> 她说什么？
<GrammarTip points="疑问句">

- **She said what?**：疑问词 what **留在句尾**的回声问句（正常语序是 What did she say?）——不倒装表难以置信（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="618.768" duration="6.72" /> **Emily:** She said, "<Note>If I’m not gonna be happy getting married somewhere that we find in a day</Note>, well then <Note>we should just postpone it</Note>."
> 她说，“如果我在一天之内找不到结婚的地方，那我们就推迟结婚。”
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,副词从句,关系从句,动名词">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="618.768" duration="6.72" />

**原句**：If I'm not gonna be happy getting married somewhere that we find in a day, well then we should just postpone it.

**层次划分**：

- 主句主干：<p class="svoc"><span class="s">we</span> <span class="v">should postpone</span> <span class="o">it</span></p>（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **If I’m not gonna be happy...**：if 条件副词从句，be gonna 表将来安排（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **getting married somewhere**：动名词短语作方面状语——"在（某处）办婚礼这件事上不开心"；get married 结婚（[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md)/[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)）
- **(that) we find in a day**：定语从句修饰 somewhere，关系词作 find 的宾语故省略（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)）

**翻译**：如果随便找个一天内能定到的地方办婚礼我不会开心，那我们就干脆延期吧。

**语法要点**：if 从句表现在假设（Ch.13）；be happy + 动名词（Ch.5）；关系词作宾语可省（Ch.14）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="625.488" duration="16.032" /> **Ross Geller:** Postpone it? Emily, do you think Monica realises <Note>how much our parents spent on this wedding</Note>? Do you my sister’s teeny-tiny little brain comprehends that <Note>people took time out of their lives to fly thousands of miles</Note> to be here, huh? (He puts his pants on backwards.) This isn’t right.
> 推迟？艾米丽，你认为莫妮卡知道我们的父母在婚礼上花了多少钱吗？你知道吗？我妹妹小小的大脑知道人们花时间飞几千英里来这里，是吗？（他把裤子穿反了。）这不对。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,原文勘误,不定词">

- ⚠️ 原文 **Do you my sister's teeny-tiny little brain comprehends that...** 听写漏词：应为 Do you **think** my sister's teeny-tiny little brain comprehends that...（承前句的 do you think）
- **do you think + 宾语从句**：插入语 do you think 之后**用陈述语序**——how much our parents spent（不倒装成 how much did our parents spend）（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)/[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **how much our parents spent on this wedding**：how much 引导宾语从句表金额"花了多少钱"；spend...on sth（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **took time out of their lives to fly thousands of miles**：不定式 to fly 表目的；thousands of + 复数表概数"好几千"（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="641.52" duration="5.568" /> **Emily:** I realize that <Note>people are going to be disappointed</Note>. But, I’m sure <Note>they’ll come back when we can do it right</Note>.
> 我知道人们会失望的。但是，我相信当我们做对的时候，他们会回来的。
<GrammarTip points="分词,副词从句">

- **people are going to be disappointed**：be going to + be + 过去分词 disappointed——"会（感到）失望"，分词已形容词化（[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **they’ll come back when we can do it right**：主句将来式，when 时间从句用现在式（can do）代未来（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- **I’m sure (that)...**：sure 后接宾语从句省 that（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="647.088" duration="5.016" /> **Ross Geller:** I can’t <Note>ask people to do that</Note>? Would you ask people to do that? (Holds out his pants)
> 我不能让别人那样做？你会让别人那样做吗？（伸出他的裤子）
<GrammarTip points="不定词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">can’t ask</span> <span class="o">people</span> <span class="c">to do that</span></p>

- **ask people to do that**：ask + 宾语 + 不定式宾补"要求某人做某事"；两个问句其实是肯定句的质问式——"我总不能叫大家白跑一趟吧"（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="652.104" duration="10.008" /> **Emily:** <Note>Don’t you point your pants at me</Note>! (She throws them on the floor.) We have no choice! <Note>Anywhere that’s half-decent would’ve be booked months ago</Note>, Ross don’t you understand? <Note>This is our wedding I’m talking about</Note>.
> 别把你的裤子指着我！（她把裤子扔到地板上。）我们别无选择！任何半体面的地方都会在几个月前被预订的，罗斯，你不明白吗？我说的是我们的婚礼。
<GrammarTip points="语气,关系从句,原文勘误">

- **Don’t you point your pants at me!**：Don't you + 原形——否定疑问形式的强硬祈使句"你敢拿裤子指我！"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **Anywhere that’s half-decent**：定语从句 that's half-decent 修饰 anywhere（anywhere 后可接关系从句）（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)）
- ⚠️ 原文 **would've be booked** 应为 would've **been** booked——情态动词 + 完成式 + 被动"（几个月前）早就被订光了"，听写漏了 been（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **This is our wedding (that) I’m talking about**：定语从句省关系词，介词 about 留句尾——"我在说的是我们的婚礼！"（强调）（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="662.112" duration="6.912" /> **Ross Geller:** <Note>The only thing I understand</Note> is; <Note>postponing it is not an option</Note>. <Note>This is when we’re getting married</Note>.
> 我唯一能理解的是，推迟是不行的。这是我们结婚的时候。
<GrammarTip points="关系从句,动名词,名词从句">

- **The only thing (that) I understand**：定语从句省关系词（作 understand 的宾语）（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)）
- **postponing it is not an option**：<p class="svoc"><span class="s">postponing it</span> <span class="v">is not</span> <span class="c">an option</span></p> **动名词短语作主语**，视为单数（[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md)）
- **This is when we’re getting married**：when 引导**表语从句**（名词从句作补语）——"（现在）就是我们结婚的（时候）"；从句用进行式表既定安排（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="669.024" duration="2.616" /> **Emily:** So what are you saying? <Note>It’s now or never</Note>?
> 那你在说什么？是现在还是永远？
<GrammarTip points="惯用语,对等连接词">

- **It’s now or never**：固定习语"机不可失，要么现在要么永远别想"——or 连接两个对等的时间副词（[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="671.64" duration="3.504" /> **Ross Geller:** No. I’m saying it’s now. (He starts putting on his pants, backwards again.)
> 不。我是说现在。（他开始穿裤子，又向后穿。）

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="675.144" duration="0.768" /> **Emily:** Or?
> 还是？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="675.912" duration="5.136" /> **Ross Geller:** <Note>There’s no ‘or’ in mind</Note>. <Note>What is wrong with these pants</Note>?!!
> 没有“或”的意思。这条裤子怎么了？！！
<GrammarTip points="主谓一致,惯用语">

- **There’s no ‘or’ in mind**：被引用的词 ‘or’ 当作单数名词（"脑子里没有'或者'这个选项"）；引词作主语按单数算（[Ch.11](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter11.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）
- **What is wrong with...?**：固定句型"……怎么了/出什么毛病了"（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="681.048" duration="9.84" /> **Emily:** It’s not the pants. <Note>It’s you that is backwards</Note>. And if, and if you don’t understand <Note>how important this is to me</Note>, well then, perhaps <Note>we shouldn’t get married at all</Note>! (She storms out.)
> 不是裤子。是你在倒退。如果，如果你不明白这对我有多重要，那么，也许我们根本不应该结婚！（她怒气冲冲地走了。）
<GrammarTip points="强调句,名词从句">

- **It’s you that is backwards**：**强调句（分裂句）**——It is + 被强调的主语 you + that...，原句 You are backwards；中文"不是裤子的问题，反的人是你"（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）
- **how important this is to me**：how + 形容词引导的宾语从句（感叹式），作 understand 的宾语——**陈述语序** this is，不倒装（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **shouldn’t get married at all**：not...at all"根本不"；get married 结婚（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="690.888" duration="8.376" /> **Ross Geller:** (chasing her, trying to zip up his pants. His got them on right now.) No, wait! Emily! No, wait, stop! Emily, please—(He catches something important in the zipper and howls like a little boy and falls to the floor.)
> （追着她，试着拉上裤子的拉链。他的裤子现在就穿上了。）不，等等！艾米丽！不，等等，停！艾米丽，求你了-（他在拉链里抓了个重要的东西，像个小男孩一样嚎叫，然后摔倒在地。）

Commercial Break
> 商业休息

---

🎬 *Monica and Rachel's, Phoebe is on the couch as Rachel returns carrying a bunch of shopping bags.*
> 🎬 *莫妮卡和瑞秋家，菲比坐在沙发上，瑞秋拿着一堆购物袋回来。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：动名词主语 / 关系副词 where / feel like + 动名词 / 比较级从句

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="699.264" duration="0.816" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Hey!
> 嘿！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="700.08" duration="1.416" /> **Rachel Green:** (depressed) Hi.
> （沮丧地）嗨。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="701.496" duration="3.192" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** So, <Note>did shopping make you feel any better</Note> about Ross?
> 那么，购物让你对罗斯感觉好点了吗？
<GrammarTip points="动名词,使役动词,形容词比较">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">shopping</span> <span class="v">did make</span> <span class="o">you</span> <span class="c">feel any better</span></p>

- **shopping 作主语**：动名词当主语，单数——"（血拼）这件事"（[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md)）
- **make you feel...**：使役动词 make + 宾语 + 原形 feel（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）
- **any better**：比较级 better 在疑问/否定句中配 any"有没有好一点"（[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="704.688" duration="2.904" /> **Rachel Green:** Manhattan <Note>does not have enough stores</Note>.
> 曼哈顿没有足够的商店。
<GrammarTip points="名词词组,夸张">

- **enough + 复数名词**：enough stores"足够多的商店"；enough 作限定词前置（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）
- 言下之意"我把曼哈顿的店逛遍了还是难受"——夸张（hyperbole）表程度

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="707.592" duration="2.544" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Well, I think I can <Note>help you get over him</Note>.
> 嗯，我想我可以帮你忘掉他。
<GrammarTip points="不定词,短语动词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">can help</span> <span class="o">you</span> <span class="c">(to) get over him</span></p>

- **help you (to) get over him**：help + 宾语 + 不定式补语，to 可省（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）
- **get over sb**：短语动词"走出（对某人的）情伤/痊愈"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="710.136" duration="1.584" /> **Rachel Green:** You can?
> 你能吗？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="711.72" duration="3.456" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Yeah. <Note>I just need you to bring me some photos of Ross</Note>.
> 是的。我只需要你给我带些罗斯的照片。
<GrammarTip points="不定词,双宾语">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">need</span> <span class="o">you</span> <span class="c">to bring me some photos</span></p>

- **need you to bring...**：need + 宾语 + 不定式宾补"需要某人做某事"；bring me some photos 内含双宾语（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="715.176" duration="1.44" /> **Rachel Green:** Um-hmm.
> 嗯。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="716.616" duration="2.04" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** And a small piece of chocolate.
> 还有一小块巧克力。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="718.656" duration="1.512" /> **Rachel Green:** Okay.
> 好的。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="720.168" duration="2.136" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** And a glass of tepid water. (She gathers up all of these things.)
> 还有一杯温水。（她收集了所有这些东西。）

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="722.304" duration="6.48" /> **Rachel Green:** Ooh, is this <Note>one of those things where you throw it in a bag with some graveyard dirt</Note> and <Note>hang it from a north-facing tree</Note>?
> 哦，这是你把它和墓地的泥土一起放在袋子里，然后挂在朝北的树上的那种东西吗？
<GrammarTip points="关系从句,分词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">this</span> <span class="v">is</span> <span class="c">one of those things</span></p>

- **one of those things where...**：**关系副词 where**（= in which）引导定语从句修饰 things——"把东西扔进袋子的那种（法术）"（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)）
- 从句内两个并列谓语：**throw** it in a bag... and **hang** it from a tree（[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)）
- **a north-facing tree**：复合形容词 north-facing（名词 + 现在分词）"朝北的"（[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="728.784" duration="17.352" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Uh, <Note>only if you have the hiccups too</Note>. Yeah, the pictures are for you, the water and the chocolate is for me. I just <Note>didn’t feel like getting up</Note>. Okay, I’m gonna show you a picture of Ross. Okay? And <Note>you’re going to remember all of the bad things about him</Note>. All right? Really focus on his flaws.
> 嗯，除非你也打嗝。是的，照片是给你的，水和巧克力是给我的。我只是不想起床。好吧，我给你看一张罗斯的照片。好吗？你会记得他所有的坏处。好吗？真的关注他的缺点。
<GrammarTip points="副词从句,动名词,主谓一致">

- **only if you have the hiccups too**：only if"只有当……才"——那句咒语只治打嗝，顺势自嘲（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- **the water and the chocolate **is** for me**：口语就近一致（把水和巧克力当"一份"）；规范写法应用 are（[Ch.11](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter11.md)）
- **didn’t feel like getting up**：feel like + **动名词**"想要做某事"（[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md)）
- **you’re going to remember all of the bad things**：be going to 表"将要"；all of + 名词（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="746.136" duration="4.8" /> **Rachel Green:** I can do that. I certainly did it <Note>when we were going out</Note>.
> 我能做到。我们出去的时候我当然做到了。
<GrammarTip points="副词从句,短语动词">

- **when we were going out**：when 时间从句用过去进行式铺背景；**go out** 此处 = 约会、交往（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="750.936" duration="21.936" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Okay. Umm, <Note>before we get started</Note>, I just wanna say <Note>for the record</Note> that I love Ross, I think he’s such a great guy. Here. (Hands her the picture, Rachel grabs it out of her hand.) Okay, now, close your eyes. And <Note>imagine that you’re with Ross</Note> okay and imagine that you’re kissing him. And you’re-you’re running your hands all over his body. And then <Note>you run your hands through his hair</Note>, but eew-oh gross it’s some kind of grease, it’s—uck! Hah?
> 好吧。嗯，在我们开始之前，我只想说我爱罗斯，我觉得他是个很棒的人。给。（把照片递给她，瑞秋从手中接过。）好吧，现在，闭上你的眼睛。想象你和罗斯在一起好吧，想象你在吻他。然后你用手抚摸他的全身。然后你用手抚摸他的头发，但哇哦，恶心，这是一种油脂，真该死！哈？
<GrammarTip points="副词从句,名词从句,动词时态">

- **before we get started**：before 时间从句现在式（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- **for the record**：习语"郑重声明一下"；**such a great guy**：such a + (形容词) + 单数名词（对比 so great a guy 较正式）（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）
- **imagine (that) you're with Ross / (that) you're kissing him**：imagine 接宾语从句，三个排比从句全用**现在进行式**——让想象画面"现场直播"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **run your hands through his hair**：介词 through"穿过"；run one's hands through 轻抚（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="772.872" duration="7.704" /> **Rachel Green:** I don’t know, his uh, <Note>his hair never really bothered me that much</Note>, and y’know it was always <Note>more crunchy than it was greasy</Note>.
> 我不知道，他的头发从来没有让我那么烦恼过，而且你知道它总是比油腻的更脆。
<GrammarTip points="形容词比较">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">it</span> <span class="v">was</span> <span class="c">more crunchy</span></p>

- **more crunchy than it was greasy**：比较级 + than 从句（从句保留主谓 it was）——"与其说油，不如说是脆"（[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)）
- **never really bothered me that much**：that much 程度状语"那么（到那种程度）"（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="780.576" duration="5.184" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Okay, this is going to be <Note>harder than I thought</Note>. Umm, let’s try some uh, <Note>aversion therapy</Note>.
> 好吧，这比我想象的要难。嗯，让我们试试，呃，厌恶疗法。
<GrammarTip points="形容词比较">

- **harder than I thought (it would be)**：比较从句的省略——than 后只留 I thought（[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)/[Ch.16](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter16.md)）
- **aversion therapy**：术语"厌恶疗法"——aversion（厌恶）+ therapy（疗法）复合名词

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="785.76" duration="1.512" /> **Rachel Green:** Okay.
> 好的。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="787.272" duration="0.816" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Okay?
> 好吗？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="788.088" duration="1.632" /> **Rachel Green:** All right.
> 好的。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="789.72" duration="2.064" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** So uh, now look at the picture…
> 那么，嗯，现在看看这张照片…

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="791.784" duration="1.512" /> **Rachel Green:** Okay.
> 好的。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="793.296" duration="1.176" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** All right, and umm… (She grabs the picture and smacks her in the head.)
> 好吧，嗯…（她抓起照片，拍了拍她的头。）

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="794.472" duration="1.344" /> **Rachel Green:** Ow!
> 哦！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="795.816" duration="1.704" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Okay, how do you feel now?
> 好吧，你现在感觉怎么样？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="797.52" duration="2.496" /> **Rachel Green:** Well, <Note>I like you less</Note>!
> 嗯，我不太喜欢你了！
<GrammarTip points="形容词比较,幽默">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">like</span> <span class="o">you</span> <span class="a">less</span></p>

- **I like you less**：比较级 less"（比以前）更不喜欢"——治疗没让 Rachel 讨厌 Ross，反而讨厌起菲比，笑点收尾（[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)）

</GrammarTip>

---

🎬 *Chandler and Joey’s hotel room. Chandler is watching TV as Joey returns, still wearing his hat.*
> 🎬 *钱德勒和乔伊的酒店房间。乔伊回来时钱德勒正在看电视，乔伊还戴着帽子。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：shouldn't have said / 现在完成进行式 / 表语从句 / 疑问词 + 不定式

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="800.016" duration="1.488" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Hey.
> 嘿。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="801.504" duration="1.44" /> **Chandler Bing:** Hey. (He nods at the hat.)
> 嘿。（他朝帽子点头。）

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="802.944" duration="2.88" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Oh. (Takes off the hat.) Sorry!
> 哦。（摘下帽子）对不起！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="805.824" duration="12.24" /> **Chandler Bing:** No-no-no, y’know what? I really <Note>shouldn’t have said that you were embarrassing me</Note>, I mean that really wasn’t cool. And <Note>if it makes you feel any better</Note>, <Note>I’ve had a really lousy day</Note>.
> 不，不，你知道吗？我真的不该说你让我难堪，我是说那真的不酷。如果这能让你感觉好一点的话，我今天过得真的很糟糕。
<GrammarTip points="语气,名词从句,动词时态">

- **shouldn’t have said...**：should have + 过去分词表懊悔"（当时）真不该说"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **said (that) you were embarrassing me**：宾语从句省 that；从句用**过去进行式**——"（那时）正在让我难堪"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **if it makes you feel any better**：if 条件句现在式；make + 宾语 + 原形 feel；any better 比较级（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)）
- **I’ve had a really lousy day**：现在完成式——今天还没过完，"（到现在为止）过了糟心的一天"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="818.064" duration="1.704" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Me too.
> 我也是。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="819.768" duration="1.392" /> **Chandler Bing:** Yeah?
> 是吗？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="821.16" duration="6.48" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Noo!! <Note>I’ve had the best day ever</Note>! Dude, check this out!
> 不！！我度过了有史以来最好的一天！哥们，看看这个！
<GrammarTip points="形容词比较,动词时态">

- **I’ve had the best day ever**：最高级 the best + ever + 现在完成式 I've had——"有生以来最棒的一天"；与 best Abbey I've ever seen 同一句型（[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="827.64" duration="1.488" /> **Monica Geller:** (entering) Hey!
> （进来）嘿！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="829.128" duration="2.04" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Sh-shh-shh! (He motions for her to watch something he has taped.)
> 嘘嘘！（他示意她看他录的东西。）

[Cut to the tape Joey made in front of some famous place in London with a rather famous English-type person.]
> [切到乔伊在伦敦某个著名的地方和一个相当有名的英国人在一起的录像带。]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="831.168" duration="4.152" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** (on tape) Okay, so <Note>say hi to my friend</Note> and <Note>tell him that you like the hat</Note>.
> （录音）好的，跟我朋友打个招呼，告诉他你喜欢这顶帽子。
<GrammarTip points="祈使句,名词从句">

- **say hi... and tell him...**：两个并列祈使句（and 连接对等谓语）（[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **tell him that you like the hat**：tell + 人 + that 宾语从句——tell 的从句前必须有间接宾语（him）（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="835.32" duration="2.208" /> **Fergie:** (Yep, Sarah, the Duchess of York) Okay, so umm, what’s your friend’s name?
> （是的，莎拉，约克公爵夫人）好的，那么，嗯，你朋友叫什么名字？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="837.528" duration="2.064" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** (on tape) Oh, Chandler.
> （录音）哦，钱德勒。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="839.592" duration="1.248" /> **Fergie:** Hi Chandler! (Waves)
> 嗨，钱德勒！（挥手）

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="840.84" duration="3.288" /> **Chandler Bing:** That’s… That’s was…
> 那是…那是…

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="844.128" duration="1.8" /> **Monica Geller:** Oh my God!
> 哦，我的天啊！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="845.928" duration="2.088" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** That’s Fergie baby!!
> 那是菲姬宝贝！！
