<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="848.016" duration="3.984" /> **Fergie:** <Note>Joey says you don’t really like his hat</Note>, but I think <Note>it’s kinda dashing</Note>.
> 乔伊说你不太喜欢他的帽子，但我觉得有点时髦。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,副词">

- **Joey says (that) you don’t really like his hat / I think (that) it’s...**：say / think 后接宾语从句，that 均省（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **kinda dashing**：kinda = kind of"有点儿"，程度副词；dashing"风度翩翩的"（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="852.0" duration="4.296" /> **Chandler Bing:** <Note>How did you</Note>? How? How?
> 你怎么做到的？怎么做到的？怎么做到的？
<GrammarTip points="省略,疑问句">

- **How did you (do that)?**：省略问句——宾语和谓语中心全省，只留 How did you，震惊三连（[Ch.16](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter16.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="856.296" duration="8.664" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Well, <Note>I was trying to figure out how to get to Buckingham Palace</Note>, right? So, I’m in my map and-and…(Ross enters) Hey!
> 嗯，我想知道怎么去白金汉宫，对吗？所以，我在我的地图上并且…（罗斯进来）嘿！
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,不定词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">was trying</span> <span class="o">to figure out how to get to...</span></p>

- **I was trying to figure out...**：过去进行式"（当时）正设法"；try to do 努力做（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）
- **how to get to Buckingham Palace**：**疑问词 + 不定式**作 figure out 的宾语 = how I could get to... 的简化（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.18](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter18.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="864.96" duration="1.488" /> **Monica Geller:** Hey!
> 嘿！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="866.448" duration="4.536" /> **Ross Geller:** Hi. <Note>I understand you had a little talk with Emily</Note>.
> 嗨，我知道你和艾米丽谈过了。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,惯用语">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">understand</span> <span class="o">(that) you had a little talk...</span></p>

- **I understand (that)...**：understand 接宾语从句，此处是正式措辞"据悉/我听说"——兴师问罪的开场白；have a talk with sb"与某人谈了谈"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="870.984" duration="4.152" /> **Monica Geller:** (laughs) Yes, I did! And <Note>you are welcome</Note>!
> （笑）是的，我做到了！不客气！
<GrammarTip points="惯用语,反讽">

- **You are welcome**：固定答谢语"不客气"——Ross 明明在质问，Monica 抢答"不客气"，把讽刺当感谢收（语用反讽）；Yes, I **did** 的 did 是代动词，替代 had a talk（[Ch.16](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter16.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="875.136" duration="4.488" /> **Ross Geller:** <Note>Am I</Note>?! And <Note>was it your idea to postpone the wedding</Note>?!
> 是我吗？！婚礼延期是你的主意吗？！
<GrammarTip points="省略,不定词">

- **Am I?!**：= Am I (welcome)?! 省略反问，跟上句 You are welcome 针锋相对（[Ch.16](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter16.md)）
- **was it your idea to postpone the wedding?**：<p class="svoc"><span class="s">it</span> <span class="v">was</span> <span class="c">your idea</span></p> 不定式 to postpone the wedding 作 idea 的同位语——"推迟婚礼（这个）主意"（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="879.624" duration="1.488" /> **Monica Geller:** Umm…
> 嗯…

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="881.112" duration="2.4" /> **Chandler Bing:** I’m gonna go to the bathroom.
> 我要去洗手间。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="883.512" duration="1.704" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Wait up! (They both run to the bathroom.)
> 等等！（他们都跑到浴室。）

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="885.216" duration="7.68" /> **Ross Geller:** Hey-hey, <Note>since you’re the ‘fix-it’ lady</Note>, <Note>here’s a pickle</Note>, <Note>what do you do when the bride says she doesn’t want to have the wedding at all</Note>?
> 嘿，嘿，既然你是“修好”的女士，这里有个问题，当新娘说她根本不想举行婚礼时，你怎么办？
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,副词从句,名词从句">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="885.216" duration="7.68" />

**原句**：What do you do when the bride says she doesn't want to have the wedding at all?

**层次划分**：

- 主句：<p class="svoc"><span class="o">what</span> <span class="v">do</span> <span class="s">you</span> <span class="v">do</span></p> 疑问句"你怎么办"；第一个 do 是助动词，第二个 do 是实义动词"做"（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）
- **when the bride says...**：when 时间副词从句"当新娘说……时"（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- **(that) she doesn’t want to have the wedding at all**：says 后的宾语从句省 that；want to do + not...at all"根本不想办婚礼"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）
- **since you’re the ‘fix-it’ lady**：since 原因从句"既然"；'fix-it' 是临时造的复合形容词"专管修（烂摊子）的"（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- **here’s a pickle**：here 引导的存在句；a pickle 口语"一个难题/窘境"

**翻译**：既然你是收拾残局的专家，那问你个难题：新娘说婚礼干脆不办了，你怎么办？

**语法要点**：when 从句套宾语从句（Ch.13/12）；since 表原因（Ch.13）；not...at all（Ch.8）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="892.896" duration="1.848" /> **Monica Geller:** She said that?!
> 她说的？！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="894.744" duration="1.368" /> **Ross Geller:** Yeah.
> 是的。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="896.112" duration="1.584" /> **Monica Geller:** Why?!
> 为什么？！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="897.696" duration="6.6" /> **Ross Geller:** I don’t know, I told her <Note>it was stupid to put off the wedding</Note> <Note>just because the hall was gone</Note> and <Note>she liked flipped out</Note>.
> 我不知道，我告诉她推迟婚礼是愚蠢的，因为大厅不见了，她喜欢发疯。
<GrammarTip points="不定词,副词从句,口语标记">

- **it was stupid to put off the wedding**：it 虚主语，不定式 to put off... 是真正主语——"推迟婚礼（这事）很蠢"；put off 推迟（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **just because the hall was gone**：because 原因从句，just 加强"仅仅因为"（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- ⚠️ **she liked flipped out** 应为 **she, like, flipped out**——口语填充词 like（"她当时就炸毛了"）被听写连成了 liked；flip out = 崩溃/暴怒（短语动词）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="904.296" duration="4.248" /> **Monica Geller:** Oh my God. <Note>You’re even dumber than I am</Note>!
> 哦，我的天啊。你比我还笨！
<GrammarTip points="形容词比较">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">You</span> <span class="v">’re</span> <span class="c">even dumber</span></p>

- **even dumber than I am**：比较级 dumber + even 加强"还要更笨"；than I am 保留主格 + be 动词是完整式（口语也说 than me）（[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="908.544" duration="1.752" /> **Ross Geller:** Excuse me?
> 劳驾？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="910.296" duration="3.504" /> **Monica Geller:** Ross, <Note>how long have you been planning this wedding</Note>?
> 罗斯，你计划这场婚礼有多久了？
<GrammarTip points="动词时态">

<p class="svoc"><span class="a">how long</span> <span class="v">have</span> <span class="s">you</span> <span class="v">been planning</span> <span class="o">this wedding</span></p>

- **how long have you been planning**：**现在完成进行式**——"从过去一直筹划到现在"；how long 问持续时段，是完成(进行)式的标志疑问词（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="913.8" duration="3.12" /> **Ross Geller:** I don’t know. A month?
> 我不知道。一个月？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="916.92" duration="26.952" /> **Monica Geller:** <Note>Emily has probably been planning it since she was five</Note>! Ever since the first time she took a pillowcase and hung it off the back of her head. <Note>That’s what we did</Note>! We dreamed about the perfect wedding, and the perfect place, with the perfect four-tiered wedding cake (Starting to cry), with the little people on top. (Ross gets thrown a box of Kleenex from the bathroom and he gives her one.) Thanks. But <Note>the most important part is that we had the perfect guy who understood just how important all that other stuff was</Note>.
> 艾米丽可能从五岁起就开始计划了！自从她第一次拿枕头套挂在脑后。我们就是这么做的！我们梦想着完美的婚礼，完美的地方，完美的四层婚礼蛋糕（开始哭泣），上面有小人物。（罗斯从浴室里扔出一盒纸巾，他给了她一盒。）谢谢。但最重要的是，我们有一个完美的男人，他知道所有其他东西是多么重要。
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,动词时态,名词从句,关系从句">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="916.92" duration="26.952" />

**原句**：The most important part is that we had the perfect guy who understood just how important all that other stuff was.

**层次划分**：

- 主干：<p class="svoc"><span class="s">The most important part</span> <span class="v">is</span> <span class="c">that we had the perfect guy...</span></p>
- **that we had the perfect guy**：that 引导**表语从句**（名词从句作补语）——"最重要的是（这一点）……"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **who understood...**：定语从句修饰 guy，who 作主语不可省（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)）
- **how important all that other stuff was**：understood 的宾语从句，how + 形容词的感叹式——**陈述语序** stuff was，不倒装（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- 前句 **has probably been planning it since she was five**：现在完成进行式 + since 从句（从句用过去式 was）——"从五岁一直筹划到现在"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- **That’s what we did**：what 表语从句"我们（小时候玩）的就是这个"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

**翻译**：但最重要的是，我们梦想中的那个完美男人，他懂得这一切对我们来说有多重要。

**语法要点**：完成进行 + since（Ch.3）；that 表语从句（Ch.12）；定语从句套宾语从句（Ch.14/12）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="943.872" duration="7.176" /> **Ross Geller:** <Note>I had no idea</Note>. And that-that pillowcase thing, <Note>I thought you guys were just doing the flying nun</Note>.
> 我不知道。那个枕套的事，我还以为你们只是在演飞行修女呢。
<GrammarTip points="惯用语,名词从句">

- **I had no idea**：固定说法"我完全不知道"——no 直接修饰名词 idea（= I didn't have any idea 更口语）（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）
- **I thought (that) you guys were just doing the flying nun**：宾语从句省 that，过去进行式"（以为你们）只是在扮"；the flying nun 指老剧《飞天修女》——枕头套挂脑后像修女头巾（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="951.048" duration="2.184" /> **Monica Geller:** Sometimes we were.
> 有时候我们是。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="953.232" duration="5.784" /> **Ross Geller:** Come on. You gotta <Note>help me figure out what to do</Note>. Okay?
> 来吧，你得帮我想想怎么做，好吗？
<GrammarTip points="不定词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">You</span> <span class="v">gotta help</span> <span class="o">me</span> <span class="c">figure out what to do</span></p>

- **help me (to) figure out...**：help + 宾语 + 不定式补语，to 可省（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）
- **what to do**：疑问词 + 不定式作 figure out 的宾语 = what I should do 的简化（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.18](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter18.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="959.016" duration="1.608" /> **Monica Geller:** Okay.
> 好的。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="960.624" duration="1.536" /> **Ross Geller:** Come on. (They leave and Chandler sticks his head out.)
> 来吧。（他们走了，钱德勒把头伸出来。）

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="962.16" duration="2.424" /> **Chandler Bing:** That was pretty intense huh?
> 那很紧张，是吗？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="964.584" duration="7.152" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Yeah. (Pause) Hey, <Note>I hope Ross didn’t think that we just went in there</Note> <Note>because we were uncomfortable being out here</Note>!
> 是的。（停顿）嘿，我希望罗斯没想到我们只是进去了，因为我们在这里不舒服！
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,副词从句,动名词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">hope</span> <span class="o">(that) Ross didn’t think...</span></p>

- **I hope (that) Ross didn’t think (that)...**：hope 接宾语从句，否定留在从句里（hope 不做否定前移）；think 后又套一层 that 从句（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **because we were uncomfortable being out here**：because 原因从句；be uncomfortable + **动名词** being out here"待在客厅（我们）不自在"（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="971.736" duration="1.968" /> **Chandler Bing:** (glares at him) <Note>I hope he did</Note>!
> （怒视着他）我希望他做到了！
<GrammarTip points="省略">

- **I hope he did**：did 是代动词，替代 **thought so**——"我倒希望他真这么以为（我们是因为尴尬才躲进去的）"（[Ch.16](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter16.md)）

</GrammarTip>

---

🎬 *Monica and Rachel's, the phone rings and Phoebe answers it.*
> 🎬 *莫妮卡和瑞秋家，电话铃响了，菲比接了电话。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：定语从句分裂 / 不定式表目的 / 现在完成进行式 / if 宾语从句

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="973.704" duration="0.864" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Hello.
> 你好。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="974.568" duration="3.744" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** (on phone) Hey, Pheebs! It’s Joey!
> （在电话里）嘿，菲比！是乔伊！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="978.312" duration="11.88" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Hey, Joey! Hey! Ooh! Ooh! I just say <Note>someone on the—that looks just like you</Note> on the subway. And <Note>I was gonna go over and say ‘hi!’</Note> but then I figured, <Note>he doesn’t care if he looks like you</Note>.
> 嘿，乔伊！嘿！哦！哦！我只是说地铁里有人长得像你。我本来想过去打声招呼的！但后来我想，他不在乎他长得像你。
<GrammarTip points="关系从句,名词从句,原文勘误">

- ⚠️ 原文 **I just say someone** 应为 I just **saw** someone（过去式听写失真）
- **someone (on the subway) that looks just like you**：定语从句 that looks... 修饰 someone，被地点状语 on the subway 隔开——**分裂式定语从句**（台：关系从句）；on the—是说到一半的自我修正（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)）
- **I was gonna go over and say ‘hi!’**：was gonna = 过去将来"（本来）打算"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **he doesn’t care if he looks like you**：care + **if 宾语从句**（= whether"是否"）；look like sb"长得像某人"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="990.192" duration="6.936" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** <Note>That just cost me four bucks</Note>. But uh listen, <Note>I just called to see how the chick and the duck are doing</Note>?
> 那只花了我四块钱。但是，呃，听着，我只是打电话来看看小鸡和小鸭怎么样了？
<GrammarTip points="双宾语,不定词,名词从句">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">That</span> <span class="v">cost</span> <span class="o">me</span> <span class="o">four bucks</span></p>

- **cost me four bucks**：cost + 人 + 钱双宾语"花了我四块"；cost 过去式与原形同形（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **called to see...**：不定式 to see 表打电话的目的（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）
- **how the chick and the duck are doing**：how 引导宾语从句，**陈述语序** are doing 不倒装（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="997.128" duration="6.864" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Ohh, <Note>they’re having a great time</Note> with their Aunt Phoebe! <Note>Aunt Rachel hasn’t been helpful at all</Note>. So, do you miss me?
> 哦，他们和菲比阿姨在一起玩得很开心！瑞秋阿姨一点帮助都没有。那么，你想我吗？
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,惯用语">

- **they’re having a great time**：have a great time"玩得开心"，进行式表眼下状态；Aunt Phoebe 称谓前不加冠词（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）
- **hasn’t been helpful at all**：现在完成式 + not...at all"一点忙都没帮上"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1003.992" duration="3.384" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Kinda, but <Note>I’ve just been having way too much fun</Note>.
> 有点，但我只是玩得太开心了。
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,副词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">’ve been having</span> <span class="o">way too much fun</span></p>

- **I’ve just been having...**：**现在完成进行式**——"这段时间一直玩得太疯"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **way too much fun**：way 作程度副词加强 too much"太多太多了"；fun 不可数（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1007.376" duration="1.872" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** So you’re <Note>not homesick yet</Note>?
> 你还没有想家？
<GrammarTip points="副词">

- **not...yet**："还（没）……"——yet 用于否定句尾表"截至目前"（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1009.248" duration="2.4" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** No, I don’t think so.
> 不，我不这么认为。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1011.648" duration="2.232" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** All right, <Note>the seven of us miss you</Note>.
> 好吧，我们七个人都很想你。
<GrammarTip points="名词词组,主谓一致">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">the seven of us</span> <span class="v">miss</span> <span class="o">you</span></p>

- **the seven of us**：the + 数词 + of 人称代词"我们七个"——整体作主语，动词用复数 miss（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)/[Ch.11](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter11.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1013.88" duration="1.752" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Who’s seven?
> 谁七岁？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1015.632" duration="2.544" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Y’know, me, Rachel, the birds, the babies…
> 你知道，我，瑞秋，那些鸟，那些婴儿…

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1018.176" duration="2.568" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Ahh, the babies miss me?
> 啊，孩子们想我了？

*(There is a knock on Rachel and Phoebe’s door.)*
> *（有人敲瑞秋和菲比的门。）*

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1020.744" duration="1.776" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Ooh, the pizza guy’s here!
> 哦，披萨店的家伙来了！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1022.52" duration="3.936" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** What? <Note>You ordered pizza without me</Note>?!
> 什么？你没有我就点了披萨？！
<GrammarTip points="疑问句,介词">

- **You ordered pizza without me?!**：陈述语序 + 升调的惊讶问句；without + 代词宾格"撇下我"（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1026.456" duration="4.488" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Yeah. But y’know <Note>we were thinking about you</Note>, y’know we ordered the Joey Special.
> 是啊。但是你知道我们在想你，你知道我们点了乔伊特餐。
<GrammarTip points="动词时态">

- **we were thinking about you**：过去进行式"（点单那会儿）正惦记着你"；think about sb 想着某人（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1030.944" duration="1.824" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Two pizzas?!
> 两个比萨饼？！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1032.768" duration="2.736" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Yep! Okay, gotta go, talk to you later.
> 是的！好了，我得走了，待会儿再谈。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1035.504" duration="3.168" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Wait, well, <Note>where did you get it from</Note>?!
> 等等，你从哪里弄来的？！
<GrammarTip points="疑问句,介词">

- **Where did you get it from?**：get sth from..."从哪儿弄到"；疑问句中 from 留在句尾（口语语序）（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)/[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）

</GrammarTip>

*(Phoebe has already hung up, leaving Joey in the dark. So Joey decides to watch some TV and turns on a rerun of Cheers, with the theme song playing. At first, he’s happy, but as the song progresses Joey gets depressed and homesick.)*
> *（菲比已经挂断了电话，把乔伊留在了黑暗中。所以乔伊决定看一些电视，打开一个欢呼的重播，播放主题曲。起初，他很高兴，但随着歌曲的发展，乔伊变得沮丧和想家。）*

---

🎬 *Ross and Emily’s planned wedding place, Monica is dragging Emily in.*
> 🎬 *罗斯和艾米丽计划的婚礼地点，莫妮卡正把艾米丽拖进来。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：现在完成式疑问 / fewer vs less / 主将从现 / whatever 名词从句

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1038.672" duration="3.864" /> **Emily:** Monica, <Note>why have you brought me here of all places</Note>?!
> 莫妮卡，你为什么把我带到这里来？！
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,惯用语">

- **why have you brought me here**：现在完成式——人已经在现场，强调"（你把我）带来了"这个结果（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **of all places**：习语"偏偏是这个地方"（众多地方中偏偏选中它）（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1042.536" duration="1.728" /> **Monica Geller:** You’ll see.
> 你会明白的。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1044.264" duration="2.952" /> **Emily:** I tell you, <Note>this wedding is not going to happen</Note>.
> 我告诉你，这场婚礼不会举行的。
<GrammarTip points="动词时态">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">this wedding</span> <span class="v">is not going to happen</span></p>

- **is not going to happen**：be going to 的否定式"不会举行了"——happen 不用被动（不及物动词无被动）（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

*(At that Ross plugs in some Christmas lights to light the place up.)*
> *（这时罗斯插上了一些圣诞灯，照亮了整个地方。）*

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1047.216" duration="0.888" /> **Emily:** Oh God.
> 哦，天啊。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1048.104" duration="9.816" /> **Ross Geller:** Okay? But-but imagine <Note>a lot more lights</Note>, okay? And-and y’know <Note>fewer bricks</Note>, and-and-and flowers, and candles…
> 好吗？但是想象更多的灯光，好吗？而且你知道的砖头、花和蜡烛都少了…
<GrammarTip points="形容词比较">

- **a lot more lights**：比较级 more + a lot 加强"多得多"（[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)/[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）
- **fewer bricks**：**fewer + 可数复数**（砖可块块数）——不可数才用 less；这条 fewer/less 之分正是本集名句（[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1057.92" duration="8.856" /> **Monica Geller:** And the musicians, look, <Note>they can go over here</Note> (Points to a little alcove), okay? And <Note>the chairs can face this way</Note> (Points), and… (Points to Ross) You go.
> 音乐家们，看，他们可以到这里（指向一个小壁龛），好吗？椅子可以朝这边（指着），然后…（指着罗斯）你走。
<GrammarTip points="语气,副词">

- **they can go over here / the chairs can face this way**：can 表安排"可以（摆）在这边"；face + 方向副词 this way"朝这边"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1066.776" duration="8.76" /> **Ross Geller:** But-but, <Note>if you don’t love this, we’ll do it in any other place at any other time</Note>. Really, it’s fine, <Note>whatever you want</Note>.
> 但是，如果你不喜欢这个，我们会在任何时间在任何其他地方做。真的，没关系，你要什么都行。
<GrammarTip points="副词从句,名词从句">

- **if you don’t love this, we’ll do it...**：if 条件从句现在式 + 主句 will do——主将从现；any other + 单数名词"任何别的"（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）
- **whatever you want**：<p class="svoc"><span class="o">whatever</span> <span class="s">you</span> <span class="v">want</span></p> whatever 引导名词从句"无论你要什么（都行）"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1075.536" duration="1.128" /> **Emily:** It’s perfect.
> 太完美了。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1076.664" duration="3.624" /> **Ross Geller:** And, I don’t know, <Note>if it starts to rain</Note>…
> 而且，我不知道，如果开始下雨…
<GrammarTip points="副词从句,不定词">

- **if it starts to rain**：if 条件句现在式代未来；start + 不定式 to rain"开始下起雨来"（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1080.288" duration="1.44" /> **Emily:** Well then <Note>we’ll get wet</Note>. (They kiss.)
> 那我们就淋湿了。（他们接吻。）
<GrammarTip points="系动词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">we</span> <span class="v">’ll get</span> <span class="c">wet</span></p>

- **get wet**：get 作系动词（台：连缀动词）+ 形容词"被淋湿"——应对上句的 if it rains，浪漫作答（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1081.728" duration="3.744" /> **Monica Geller:** Ohh. And <Note>I don’t even have a date</Note>.
> 哦。我甚至没有约会。
<GrammarTip points="副词,双关">

- **I don’t even have a date**：even 加强否定"连……都没有"；date 一语双关——舞伴/约会对象，看着人家拥吻自嘲（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）

</GrammarTip>

---

🎬 *Monica and Rachel's, Rachel is in her bedroom.*
> 🎬 *莫妮卡和瑞秋的，瑞秋在她的卧室。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：宾语从句陈述语序 / not...until / 表语从句 why / must 表推测

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1085.472" duration="1.536" /> **Rachel Green:** Pheebs?
> 菲比？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1087.008" duration="0.624" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Yeah?
> 是吗？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1087.632" duration="2.712" /> **Rachel Green:** Do you remember <Note>where the duck food is</Note>?
> 你记得鸭子的食物在哪里吗？
<GrammarTip points="名词从句">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">you</span> <span class="v">Do remember</span> <span class="o">where the duck food is</span></p>

- **where the duck food is**：疑问词引导的宾语从句用**陈述语序**（主语 the duck food 在动词 is 前）——疑问句的倒装只在主句 Do you remember 里（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1090.344" duration="3.528" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Yeah, it’s in the guys’ apartment under the sink. Why?
> 是的，在男人的公寓水槽下面。为什么？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1093.872" duration="2.328" /> **Rachel Green:** (enters with a bag packed) Because <Note>I’m going to London</Note>.
> （带着包进来）因为我要去伦敦。
<GrammarTip points="动词时态">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">’m going</span> <span class="a">to London</span></p>

- **I’m going to London**：**现在进行式表将来**——票已下决心要买，"我这就去伦敦"；本集标题级核心句型（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1096.2" duration="2.904" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** What?! <Note>What do you mean you’re going to London</Note>?
> 什么？！你去伦敦是什么意思？
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,疑问句">

<p class="svoc"><span class="o">What</span> <span class="v">do</span> <span class="s">you</span> <span class="v">mean</span></p>

- **What do you mean (that) you’re going to London?**：mean 后接宾语从句省 that，从句保持陈述语序 you're going——插入语 do you mean 不影响从句语序（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1099.104" duration="8.088" /> **Rachel Green:** Yeah, I have to <Note>tell Ross that I love him</Note>. Now honey, you take care, <Note>you don’t have those babies until I get back</Note>. (Kisses her stomach.)
> 是啊，我得告诉罗斯我爱他。亲爱的，你保重，在我回来之前你不能生孩子。（吻了吻她的肚子。）
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,副词从句">

- **tell Ross that I love him**：tell + 人 + that 宾语从句（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **you don’t have those babies until I get back**：**not...until**"直到……才/在……之前不许"；until 时间从句用现在式 get 代未来（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- **you take care**：祈使句"你保重"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1107.192" duration="3.336" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** I—Rachel, you can’t go! Ross loves Emily!
> 我，瑞秋，你不能去！罗斯爱艾米丽！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1110.528" duration="10.824" /> **Rachel Green:** Yeah, I know, I know, <Note>I know he does</Note>. But I have to <Note>tell him how I feel</Note>! <Note>He deserves to have all the information</Note> and then he can make an informed decision.
> 是的，我知道，我知道，我知道他知道。但我必须告诉他我的感受！他应该得到所有的信息，然后才能做出明智的决定。
<GrammarTip points="省略,名词从句,不定词">

- **I know he does**：does 是代动词，替代 loves Emily——"我知道他爱她"（[Ch.16](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter16.md)）
- **tell him how I feel**：how 引导宾语从句"我的感受如何"，陈述语序（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **deserves to have all the information**：deserve + 不定式"理应得到"；**an informed decision** 过去分词 informed 作形容词"（信息充分的）明智决定"（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1121.352" duration="7.584" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** <Note>That’s not why you’re going</Note>! You’re going <Note>because you hope he’s gonna say</Note>, "Yeah, I love you too, Rach. Forget that British chippy."
> 这不是你要去的原因！你去是因为你希望他会说，“是的，我也爱你，瑞秋。忘了那个英国小鬼吧。”
<GrammarTip points="名词从句">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">That</span> <span class="v">’s not</span> <span class="c">why you’re going</span></p>

- **That’s not why you’re going**：why 引导**表语从句**——"那才是你去（伦敦）的原因"，从句陈述语序（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **because you hope (that) he’s gonna say...**：because 从句内再嵌 hope 的宾语从句——一层套一层（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **Forget that British chippy**：祈使句；chippy 英式俚语"轻浮小妞"，菲比现学现卖

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1128.936" duration="2.496" /> **Rachel Green:** Ohh—<Note>Do you think he will</Note>?!
> 哦，你认为他会吗？！
<GrammarTip points="疑问句,省略">

- **Do you think he will (say that)?**：do you think 插入疑问句，从句用陈述语序 he will；will 后省略 say so——她只听见了自己想听的部分（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)/[Ch.16](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter16.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1131.432" duration="13.896" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** No! Because he’s in love with the British chippy! Look, Rachel, <Note>if you go, you’re just gonna mess with his head and ruin his wedding</Note>! Y’know, it’s too late! <Note>You missed you’re chance</Note>! I’m sorry, I know <Note>this must be really hard</Note>, it’s over.
> 不！因为他爱上了英国的奇比！听着，瑞秋，如果你走了，你只会弄乱他的脑袋，毁掉他的婚礼！你知道，太晚了！你错过了机会！对不起，我知道这一定很难，结束了。
<GrammarTip points="副词从句,语气,原文勘误">

- **if you go, you’re just gonna mess with his head and ruin his wedding**：if 条件现在式 + 主句 gonna 将来；mess with 和 ruin 共用一个 gonna（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)）
- ⚠️ 原文 **You missed you're chance** 应为 **your** chance——your/you're 同音误写的经典案例（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）
- **this must be really hard**：**must + 原形**表肯定推测"这一定很难受"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1145.328" duration="6.48" /> **Rachel Green:** Y’know what? No. <Note>It’s not over until someone says, "I do."</Note> (Exits)
> 你知道吗？不，直到有人说“我愿意”，一切才结束
<GrammarTip points="副词从句,双关">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">It</span> <span class="v">’s not over</span> <span class="a">until someone says, "I do."</span></p>

- **not...until + 时间从句**："直到有人说'我愿意'才算完"——从句现在式 says 代未来（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- **"I do"**：直接引语作 says 的宾语；婚礼誓言"我愿意"在此双关——只要誓词没说出口，她就还有机会（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1151.808" duration="7.32" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** I do! I do! I do! (Chases her into the hall, but Rachel doesn’t stop.) I do! (Gives up.) Ugh, <Note>like I can really chase you</Note>. <Note>I’m carrying a litter</Note>.
> 我知道！我愿意！我愿意！（追着她进了大厅，但瑞秋没有停下来。）我停下来了！（放弃）啊，好像我真的能追上你。我带着一个垃圾。
<GrammarTip points="副词从句,双关">

- **like I can really chase you**：口语 like 接从句 = as if"好像我真追得上你似的"（反话——我这样子哪追得动）（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- **I’m carrying a litter**：双关——litter 本义"一窝幼崽"（怀着三胞胎），字面上又像"拎着一筐垃圾"，自嘲负重难行

</GrammarTip>

Commercial Break
> 商业休息

---

🎬 *Moving Shot towards The Waltham House. A phone   is ringing.*
> 🎬 *向沃尔瑟姆家移动的镜头。电话铃响了。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：正式英语 one 作主语 / 介词 + whom / I was wondering if 委婉句 / somebody...their

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1159.128" duration="1.632" /> **Housekeeper:** The Waltham Residence.
> 沃尔瑟姆公寓。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1160.76" duration="4.584" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Oh...yes..<Note>is this</Note>..umm..Emily’s Parents’ house.
> 哦……是的……这是……嗯……艾米丽父母的房子。
<GrammarTip points="疑问句,名词所有格">

- **is this Emily's Parents' house?**：陈述语序 + 升调的电话确认句式；**Parents'** 复数名词所有格只加撇号（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1165.344" duration="13.224" /> **Housekeeper:** <Note>This is the housekeeper speaking</Note>. And by the way, young lady, that is not <Note>how one addresses oneself on the telephone</Note>. First one identifies oneself and then asks for <Note>the person with whom one wishes to speak</Note>.
> 我是管家。顺便说一句，年轻的女士，在电话里不是这样称呼自己的。首先，一个人表明自己的身份，然后要求与之交谈的人。
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,关系从句,正式文体">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1165.344" duration="13.224" />

**原句**：First one identifies oneself and then asks for the person with whom one wishes to speak.

**层次划分**：

- 主干：<p class="svoc"><span class="s">one</span> <span class="v">identifies</span> <span class="o">oneself</span> and <span class="v">asks for</span> <span class="o">the person...</span></p> **one** 作泛指主语"（任何）人"——正式文体的无人称表达，配单数动词（[Ch.11](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter11.md)）
- **with whom one wishes to speak**：定语从句修饰 the person——**介词 with 前置 + whom**（宾格关系代词）是正式语序；口语版本是 who you wish to speak **with**（介词甩到句尾、whom 变 who）（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **This is the housekeeper speaking**：电话自报家门用 This is...speaking（不说 I am...）（惯用语）
- **how one addresses oneself**：how 引导表语从句；oneself 是 one 的反身代词（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

**翻译**：（打电话的规矩是）先自报家门，再说您要找哪一位。

**语法要点**：one/oneself 泛指人称（Ch.11）；介词 + whom 正式关系从句（Ch.14）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1178.568" duration="7.488" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** (In a British accent) This is Phoebe Buffay. <Note>I was wondering</Note>, please, <Note>if-if it’s not too much trouble</Note>, please, umm, <Note>might I speak to Miss Emily Waltham</Note>, please?
> （用英国口音）这是菲比·布菲。我在想，如果不是太麻烦的话，我能和艾米丽·沃尔瑟姆小姐说话吗？
<GrammarTip points="语气,动词时态">

- **I was wondering (if)...**：**过去进行式表委婉**——把请求包在"我刚才一直在想"里，语气瞬间软化；标准礼貌请求模板（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **if it's not too much trouble**：if 引导宾语从句（= whether）"是否太麻烦"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **might I speak to...?**：might 比 may 更谦卑；三个 please 加足卑微感（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1186.056" duration="5.832" /> **Housekeeper:** Miss Waltham, is at the rehearsal dinner and <Note>it’s not polite to make fun of people</Note>. Goodbye.
> 沃尔瑟姆小姐，在排练晚宴上，取笑别人是不礼貌的。再见。
<GrammarTip points="不定词,短语动词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">it</span> <span class="v">’s not</span> <span class="c">polite</span> <span class="s">to make fun of people</span></p>

- **it’s not polite to make fun of people**：it 虚主语，不定式 to make fun of... 是真正主语；make fun of = 取笑（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1191.888" duration="5.304" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** No no no, <Note>I’ll be nice</Note>, I swear!!!   Could you just give me <Note>the number for where they are</Note>?
> 不，不，我会很好的，我发誓！！！你能告诉我他们在哪里吗？
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,名词从句">

- **I’ll be nice, I swear**：will + be + 形容词表"（我保证）会乖的"；I swear 发誓（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **the number for where they are**：**where 名词从句作介词 for 的宾语**——"他们所在之处的（电话）号码"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1197.192" duration="3.888" /> **Housekeeper:** I’m afraid, <Note>I’m not at liberty to divulge that information</Note>.
> 恐怕我无权泄露这些信息。
<GrammarTip points="惯用语,不定词">

- **I’m afraid (that)...**：委婉拒绝的缓冲语"恐怕……"；**be (not) at liberty to do**"（无）有权做某事"——正式表达，不定式作方面状语（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1201.08" duration="12.024" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Ok, <Note>somebody is on their way to ruin wedding</Note> okay. And I have to warn somebody, alright. So <Note>if you don’t give me that number then I’m going to come over there and kick your snooty ass all the way to New Glocken..shire</Note>.
> 好吧，有人要破坏婚礼了，好吧。我必须警告某人，好吗。所以如果你不给我那个号码，我就去那里踢你的臭屁股，一直踢到新格洛克郡。
<GrammarTip points="主谓一致,副词从句,幽默">

- **somebody is on their way**：单数不定代词 somebody 配**复数代词 their**——口语里通行（语法书传统写法是 his or her way）；be on one's way to do"正要去/正在路上"（[Ch.11](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter11.md)）
- **if you don’t give me that number, then I’m going to come... and kick...**：if 条件现在式 + 主句 be going to 将来；两个并列谓语 come / kick 共用一个 going to（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)）
- **all the way to New Glocken..shire**：生造英式郡名（-shire 后缀）——菲比的英式威胁，配合她的假口音（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1213.104" duration="0.936" /> **Housekeeper:** Hangs up.
> 挂断。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1214.04" duration="3.336" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Hello, Hello. Ohh, OHH, <Note>she knew I could kick her ass</Note>.
> 你好，你好。哦，哦，她知道我可以踢她的屁股。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,动词时态,反讽">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">she</span> <span class="v">knew</span> <span class="o">(that) I could kick her ass</span></p>

- **she knew (that) I could kick her ass**：宾语从句省 that；主句过去式 knew，从句 can 变 could（时态呼应）；阿Q 式反讽——对方挂断=怕了我（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

---

🎬 *Rehearsal dinner hall. Ross and Emily are standing in the reception area. Monica arrives with her parents.*
> 🎬 *排练宴会厅。罗斯和艾米丽站在接待区。莫妮卡和她的父母一起到达。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：Here comes 倒装 / should never have done / nice of you to do / how 感叹式宾语从句

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1217.376" duration="1.488" /> **Monica Geller:** Hey.
> 嘿。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1218.864" duration="4.248" /> **Ross Geller:** (Ross hugs his mom and dad)Hi. Mom. Dad.
> （罗斯拥抱他的爸爸妈妈）嗨。妈妈。爸爸。

Mrs. Geller: Sweetheart. Oh sorry <Note>were late</Note>, my fault, <Note>I insisted on riding the tube</Note>.
> 亲爱的。哦，对不起，我们迟到了，是我的错，我坚持坐地铁。
<GrammarTip points="原文勘误,动名词,惯用语">

- ⚠️ 原文 **sorry were late** 应为 sorry **we're** late（听写漏词）——(We're) sorry we're late 的紧缩
- **insisted on riding the tube**：insist on + **动名词**"坚持要"；the tube = 伦敦地铁（英式说法）（[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

Mr. Geller: (embarrassed) Judy, the kids..
> （尴尬地）朱迪，孩子们。。

Mrs. Geller: Jack, <Note>that’s what they call the subway</Note>.
> 杰克，那就是他们所说的地铁。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">that</span> <span class="v">’s</span> <span class="c">what they call the subway</span></p>

- **that’s what they call the subway**：what 引导**表语从句** = the thing that they call...——"他们（英国人）管地铁就叫这个"；call + 宾语 + 名词补语的结构收进 what 从句（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

Mr. Geller: Ohh, I thought that you....
> 哦，我还以为你。。。。

Ross and **Monica Geller: **Dad, dad. <Note>We got it</Note>!! We Got It!!!

> 罗斯和**莫妮卡·盖勒：**爸爸，爸爸。我们成功了！！我们明白了！！！
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,惯用语">

- **We got it**：got it = 明白了/领会了——过去式表"（刚刚）领悟"；此处兄妹俩慌忙打断爸爸，怕他往下说（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1223.112" duration="5.88" /> **Emily:** Ohh, <Note>here comes my dad and stepmum</Note>. Mister and Misses Geller, <Note>this is Steven and Andrea Waltham</Note>.
> 哦，我爸爸和继母来了。盖勒先生和小姐，这是史蒂文和安德里亚·沃尔瑟姆。
<GrammarTip points="倒装,惯用语">

<p class="svoc"><span class="a">here</span> <span class="v">comes</span> <span class="s">my dad and stepmum</span></p>

- **Here comes my dad and stepmum**：here 置句首 + 主语是**名词** → **完全倒装**（comes 提到主语前）；对照 Here **we** go（代词主语不倒装）——本集两种倒装凑齐（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）
- **this is Steven and Andrea Waltham**：介绍人用 this is...；stepmum 继母（英式 mum）

</GrammarTip>

Mr. Waltham: (Shaking everyone's hand.) Hello. Hello. <Note>How do you do</Note>? How do you do? <Note>Very nice to meet you</Note>. (Looking over at his wife.) Darling it’s the Gellers. (She pays no attention she’s talking on a cellular phone.) (Louder) Darling, it’s the Gellers. (She’s still not responding.) She’s very self-absorbed, you know. <Note>I should never have married her</Note>.
> （和大家握手）你好。你好你好？你好？很高兴认识你。（看着他的妻子）亲爱的，是盖勒夫妇。（她没有注意到她在用手机说话。）（大声一点）亲爱的，是盖勒夫妇。（她仍然没有反应。）你知道，她非常专心致志。我不该娶她。
<GrammarTip points="惯用语,不定词,语气">

- **How do you do?**：正式初见问候——按礼节对方也回 How do you do?，不需要回答内容（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **(It's) very nice to meet you**：省略 it is，不定式 to meet you 是真正主语（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）
- **I should never have married her**：**should have + 过去分词 + never**——"（当初）真不该娶她"，对过去的虚拟懊悔，与本集 shouldn't have said/lied 同族（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **self-absorbed**：复合形容词"自我陶醉的"（self + 过去分词）（[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)）

</GrammarTip>

Mrs. Waltham: (Looking evilly at her husband) Sorry, what?
> （邪恶地看着她的丈夫）对不起，什么？

Mr. Waltham: It’s the Gellers!
> 是盖勒家！

Mrs. Waltham: Where?
> 在哪里？

Mr. Waltham: Well <Note>there’s one</Note> (pointing towards Jack) and <Note>there’s another</Note> (pointing towards Judy).
> 有一个（指着杰克）还有一个（指着朱迪）。
<GrammarTip points="存在句,代词">

- **there's one... and there's another**：There be 存在句 + 不定代词 **one... another...**"一个……另一个……"——把岳父岳母当展品指认，冷幽默（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

Mrs. Waltham: Lovely to meet you.
> 很高兴见到你。

Mr. Waltham: <Note>Terribly nice of you to offer to pay for half the wedding</Note>. (He hand a multipage bill to Jack.)
> 非常感谢你提出支付婚礼的一半费用。（他递给杰克一张多页的钞票。）
<GrammarTip points="不定词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">(It’s)</span> <span class="c">Terribly nice</span> <span class="a">of you</span> <span class="s">to offer to pay...</span></p>

- **nice of you to offer...**：不定式的逻辑主语用 **of you**——形容词 nice 描述"人好"用 of；若描述事情性质才用 for you；offer to do 主动提出；pay for 付账（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）
- **terribly**：程度副词"非常"（英式礼貌夸张）（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）

</GrammarTip>

Mr. Geller: Ohh forget it. <Note>Too hell with tradition</Note>, <Note>we’re happy to do it</Note>.
> 哦，算了吧。传统太糟糕了，我们很乐意这么做。
<GrammarTip points="原文勘误,惯用语,不定词">

- ⚠️ 原文 **Too hell with tradition** 应为 **To hell with tradition**——"去他的传统"（诅咒式习语，介词 to 听写成了 too）
- **we’re happy to do it**：be happy + 不定式"很乐意（出这笔钱）"——咬牙切齿的客套（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）

</GrammarTip>

Mrs. Geller: We know <Note>how expensive weddings can be</Note>, besides this may be <Note>the only wedding we get to throw</Note> (patting Monica on the shoulder.).
> 我们知道婚礼有多贵，而且这可能是我们唯一可以举办的婚礼（拍莫妮卡的肩膀）。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,关系从句">

- **how expensive weddings can be**：how + 形容词的**感叹式宾语从句**，作 know 的宾语——陈述语序 weddings can be（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **the only wedding (that) we get to throw**：定语从句省关系词；**get to do** = 有机会做；throw a wedding"操办婚礼"——拍 Monica 肩膀的潜台词：她可能嫁不出去（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)/[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1228.992" duration="3.48" /> **Monica Geller:** Ha ha, <Note>a joke that’s funny in all countries</Note>.
> 哈哈，一个在所有国家都很有趣的笑话。
<GrammarTip points="关系从句,反讽">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">a joke</span> <span class="v">’s</span> <span class="c">funny in all countries</span></p>

- **a joke that's funny in all countries**：定语从句 that's funny... 修饰 joke；Monica 干笑自嘲——这玩笑放哪个国家都"好笑"（反讽：到哪儿都被催婚）（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)）

</GrammarTip>

*(Ross quickly directs the families to their tables.)*
> *（罗斯很快将这些家庭带到他们的桌子上。）*

---

🎬 *Chandler and Joey are standing by the kitchen entrance. A waiter comes out.*
> 🎬 *钱德勒和乔伊站在厨房门口，一个服务员走了出来。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：不可数名词 food / It's been + 时段 / want + 宾语 + 不定式 / 习语 a piece of my mind

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1232.472" duration="0.888" /> **Waiter:** Sir? (Looking at Joey.)
> 先生？（看着乔伊。）

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1233.36" duration="1.728" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** What’s in it?
> 里面有什么？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1235.088" duration="3.264" /> **Waiter:** Goat cheese, water chestnuts, and panchetta. (Joey Looks down disgustingly at the food.)
> 山羊奶酪、荸荠和薄饼。（乔伊厌恶地低头看着食物。）

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1238.352" duration="14.52" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** (Looking up at the waiter)<Note>That’s not food</Note>...No, I don’t, no...(Taps Chandler on the shoulder.) <Note>Everything's different here</Note>...I want to go home. I...I miss my family. I miss the coffee house. <Note>I can’t even remember what Phoebe looks like</Note>.
> （抬头看着服务员）那不是食物…不，我不，不…（轻拍钱德勒的肩膀）这里一切都不一样…我想回家。我…我想念我的家人。我想念咖啡馆。我都不记得菲比长什么样了。
<GrammarTip points="名词,名词从句">

- **That’s not food**：food 不可数名词——"那根本不叫吃的"，不加 a（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）
- **Everything's different here**：复合不定代词 + 单数's；different 作补语（[Ch.11](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter11.md)）
- **what Phoebe looks like**：<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">can’t remember</span> <span class="o">what Phoebe looks like</span></p> what 引导宾语从句，**陈述语序**；what does sb look like 问长相，收进从句后不倒装（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1252.872" duration="12.264" /> **Chandler Bing:** Joey, <Note>it’s been three days</Note>, okay.. <Note>Your just a little homesick</Note>, Okay. <Note>Would you just try to relax</Note>. Just, just try to enjoy yourself.
> 乔伊，已经三天了，好吧。。你只是有点想家，好吧。你能试着放松一下吗。只是，只是试着享受一下。
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,原文勘误,语气">

- **it’s been three days**：**现在完成式**——"（到现在）才三天"；It's been + 时段表从某事开始至今的时长（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- ⚠️ 原文 **Your just a little homesick** 应为 **You're**——your/you're 同音误写（本集剧本已多次出现）
- **Would you just try to relax**：Would you just...? 委婉中带不耐烦的请求；try to do 努力做；enjoy yourself 玩得开心（反身代词固定搭配）（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1265.136" duration="4.392" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** (Pointing  at Chandler.) <Note>Your different here too</Note>. Your mean in England. (Chandler throws his hands up to his head in frustration. They walk away from each other.)
> （指着钱德勒）你在这里也不一样。你的意思是在英国。（钱德勒沮丧地把手举到头上。他们彼此走开了。）
<GrammarTip points="原文勘误">

- ⚠️ 原文两处 **Your** 都应为 **You're**（= You are）：You're different here too / You're mean in England——mean 此处是形容词"刻薄的"

</GrammarTip>

[Camera pans to the Geller family table. Ross, Rachel, Mr. and Mrs. Geller are there.]
> [镜头移到盖勒家的桌子上。罗斯、瑞秋、盖勒先生和夫人在那里。]

Mr. Geller: (Looking at ht wedding bill.) What the hell!!!
> （看着ht婚礼账单。）见鬼！！！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1269.528" duration="2.136" /> **Ross Geller:** what’s up, Dad?
> 怎么了，爸爸？

Mr. Geller: This bill for my half of the wedding. it’s insane.
> 这是我婚礼一半的账单。这太疯狂了。

Mrs. Geller: <Note>How could it be so much</Note>? The receptions at their house.
> 怎么会这么多？他们家的招待会。
<GrammarTip points="疑问句,语气,原文勘误">

- **How could it be so much?**：could 表"怎么可能"；so much（钱）作主语补语——数额用 much 不用 many（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）
- ⚠️ 原文 **The receptions at their house** 应为 The **reception's** at their house（招待会在他们家办，所有格听写失真）

</GrammarTip>

Mr. Geller: (Pointing items out on the bill.) Flowers, liquor, recarpet first floor. New guest bath, landscaping. <Note>I’m paying to remodel this guys house</Note>. (Angrily gets up.) I’m going to give that son on a bitch, <Note>a piece of my mind</Note>.
> （指着账单上的项目。）鲜花，酒，一楼的咖啡。新客人浴室，景观美化。我付钱来改造这所房子。（怒气冲冲地站起来）我要狠狠揍那个狗娘养的，狠狠揍他一顿。
<GrammarTip points="不定词,惯用语,原文勘误">

- **I’m paying to remodel this guy's house**：pay (money) **to do**——不定式表花钱的目的"我这是在出钱给他装修房子"；进行式表正在发生的荒谬事（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- ⚠️ 原文 **this guys house** 应为 this **guy's** house（所有格）；**son on a bitch** 应为 son **of** a bitch（粗口听写失真）
- **give sb a piece of my mind**：习语"把某人狠狠数落一顿"——give + 间接宾语 + 直接宾语的双宾结构（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1271.664" duration="14.328" /> **Ross Geller:** (Holding him back.)Dad, dad, please. Look <Note>I don’t want anything to upset Emily tonight</Note>. Alright, <Note>she's had a hard enough couple of days as it is</Note>. (Picks up the bill.) Now here, here, <Note>let me go talk to him</Note>, okay?
> （拦住他）爸爸，爸爸，求你了。听着，我今晚不想让艾米丽难过。好吧，她已经够辛苦的了。（拿起账单）现在，让我去和他谈谈，好吗？
<GrammarTip points="不定词,动词时态,惯用语">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">don’t want</span> <span class="o">anything</span> <span class="c">to upset Emily</span></p>

- **want anything to upset Emily**：want + 宾语 + 不定式宾补"不想让任何事惹 Emily 难过"（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）
- **she's had a hard enough couple of days**：现在完成式"这几天（一直）够难熬"；enough 后置修饰形容词 hard（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）
- **as it is**：习语"照目前情况（就已经）"（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）
- **let me go talk to him**：let + 宾语 + 原形 go；go talk 是口语连动（= go and talk）（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

</GrammarTip>

Mr. Geller: And you tell him <Note>no one takes advantage of the Gellers</Note>.
> 你告诉他没有人利用盖勒家。
<GrammarTip points="主谓一致,名词词组">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">no one</span> <span class="v">takes advantage of</span> <span class="o">the Gellers</span></p>

- **no one takes**：no one 永远单数，动词加 -s；take advantage of = 占……便宜（[Ch.11](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter11.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **the Gellers**：the + 姓氏复数 = 盖勒一家人（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

Mrs. Geller: Ooh, Jack....(He looks over to her) Sometimes I forget <Note>how powerful you can be</Note>. (They embrace and kiss passionately.)
> 噢，杰克……（他看着她）有时候我忘了你有多强大。（他们热情地拥抱和亲吻。）
<GrammarTip points="名词从句">

- **how powerful you can be**：how + 形容词的感叹式宾语从句，作 forget 的宾语——陈述语序 you can be（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1285.992" duration="2.496" /> **Monica Geller:** (Looking nauseous from her parents kissing.) And <Note>I’m going to go get drunk</Note>. (Gets up to get a drink.)
> （从她父母的接吻中看起来很恶心。）我要去喝醉了。（起身去喝一杯。）
<GrammarTip points="连动,分词">

- **go get drunk**：口语连动式 = go **and** get drunk；get + 形容词 drunk"喝醉"（get 表状态变化）（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)）

</GrammarTip>

---

🎬 *An airport in New York.*
> 🎬 *纽约的一个机场*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：分词后置定语 / 强调助动词 do / 否定前移 / 数词 hundred

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1288.488" duration="3.984" /> **Rachel Green:** (Running to the ticket counter) Ooh, ooh, ooh,ooh,ooh. (Slightly out of breath) Hi.
> （跑到售票处）哦，哦，哦，哦，哦，哦。（有点上气不接下气）嗨。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1292.472" duration="0.792" /> **Ticket Agent:** (Cheerfully.) Hello.
> （高兴地）你好。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1293.264" duration="4.608" /> **Rachel Green:** (Faking cheerfulness.) Hello. Umm, <Note>when is your next flight to London</Note>?
> （假装高兴）你好。嗯，你下一班去伦敦的航班是什么时候？
<GrammarTip points="疑问句">

- **when is your next flight...?**：特殊疑问句主句要倒装（when **is** your next flight）——对照宾语从句 where the duck food **is** 的不倒装，两种语序本集可对照记（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1297.872" duration="2.304" /> **Ticket Agent:** (looking at her computer terminal) <Note>There’s one leaving in thirty minutes</Note>.
> （看着她的电脑终端）30分钟后还有一个。
<GrammarTip points="存在句,分词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="a">There</span> <span class="v">’s</span> <span class="s">one</span> <span class="a">leaving in thirty minutes</span></p>

- **one leaving in thirty minutes**：There be 存在句；**现在分词短语 leaving... 作后置定语**修饰 one（= one that is leaving...）——分词简化关系从句（[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)/[Ch.16](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter16.md)）
- **in thirty minutes**：in + 时段表"……之后"（将来）（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1300.176" duration="1.944" /> **Rachel Green:** Ohh, good.
> 哦，很好。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1302.12" duration="2.016" /> **Ticket Agent:** And <Note>I do have one seat left</Note>.
> 我还有一个座位。
<GrammarTip points="强调,分词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">do have</span> <span class="o">one seat left</span></p>

- **I **do** have**：助动词 do 强调谓语"（确实）还有"——回应她的急切（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **one seat left**：过去分词 left 作后置定语"剩下的（座位）"（[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1304.136" duration="3.408" /> **Rachel Green:** Ohh, thank you, thank you, thank you.
> 哦，谢谢，谢谢，谢谢。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1307.544" duration="4.032" /> **Ticket Agent:** <Note>The last minute fare</Note> on this ticket is <Note>twenty seven hundred dollars</Note>.
> 这张票的最后一分钟票价是两千七百美元。
<GrammarTip points="名词词组,数词">

- **The last minute fare**：last minute"临起飞前的"作定语修饰 fare（票价）；规范写法常加连字符 last-minute fare（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）
- **twenty seven hundred dollars**：hundred 前有具体数字时**不加 s**（two thousand seven hundred 的口语念法）（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1311.576" duration="4.032" /> **Rachel Green:** (looking through her wallet.) Ohh, <Note>I just don’t think I have enough left on my credit card</Note>.
> （翻了翻钱包。）哦，我觉得我的信用卡上的钱不够了。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,否定前移">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">don’t think</span> <span class="o">(that) I have enough left...</span></p>

- **I don't think I have enough...**：**否定前移**——语义上是否定从句（我觉得额度不够了），形式上 not 移到主句 think 前；think/believe/suppose 族动词的固定习惯（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **enough left on my credit card**：left"剩余的"后置；on my credit card 用介词 on（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1315.608" duration="2.256" /> **Ticket Agent:** Well <Note>you can split it with another credit card</Note>.
> 嗯，你可以用另一张信用卡把它分开。
<GrammarTip points="短语动词">

- **split it with another credit card**：split sth (with sb/sth)"分摊/分开付"；it 指代票价（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1317.864" duration="5.16" /> **Rachel Green:** Ohh, okay, <Note>how about five</Note>. (She hands her all the credit cards.) Ohh, thank you.
> 哦，好吧，五个怎么样。（她把所有的信用卡都递给了她。）哦，谢谢。
<GrammarTip points="惯用语">

- **How about five (cards)?**：How about + 名词"……（张）行不行"——把五张卡全递过去的豪赌（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1323.024" duration="1.824" /> **Ticket Agent:** <Note>I’m just going to need to see your passport</Note>.
> 我只是想看看你的护照。
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,不定词">

- **I'm going to need to see...**：be going to + need + 不定式——柜台人员的职业缓冲语"（待会儿）得请您出示护照"，比直接说 Show me... 委婉（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1324.848" duration="13.08" /> **Rachel Green:** (Looking through her purse.) Okay, you know what? I don’t have it, but <Note>I can tell you exactly where it is on my night stand</Note>, and...okay. But you know what? <Note>I have my drivers license and I have a twenty</Note>. (She slides it across the counter.)
> （翻钱包）好吧，你知道吗？我没有，但我可以告诉你它在我的床头柜上的确切位置，好的。但是你知道吗？我有我的驾驶执照，我有一张20美元的支票。（她把它滑过柜台。）
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,名词词组,原文勘误">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">can tell</span> <span class="o">you</span> <span class="o">where it is...</span></p>

- **tell you where it is**：双宾语 + where 宾语从句（**陈述语序** it is）——"告诉你它（在家里的）确切位置"，于事无补的抢答（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- ⚠️ 原文 **drivers license** 应为 **driver's** license（所有格撇号漏写）
- **a twenty**：= a twenty-dollar bill——数字名词化"一张二十的（钞票）"，此处想拿来贿赂（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1337.928" duration="3.84" /> **Ticket Agent:** (Slides the twenty back and tosses her credit card onto the counter.)
> （把20号滑回去，把她的信用卡扔到柜台上。）

---

🎬 *The Rehearsal dinner hall. Ross is at the Walthams’ table discussing the bill.*
> 🎬 *排练宴会厅。罗斯在沃尔瑟姆家的桌子旁讨论账单。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：习语 kiss sth goodbye / 双宾语 / 口语省略 if 的条件句

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1341.768" duration="10.416" /> **Ross Geller:** Look, <Note>face it</Note>, my father is not going to pay for the build-in barbecue and believe me <Note>you can kiss you gazebo goodbye</Note>. Now <Note>I might be able to get you the new lawn</Note>.
> 听着，面对现实吧，我父亲不会为内置烤肉付钱的，相信我，你可以和露台吻别。现在我也许能给你弄到新草坪了。
<GrammarTip points="惯用语,双宾语,原文勘误">

- **face it**：祈使句"面对现实吧"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **kiss your gazebo goodbye**：习语 kiss sth goodbye"跟……说拜拜/泡汤"——凉亭别想要了；⚠️ 原文 **kiss you gazebo** 应为 kiss **your** gazebo；**build-in** 应为 **built-in**（过去分词构成的复合形容词"内置的"）（[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)）
- **I might be able to get you the new lawn**：might + be able to"也许能"；**get you the lawn** 双宾语 = get the lawn for you（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

</GrammarTip>

Mr. Waltham: Ahh, then you have to <Note>give us the lawn ornaments</Note>.
> 啊，那你得把草坪装饰物给我们。
<GrammarTip points="双宾语">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">you</span> <span class="v">have to give</span> <span class="o">us</span> <span class="o">the lawn ornaments</span></p>

- **give us the lawn ornaments**：give + 人 + 物双宾语；lawn ornaments 草坪装饰（复合名词）（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1352.184" duration="4.488" /> **Ross Geller:** <Note>I go back there with lawn ornaments</Note>, he’s going to laugh in my face.
> 我带着草坪装饰回去，他会当着我的面笑的。
<GrammarTip points="副词从句,惯用语">

- **I go back there with lawn ornaments, he's going to laugh...**：口语条件句——省略 if，两个分句直接相接（= If I go back..., he's going to...）；从句现在式 go、主句将来式（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- **laugh in my face**：当面嘲笑（介词 in）（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

Mrs. Waltham: This is ridiculous. I mean <Note>we had an agreement</Note>. (Ross looks frustrated. She begins to scream at her husband.) <Note>Will you say something</Note>, Steven?! Please!!!
> 这太荒谬了。我是说我们有协议。（罗斯看起来很沮丧。她开始对丈夫尖叫。）你能说点什么吗，史蒂文？！请
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,语气">

- **we had an agreement**：过去式——协议是"（早先）达成的"；have an agreement 达成协议（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **Will you say something?!**：Will you...? 表不耐烦的催促"你到底说句话行不行"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

Mr. Waltham: <Note>Don’t take that tone with me</Note>. (She looks evilly at him.) All-all right you can. (He looks over at Ross and Shrugs.)
> 别用那种语气跟我说话。（她邪恶地看着他）好吧，你可以。（他看着罗斯耸耸肩。）
<GrammarTip points="祈使句,惯用语">

- **Don't take that tone with me**：take a (certain) tone with sb"用……口气跟某人说话"；否定祈使句——硬气半句就秒怂 All right you can（喜剧节奏）（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

---

🎬 *The Girls apartment. Phoebe is dialing the phone and Rachel runs in the door.*
> 🎬 *女孩公寓。菲比正在拨电话，瑞秋跑进了门。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：want + 宾语 + 不定式 / be always doing 抱怨句型 / change one's mind

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1356.672" duration="2.136" /> **Rachel Green:** (Running to her room.) Hi, Pheebs.
> （跑回她的房间）嗨，菲比。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1358.808" duration="10.608" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** (Looking relieved. She puts down the phone.) Oh thank god. Oh, <Note>you changed your mind</Note>. Oh, look I know <Note>you probably want to be alone</Note>, and you don’t want to talk about it, that’s fine. <Note>I just want you to know</Note>, I think <Note>you are doing the right thing</Note> and...
> （看起来松了一口气。她放下电话。）哦，感谢上帝。哦，你改变主意了。哦，听着，我知道你可能想一个人呆着，你不想谈论这件事，没关系。我只是想让你知道，我认为你做的是对的，而且。。。
<GrammarTip points="惯用语,不定词,动词时态">

- **you changed your mind**：change one's mind"改变主意"——过去式表刚发生的决定（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **you probably want to be alone**：know + 宾语从句（省 that）；want to be + 形容词"想一个人待着"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）
- **I just want you to know**：<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">want</span> <span class="o">you</span> <span class="c">to know</span></p> want + 宾语 + 不定式宾补（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）
- **you are doing the right thing**：现在进行式"你（现在）做的是对的"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1369.416" duration="2.112" /> **Rachel Green:** (Running back out the door with her passport.) Bye, Pheebs.
> （拿着护照跑回门外）再见，菲比。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1371.528" duration="9.192" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** (Trying to get up.) Wait!! <Note>Where are you going</Note>?! What are you doing?! No!! Wait!! (Not able to get up.) God!! <Note>Why am I always pregnant when she does that</Note>?!
> （试图站起来）等等！！你要去哪里？！你在做什么？不等待（站不起来）天哪！！为什么她这么做的时候我总是怀孕？！
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,副词从句">

- **Where are you going? / What are you doing?**：进行式问"（这就要）去哪儿/干嘛"——眼下动作用进行式（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **Why am I always pregnant when she does that?**：**be always + 形容词/分词**的抱怨句型"怎么老是我……"；when 时间从句用 does（代动词，替代 runs off）（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）

</GrammarTip>

---

🎬 *The Rehearsal Dinner Hall. Chandler, Joey, Ross, Emily, Monica, and all the bridal party are seated at the table. Chandler gets up to make a toast.*
> 🎬 *彩排宴会厅。钱德勒、乔伊、罗斯、艾米丽、莫妮卡和所有的婚礼派对都坐在桌旁。钱德勒站起来敬酒。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：现在完成式 + for 时段 / 过去将来 / 双关语 toast / be excited that 从句

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1380.72" duration="26.952" /> **Chandler Bing:** <Note>I’d like to toast, Ross and Emily</Note>. Of course, my big toast will be tomorrow at the wedding, so this is kind of my little toast or Melba toast, if you will. (No one in the room laughs. He starts to get flustered.) Okay. <Note>I known Ross for a long time</Note>. In fact, <Note>I knew him when he was going out with his first girlfriend</Note>. (Ross looks embarrassed.) And I thought <Note>things were going to work out for him</Note>..Until the day he over inflated her. (He laughs. Jack looks at Judy and no one in the room laughs.) Ohh, Dear God.. (A cellular phone rings.)
> 我想干杯，罗斯和艾米丽。当然，我的大吐司将在明天的婚礼上，所以这是我的小吐司或梅尔巴吐司，如果你愿意的话。（房间里没人笑。他开始慌乱起来。）好的。我认识罗斯很久了。事实上，他和第一个女朋友约会时我就认识他了。（罗斯看起来很尴尬。）我还以为他会成功的……直到有一天他给她过度充气。（他笑了。杰克看着朱迪，屋里没有人笑。）哦，亲爱的上帝。。（手机响了。）
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,动词时态,双关">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1380.72" duration="26.952" />

**原句**：I thought things were going to work out for him... until the day he over-inflated her.

**层次划分**：

- **I’d like to toast Ross and Emily**：would like + 不定式"我想敬一杯"；**toast 双关**——祝酒词/吐司面包，于是有了 big toast / little toast / Melba toast（一种薄脆吐司）的谐音滑坡（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）
- **if you will**：插入语习语"如果您允许我这么（比喻）的话"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- ⚠️ 原文 **I known Ross for a long time** 应为 **I've known** Ross...——现在完成式 + for 时段"认识他很久了"，听写漏了 've（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **I knew him when he was going out with his first girlfriend**：when 时间从句用过去进行式铺背景；go out with sb 与某人交往（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **things were going to work out for him**：<p class="svoc"><span class="s">things</span> <span class="v">were going to work out</span></p> **过去将来式**——"（当时）眼看就要顺了"；work out 顺利（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **until the day (when) he over-inflated her**：until 时间从句；the day 后省略关系副词 when；over-inflate"过度充气"——包袱：那"女朋友"是充气娃娃（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)）

**翻译**：我原以为他那段恋情会有结果的……直到他把"她"打爆气的那天。

**语法要点**：完成式 + for 时段（Ch.3）；过去将来（Ch.3）；the day (when) 关系词省略（Ch.14）

</GrammarTip>

Mrs. Waltham: Hello, Waltham Interiors.
> 你好，沃尔瑟姆室内设计公司。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1407.672" duration="6.696" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Oh, hi, Mrs. Waltham. I need to speak with <Note>ether one of the best men</Note>, or Ross’s sister Monica.
> 哦，嗨，沃尔瑟姆太太。我需要找一个最好的男人或者罗斯的妹妹莫妮卡谈谈。
<GrammarTip points="原文勘误,名词词组">

- ⚠️ 原文 **ether one** 应为 **either** one——**either one of the best men**"（两位）伴郎中的任何一位"；one of + 复数名词（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）
- **Ross's sister Monica**：Monica 作 sister 的同位语（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

Mrs. Waltham: Who is this?
> 这是谁？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1414.368" duration="4.128" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Oh, I’m Phoebe Buffay. I’m <Note>one of Ross’s best friends</Note>.
> 哦，我是菲比·布菲。我是罗斯最好的朋友之一。
<GrammarTip points="名词词组">

- **one of Ross's best friends**：one of + 所有格 + **最高级 + 复数名词**——"最好的朋友之一"（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)/[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)）

</GrammarTip>

Mrs. Waltham: Well, if you’re on e of Ross’s best friends, why aren’t you here?
> 好吧，如果你在罗斯最好朋友的e台上，你为什么不在这里？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1418.496" duration="3.864" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Yeah, um, I can’t fly. <Note>I’m having my brother’s babies</Note>.
> 是的，嗯，我不会飞。我要生我哥哥的孩子了。
<GrammarTip points="动词时态">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">’m having</span> <span class="o">my brother’s babies</span></p>

- **I'm having my brother's babies**：have a baby 生孩子；现在进行式表"怀着/快要生"——替弟弟代孕三胞胎是第四季剧情线；注意 babies 复数（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

Mrs. Waltham: Oh, am I on the radio?
> 哦，我在广播里吗？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1422.36" duration="4.512" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** No..umm, could I talk to one of them? It’s very very important.
> 不……嗯，我能和他们中的一个谈谈吗？这非常重要。

Mrs. Waltham: No, <Note>I’m bored with you</Note> now. I’m going to <Note>cut you off</Note>. (She hangs up.)
> 不，我现在对你感到厌烦了。我要打断你的话。（她挂断了。）
<GrammarTip points="分词,短语动词">

- **I'm bored with you**：bored"感到厌烦的"（过去分词作形容词，修饰人）——对比 boring"令人厌烦的"（修饰事）（[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)）
- **cut you off**：挂断/打断（电话）；可分短语动词，代词宾语 you 夹中间（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1426.872" duration="2.976" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Ohh! Okay, I’m going to have to kick her ass too.
> 哦！好吧，我也得踢她的屁股。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1429.848" duration="23.736" /> **Chandler Bing:** (Continuing his toast.) And I’m sure <Note>we’re all very excited that Ross and Emily are getting married at Montgomery Hall</Note>. I mean to think, <Note>my friend getting married in Monty Hall</Note>. (No reaction from the people.) Ohh, come on!! Monty Hall!! <Note>Lets make a Deal</Note>!! Come on, you people!! All right, forget it!! Congratulations, Ross and Emily. (He sits down.)
> （继续祝酒）我相信罗斯和艾米丽要在蒙哥马利大厅结婚了，我们都很兴奋。我想，我的朋友在蒙蒂霍尔结婚了。（人们没有反应。）哦，来吧！！蒙蒂霍尔！！让我们做个交易！！来吧，你们这些人！！好吧，算了吧！！恭喜你，罗斯和艾米丽。（他坐了下来。）
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,动名词,双关,原文勘误">

- **we're all very excited (that) Ross and Emily are getting married...**：形容词 excited 后接 **that 名词从句**（宾语性质的从句）；get married 进行式表将来（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **my friend getting married in Monty Hall**：动名词复合结构——my friend 是 getting married 的逻辑主语（口语中所有格 's 常省）（[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md)）
- ⚠️ 原文 **Lets make a Deal** 应为 **Let's make a Deal**——且 Let's Make a Deal 是美国老牌竞猜节目名，主持人就叫 **Monty Hall**——这是 Chandler 的包袱所在，可惜英国人没接住（双关 + 文化梗）
- **you people**："你们这些人"——带点火上头的称呼（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1453.584" duration="25.752" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Hey, <Note>best man number two</Note>, Joey Tribbiani. Now I’m not good with the jokes like Chandler here. Boy...but ahh, I just want to say congratulation to the happy couple. I first met Ross in this coffee house back home...Home...New York City...<Note>Where everybody knows my name</Note>. Well anyway, I love you guys. (pointing at everyone.) But <Note>not as much as I love America</Note>. (Looking at Chandler.) <Note>Could we please..go home now</Note>?
> 嘿，二号伴郎乔伊·特里比亚尼。现在我不擅长像钱德勒这样的笑话。男孩…但是啊，我只想向这对幸福的夫妇表示祝贺。我第一次见到罗斯是在家里的咖啡馆里…家里…纽约市…那里每个人都知道我的名字。不管怎样，我爱你们。（指着每个人）但没有我那么爱美国。（看着钱德勒）我们现在可以回家了吗？
<GrammarTip points="同位语,关系从句,形容词比较">

- **best man number two, Joey Tribbiani**：Joey Tribbiani 是 best man number two 的同位语（自我介绍式）（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）
- **(New York City)...Where everybody knows my name**：关系副词 where 引导定语从句修饰 New York City——直接引用《Cheers》主题曲歌词（想家的乔伊）（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)）
- **not as much as I love America**：as much as 同级比较的否定式——"（爱你们）但比不上爱美国"（[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)）
- **Could we please go home now?**：could + please 恳求句型（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

*(One of the bridesmaids, Felicity, puts her arm around Joey.)*
> *（其中一位伴娘费利西蒂挽着乔伊。）*

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1479.336" duration="2.904" /> **Felicity:** (Putting her arm around Joey.) Are you going home? <Note>I was hoping to get to know you better</Note>.
> （搂着乔伊）你要回家吗？我希望能更好地了解你。
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,不定词,形容词比较">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">was hoping</span> <span class="o">to get to know you better</span></p>

- **I was hoping to...**：**过去进行式表委婉**（= I hope...）——搭讪的柔软开场（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **get to know you better**：get to do"得以/渐渐"；比较级 better"更深入地"（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1482.24" duration="3.024" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** (Putting his arm around her.) <Note>I’m not going anywhere</Note>, sweetheart.
> （搂住她）我哪儿也不去，甜心。
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,双关">

- **I'm not going anywhere**：进行式表将来"我哪儿都不去了"——一秒叛变（刚才还要回家）；not...anywhere = nowhere（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）

</GrammarTip>

---

🎬 *Later that evening at the rehearsal dinner. Chandler and Monica are sitting on a sofa. Chandler is covering his face in embarrassment because of the toast.*
> 🎬 *那天晚上晚些时候彩排晚宴上。钱德勒和莫妮卡坐在沙发上。钱德勒因为祝酒而尴尬地捂着脸。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：drive sb crazy / must have been 过去推测 / if it weren't for 虚拟 / 修辞问句

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1485.264" duration="1.896" /> **Monica Geller:** <Note>I was laughing</Note>. (Patting him on the knee.)
> 我在笑。（拍拍他的膝盖。）
<GrammarTip points="动词时态">

- **I was laughing**：过去进行式——"（你讲段子那会儿）我是在笑的"（只是没出声）（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1487.16" duration="1.68" /> **Chandler Bing:** <Note>Out loud</Note>?
> 大声说？
<GrammarTip points="省略,副词">

- **Out loud?**：副词短语独立成问句 = (Were you laughing) out loud?"笑出声了吗"——loud 在此是副词（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)/[Ch.16](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter16.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1488.84" duration="3.432" /> **Monica Geller:** Well <Note>I didn’t want everyone to think I was stupid</Note>.
> 我不想让每个人都认为我很愚蠢。
<GrammarTip points="不定词,名词从句">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">didn’t want</span> <span class="o">everyone</span> <span class="c">to think (that) I was stupid</span></p>

- **want everyone to think...**：want + 宾语 + 不定式宾补；think 后面再嵌一个宾语从句 (that) I was stupid——"不想让大家觉得我傻"（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1492.272" duration="2.04" /> **Chandler Bing:** So how are you doing?
> 那你怎么样？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1494.312" duration="8.928" /> **Monica Geller:** <Note>My mother’s driving me crazy</Note>, but <Note>Ross is getting married</Note>. I’m happy. (A drunken man approaches.) <Note>I’m not going to let anything spoil that</Note>.
> 我妈妈快把我逼疯了，但罗斯要结婚了。我很高兴。（一个醉汉走近）我不会让任何事情破坏它。
<GrammarTip points="使役结构,动词时态">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">My mother</span> <span class="v">’s driving</span> <span class="o">me</span> <span class="c">crazy</span></p>

- **drive me crazy**：drive + 宾语 + 形容词补语"把我逼疯"（S+V+O+C 句型）；进行式表"一直在逼"（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **Ross is getting married**：进行式表将来安排（本集核心句型）（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **let anything spoil that**：let + 宾语 + 原形 spoil"不让任何事破坏这份心情"（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1503.24" duration="3.12" /> **Drunk Man:** I just want to say that Ross is a wonderful young man.
> 我只想说罗斯是个很棒的年轻人。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1506.36" duration="2.76" /> **Monica Geller:** Well, thanks, we like him.
> 谢谢，我们喜欢他。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1509.12" duration="2.976" /> **Drunk Man:** My god!! <Note>You must have been a teenage when you had him</Note>. (Monica stares straight forward after the comment. Chandler tries to console her by patting her on the shoulder.)
> 天哪！！你生他的时候一定是个十几岁的孩子。（莫妮卡在评论后直视着她。钱德勒拍拍她的肩膀试图安慰她。）
<GrammarTip points="语气,原文勘误">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">You</span> <span class="v">must have been</span> <span class="c">a teenager</span></p>

- **must have been a teenager**：**must have + 过去分词**表对过去的肯定推测"当时一定是"——醉汉把 Monica 当成 Ross 的妈妈，精准踩雷（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- ⚠️ 原文 **a teenage** 应为 a **teenager**（名词）
- **when you had him**：when 时间从句过去式——"生他那会儿"（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）

</GrammarTip>

*(Ross and Emily’s parents are seated at a table. Ross is between them and they are discussing the wedding bill.)*
> *（罗斯和艾米丽的父母坐在一张桌子旁。罗斯在他们中间，他们正在讨论婚礼账单。）*

Mr. Geller: <Note>There’s no way in hell</Note>, <Note>I’m paying for it</Note>.
> 地狱里没有办法，我要付钱。
<GrammarTip points="存在句,动词时态">

- **There's no way (that)... in hell**：There's no way + 从句"绝不可能"；in hell 加强语气"门都没有"（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）
- **I'm paying for it**：现在进行式此处表**态度强烈的否定**——"让我付钱？想都别想"（进行式不只表将来，也表眼下态度）（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1512.096" duration="6.744" /> **Ross Geller:** Look, <Note>were down to just one point</Note>. Could we please, maybe just <Note>settle it after the wedding</Note>.
> 看，我们只谈了一点。我们可以在婚礼结束后再谈吗。
<GrammarTip points="原文勘误,惯用语,语气">

- ⚠️ 原文 **were down to** 应为 **we're** down to——be down to = 只剩下（一个分歧点）
- **Could we please just settle it after the wedding?**：Could we please...? 恳求句型；settle 解决（分歧）（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

Mr. Geller: All-right fine, but I just want to say, I’m not paying for your wine cellar. You thieving, <Note>would be speaking German if it weren’t for us</Note>, cheap little man. (Emily’s stepmum looks shocked. Jack and Judy get up and leave.)
> 好吧，但我只想说，我不会为你的酒窖付钱。你这个小偷，要不是我们，你会说德语的，卑鄙小人。（艾米丽的继母看起来很震惊。杰克和朱迪站起来离开了。）
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,虚拟语气">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1512.096" duration="6.744" />

**原句**：（You thieving little man, you）would be speaking German if it weren't for us.

**层次划分**：

- ⚠️ 原文语序听写混乱，还原后为：You thieving, cheap little man, (**you**) would be speaking German if it weren't for us.
- 条件从句：**if it weren't for us**——与事实相反的假设用**过去式** weren't（"要是没有我们"）；if it weren't for sb/sth 是固定虚拟句式（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- 主句：<p class="svoc"><span class="s">you</span> <span class="v">would be speaking</span> <span class="o">German</span></p> **would + 进行式**表"（如今）正在说德语"的假想画面（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **thieving / cheap little man**：分词 thieving + 形容词 cheap 连珠炮作定语骂人——二战梗：美国岳父讽刺英国岳父"没我们你们早输了"

**翻译**：你这个抠门的贼骨头——要不是有我们，你们现在都得说德语！

**语法要点**：if it weren't for... 虚拟条件（Ch.9/13）；would be doing 假想进行（Ch.9）

</GrammarTip>

*(Chandler’s trying to console Monica.)*
> *（钱德勒试图安慰莫妮卡。）*

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1518.84" duration="6.096" /> **Chandler Bing:** <Note>The guy was hammered</Note>, okay? There’s no way, <Note>you look like Ross’s mother</Note>.
> 那家伙被打了，好吗？不可能，你看起来像罗斯的妈妈。
<GrammarTip points="分词,存在句">

- **The guy was hammered**：be hammered = 烂醉如泥（分词形容词化的口语说法）（[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)）
- **There's no way (that) you look like Ross's mother**：no way + 从句省 that"你绝不可能像 Ross 他妈"；look like 长得像（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1524.936" duration="2.232" /> **Monica Geller:** Then <Note>why would he say it</Note>?
> 那他为什么这么说？
<GrammarTip points="语气">

- **why would he say it?**：would 表"（他）怎么会/干嘛要"——对动机的不解（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1527.168" duration="8.856" /> **Chandler Bing:** Because he’s crazy. Okay? <Note>He came up to me earlier</Note> and <Note>thanked me for my very moving performance in Titanic</Note>.
> 因为他疯了。可以他早些时候来找我，感谢我在《泰坦尼克号》中非常感人的表演。
<GrammarTip points="短语动词,分词">

- **came up to me earlier**：come up to sb"走到某人跟前"；earlier 副词"早些时候"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)/[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）
- **thanked me for my very moving performance**：thank sb for + 名词"为……道谢"；**moving** 现在分词作形容词"感人的"——对照 moved"受感动的"（醉汉把他认成莱昂纳多了）（[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1536.024" duration="4.848" /> **Monica Geller:** Oh, my mother’s right. <Note>I’m never going to get married</Note>.
> 哦，我妈妈说得对。我永远不会结婚。
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,副词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">’m never going to get married</span></p>

- **I'm never going to get married**：be going to 将来式 + **never 插在 be 之后**——"我这辈子都嫁不出去了"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1540.872" duration="4.944" /> **Chandler Bing:** Ahh, you know what? That is....<Note>Who wouldn’t want you</Note>?
> 啊，你知道吗？那就是…谁不想要你？
<GrammarTip points="疑问句,修辞">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">Who</span> <span class="v">wouldn’t want</span> <span class="o">you</span></p>

- **Who wouldn't want you?**：**修辞问句**——字面问"谁不想要你"，实际意思是"谁都想要你"；否定疑问表强烈肯定（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1545.816" duration="5.304" /> **Monica Geller:** Ohh, Please?! <Note>I’m a single mom, with a thirty year old son</Note>!!
> 哦，求你了？！我是单身妈妈，有一个三十岁的儿子！！
<GrammarTip points="名词词组,夸张">

- **a thirty year old son**：规范写法 a **thirty-year-old** son——数词-单数名词-old 复合形容词（连字符）；Monica 自嘲：被当成 Ross 的妈，那我不就是"带个三十岁儿子的单亲妈妈"（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)/[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)）

</GrammarTip>

---

🎬 *The airport. Rachel runs up to the ticket counter.*
> 🎬 *机场。瑞秋跑到售票处。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：现在完成 + already / tell + 宾语 + 不定式 / marry 及物用法 / not...until

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1551.12" duration="4.224" /> **Rachel Green:** Hi, I’m back. Listen, I need to...
> 嗨，我回来了。听着，我需要。。。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1555.344" duration="0.816" /> **Ticket Agent:** Hello.
> 你好。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1556.16" duration="4.488" /> **Rachel Green:** Hello. I need to <Note>get on the 11 o'clock flight</Note>.
> 你好。我需要上11点的航班。
<GrammarTip points="短语动词,名词词组">

- **get on the 11 o'clock flight**：get on + 交通工具"登上"；**o'clock** = of the clock 的缩略（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1560.648" duration="3.36" /> **Ticket Agent:** Oh I’m afraid <Note>that plane has already pulled away from the gate</Note>.
> 噢，恐怕那架飞机已经离开登机口了。
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,短语动词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">that plane</span> <span class="v">has pulled away</span> <span class="a">from the gate</span></p>

- **has already pulled away**：**现在完成式 + already**——"已经滑走了"，强调对现在的影响（你赶不上了）；pull away from 驶离（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1564.008" duration="6.408" /> **Rachel Green:** Okay, you know what/ <Note>You’re going to have to call that plane</Note> and <Note>tell them to swing around and come and pick me up</Note>.
> 好吧，你知道你得给那架飞机打个电话，让他们转转过来接我。
<GrammarTip points="不定词,并列结构">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">You</span> <span class="v">’re going to have to call</span> <span class="o">that plane</span></p>

- **You're going to have to call...**：be going to + have to"你就得（给飞机打个电话）"——无理要求的 command 语气（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **tell them to swing around and (to) come and (to) pick me up**：tell + 宾语 + 不定式宾补，三个动作并列共用一个 to；**pick me up** 接我（代词夹中间）（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1570.416" duration="1.296" /> **Ticket Agent:** I can’t do that.
> 我不能那样做。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1571.712" duration="6.744" /> **Rachel Green:** Sure, you know what? Come on, we’ll just tell them that <Note>there was like a problem with like the "engine"</Note>.
> 当然，你知道吗？来吧，我们会告诉他们“引擎”有问题。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,口语标记">
