<p class="svoc"><span class="s">we</span> <span class="v">’ll tell</span> <span class="o">them</span> <span class="o">(that) there was a problem...</span></p>

- **tell them (that) there was a problem with the engine**：双宾语 + that 宾语从句，从句内含 there be 存在句（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）
- **like... like...**：口语填充词 like，配"engine"的引号手势——明示"咱们现编个理由"（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1578.456" duration="3.408" /> **Ticket Agent:** I’m afraid I’m going to have to <Note>ask you to step aside</Note>, Miss.
> 小姐，恐怕我得请你让开。
<GrammarTip points="不定词,短语动词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">’m going to have to ask</span> <span class="o">you</span> <span class="c">to step aside</span></p>

- **ask you to step aside**：ask + 宾语 + 不定式宾补"请您让一让"；step aside 站到旁边——下逐客令的礼貌版（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1581.864" duration="5.376" /> **Rachel Green:** Look, <Note>If I don’t get to London</Note>!! <Note>He is going to marry that other girl</Note>!!!
> 听着，如果我到不了伦敦！！他要娶另一个女孩！！！
<GrammarTip points="副词从句,动词用法">

- **If I don't get to London**：if 条件句现在式代未来；get to = 到达（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **He is going to marry that other girl**：**marry 是及物动词，直接接宾语**——marry sb（不说 marry with sb）；be going to 表"眼看就要"（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1587.24" duration="1.488" /> **Ticket Agent:** I can’t imagine why.
> 我无法想象为什么。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,反讽">

- **I can't imagine why (you'd want to stop him)**：imagine + why 宾语从句的省略式——柜台小姐冷眼旁观的神吐槽（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.16](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter16.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1588.728" duration="6.144" /> **Rachel Green:** All right, you know what? <Note>I am not leaving here, until you call that plane back</Note>!! (She pounds her hand on the counter twice. The ticket agent counters by placing the closed sign on the counter and tapping it twice.)
> 好吧，你知道吗？我不会离开这里，除非你把飞机叫回来！！（她用手在柜台上捶了两下。售票员把“关闭”的标志放在柜台上，然后轻敲两下，进行柜台操作。）
<GrammarTip points="副词从句,动词时态">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">am not leaving</span> <span class="a">until you call that plane back</span></p>

- **not...until**："在你把飞机叫回来之前，我绝不走"；until 从句现在式 call 代未来（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- **I am not leaving**：进行式表"（我）铁了心不走"；call back 叫回（可分短语动词）（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

---

🎬 *Chandlers hotel room. Ross bursts into the room.*
> 🎬 *钱德勒酒店房间。罗斯冲进房间。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：进行式表将来 / do you think + 宾语从句 / 代动词强调

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1594.872" duration="3.744" /> **Ross Geller:** (Screaming) <Note>I’m getting married today</Note>!! Whoo-hoo!!
> （尖叫）我今天要结婚了！！呜呼！！
<GrammarTip points="动词时态">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">’m getting married</span> <span class="a">today</span></p>

- **I'm getting married today**：现在进行式 + today 表**板上钉钉的将来**——婚礼当天清晨的欢呼（本集核心句型再现）（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1598.616" duration="2.352" /> **Chandler Bing:** (With the covers pulled up to his chin.) Morning, Ross.
> （把被子拉到下巴）早上好，罗斯。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1600.968" duration="2.808" /> **Ross Geller:** I’m getting married, to..day!!
> 我要结婚了，到……天！！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1603.776" duration="1.968" /> **Chandler Bing:** <Note>Yeah you are</Note>!!
> 是的，你是！！
<GrammarTip points="省略,强调">

- **Yeah you are (getting married)**：助动词 are 单独撑句表附和——"没错，你确实要结了"（[Ch.16](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter16.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1605.744" duration="4.584" /> **Ross Geller:** Ahh, whoo-hoo!!(He runs back out the door.
> 啊，呜呼！！（他跑回门外。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1610.328" duration="2.4" /> **Monica Geller:** (Comes up for below the covers and looks concerned.) <Note>Do you think he knew I was here</Note>? (Chandler quickly looks at Monica not knowing what to say.)
> （走到封面下，看起来很担心。）你认为他知道我在这里吗？（钱德勒快速地看着莫妮卡，不知道该说什么。）
<GrammarTip points="疑问句,名词从句">

<p class="svoc"><span class="v">Do</span> <span class="s">you</span> <span class="v">think</span> <span class="o">(that) he knew I was here</span></p>

- **Do you think he knew I was here?**：do you think 插入疑问句，其后**陈述语序**；knew 后又嵌一层宾语从句 (that) I was here——被窝里的 Monica 惊魂发问（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)/[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

Commercial Break
> 商业休息

---

🎬 *Chandlers hotel room. Chandler and Monica are lying in the bed together talking. There’s an awkward air between them. They are both clutching the covers in from of them.*
> 🎬 *钱德勒的酒店房间。钱德勒和莫妮卡躺在床上说话，两人之间气氛尴尬，都把被子紧紧捂在身前。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：现在完成 + never...before / had better / could you not 否定位置

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1612.728" duration="3.408" /> **Chandler Bing:** Well <Note>I’ve-I’ve never done that with you before</Note>.
> 嗯，我以前从未和你这样做过。
<GrammarTip points="动词时态">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">’ve never done</span> <span class="o">that</span> <span class="a">with you before</span></p>

- **I've never done that with you before**：**现在完成式 + never...before**——"有史以来头一回"的固定句型（before 把时段拉到此刻）（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1616.136" duration="1.512" /> **Monica Geller:** (In am uneasy voice.) Nope. (She chuckles uneasily.)
> （用我不安的声音）不。（她不安地笑了。）

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1617.648" duration="6.792" /> **Chandler Bing:** So, ahh, how are ya? How ya...How ya... You okay?
> 那么，啊，你好吗？你好…你好…你还好吗？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1624.44" duration="2.208" /> **Monica Geller:** Yep, yep...You?
> 是的，是的……你呢？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1626.648" duration="4.752" /> **Chandler Bing:** Yes...Yes..Uh-huh, You?(Looking over at her. She looks back.) <Note>We did you</Note>.
> 是的……是的……嗯，你？（看着她。她回头。）我们杀了你。
<GrammarTip points="原文勘误,口误">

- ⚠️ 原文 **We did you** 疑为 **We did it** 的慌张口误（或听写失真）——两人对"昨晚那档事"心照不宣，Chandler 一紧张把宾语说错，尴尬加倍（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1631.4" duration="2.544" /> **Monica Geller:** Well...<Note>I’d better get going</Note>.
> 嗯……我得走了。
<GrammarTip points="语气,惯用语">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">’d better get going</span></p>

- **I'd better + 原形**：had better"最好还是……"——had 缩成 'd，后接**原形**；get going = 起身走人（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1633.944" duration="2.832" /> **Chandler Bing:** Oh yea yea, absolutely.
> 哦，是的，绝对是。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1636.776" duration="1.944" /> **Monica Geller:** (Scoots towards the side of the bed.) <Note>Could you not look</Note>?
> （朝床边挪了挪）你能不能看一下？
<GrammarTip points="疑问句,语气">

- **Could you not look?**：请求对方**别做**某事——否定词 not 放在主语 you 之后、动词之前（Could you **not**...），比 Couldn't you...? 更明确地要求"回避"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1638.72" duration="2.064" /> **Chandler Bing:** I don’t want to look.
> 我不想看。

---

🎬 *The Virgin Atlantic flight to London that Rachel is on.*
> 🎬 *瑞秋乘坐的维珍航空公司飞往伦敦的航班*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：plan on + 动名词 / so that 目的从句 / 不定式表目的

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1640.784" duration="1.44" /> **Rachel Green:** Ohhh.(she rhythmically taps her hands on the magazine on her lap.)
> 哦。（她有节奏地用手轻拍膝盖上的杂志。）

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1642.224" duration="1.512" /> **Passenger:** Ahh, ahh, excuse me.
> 啊，啊，对不起。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1643.736" duration="1.344" /> **Rachel Green:** Yeah?
> 是吗？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1645.08" duration="8.28" /> **Passenger:** <Note>If you’re planning on doing that throughout the entire flight</Note>. Please tell me now. <Note>So that I could that a sedative</Note>...or perhaps <Note>slip you one</Note>.
> 如果你打算在整个飞行过程中都这样做。请现在告诉我。这样我就可以给你一片镇静剂……或者给你一片。
<GrammarTip points="动名词,副词从句,双宾语">

- **If you're planning on doing that**：**plan on + 动名词**"打算做某事"（on 是介词）；throughout the entire flight 全程（[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **So that I could take a sedative**：**so that** 引导目的从句"好让我能……"；⚠️ 原文 **could that** 应为 could **take**（听写漏词）（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- **slip you one**：slip + 双宾语"（偷偷）塞给你一片"；one 替代 a sedative（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1653.36" duration="14.472" /> **Rachel Green:** Oh. I’m sorry. I’m very sorry. Sorry. (She hums and sighs happily.) It’s just, <Note>I’m ahh, I’m kinda excited</Note>. I’m, ahh, <Note>going to London to ahh, tell this guy that I love him</Note> and... (He puts his headphones on to ignore her.)
> 哦。对不起。我很抱歉。对不起。（她高兴地哼哼着，叹息着。）只是，我啊，我有点兴奋。我啊，要去伦敦啊，告诉这个家伙我爱他，还有…（他戴上耳机不理她。）
<GrammarTip points="不定词,名词从句">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">’m going</span> <span class="a">to London</span> <span class="a">to tell this guy (that) I love him</span></p>

- **going to London to tell...**：不定式 to tell 作**目的状语**——"去伦敦就是为了告诉他"（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）
- **tell this guy (that) I love him**：tell + 人 + that 宾语从句（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **kinda excited**：kinda = kind of"有点儿"（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）

</GrammarTip>

---

🎬 *Joey enters his hotel room. The phone is ringing.*
> 🎬 *乔伊进入酒店房间，电话铃响了。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：现在完成式 / see 的进行式双关 / That's why 表语从句 / What's with...?

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1667.832" duration="1.464" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Hello?
> 喂？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1669.296" duration="2.04" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** (Angrily.) Hey, <Note>were the hell have you been</Note>?!
> （愤怒地）嘿，你到底去了哪里？！
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,疑问句,原文勘误">

<p class="svoc"><span class="a">where the hell</span> <span class="v">have</span> <span class="s">you</span> <span class="v">been</span></p>

- **where the hell have you been?!**：现在完成式"你（一直）死哪儿去了"；插入语 **the hell** 加强质问（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）
- ⚠️ 原文 **were** the hell 应为 **where** the hell（同音误写）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1671.336" duration="7.848" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Hey. <Note>I spent the night out</Note>. I met this cute bridesmaid. She is so...
> 嘿。我在外面过夜。我遇到了一个可爱的伴娘。她太。。。
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,惯用语">

- **I spent the night out**：spend the night (out)"在外面过了夜"——过去式陈述昨夜去向（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1679.184" duration="2.16" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** I don’t want to hear about her!!
> 我不想听她的事！！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1681.344" duration="3.552" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Ahh Pheebs, you know you’re still my number one girl.
> 啊，菲比，你知道你还是我的头号女孩。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1684.896" duration="5.328" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** No! No, we have an emergency. Okay? <Note>Rachel’s coming to London</Note>.
> 不！不，我们有急事。好吗？瑞秋要来伦敦。
<GrammarTip points="动词时态">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">Rachel</span> <span class="v">’s coming</span> <span class="a">to London</span></p>

- **Rachel's coming to London**：进行式表将来——人已上飞机，"正在杀过来"（本集核心句型）（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1690.224" duration="1.728" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Ohh great!!!
> 哦，太棒了！！！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1691.952" duration="4.512" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** No it’s not great. No, <Note>she’s coming to tell Ross that she loves him</Note>.
> 不，不太好。不，她是来告诉罗斯她爱他。
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,不定词,名词从句">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">she</span> <span class="v">’s coming</span> <span class="a">to tell Ross (that) she loves him</span></p>

- **she's coming to tell Ross (that) she loves him**：进行式表将来 + 不定式 to tell 表目的 + that 宾语从句（省 that）——三层结构一句话搞定（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1696.464" duration="2.448" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** (Confused.) But, he loves Emily?
> （困惑）但是，他爱艾米丽？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1698.912" duration="4.848" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** I KNOW THAT!!! <Note>You have to stop her</Note>!! <Note>She’s going to ruin the wedding</Note>!!
> 我知道！！！你必须阻止她！！她会毁掉婚礼的！！
<GrammarTip points="语气,动词时态">

- **You have to stop her**：have to + 原形"必须拦住她"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **She's going to ruin the wedding**：be going to 表"眼看就要发生"的预言（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1703.76" duration="1.656" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Okay.
> 好的。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1705.416" duration="1.656" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** All right, so, okay...
> 好吧，那么，好吧。。。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1707.072" duration="7.08" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Hold on. Hold on. (Picking up a note pad and writing and reading the message aloud.) <Note>Rachel coming. Do...Something</Note>.
> 等一下。等一下。（拿起便笺簿，大声读写信息。）瑞秋来了。做点什么。
<GrammarTip points="省略,幽默">

- **Rachel coming. Do...Something.**：电报式便条体——(Rachel's) coming 省掉 be 动词，(You) do something 省主语；乔伊的"备忘录"把紧迫指令记得毫无信息量（[Ch.16](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter16.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1714.152" duration="6.432" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Okay, so <Note>I’m done my part</Note>, okay. It’s your responsibility now, okay. <Note>The burden is off me</Note>, right?
> 好了，我已经尽了我的责任，好了。现在是你的责任了，好了。责任在我身上，对吗？
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,介词">

- **I'm done my part**：口语 = I've done my part（现在完成式的口语变体，美式常见）"我这摊事儿办完了"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **The burden is off me**：be off sb"从某人身上卸下"——介词 off 表脱离（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1720.584" duration="1.536" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Right!
> 对！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1722.12" duration="1.872" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** So tell me about this girl?
> 那跟我说说这个女孩吧？

*(The guys hotel room. Joey’s there. Chandler comes out of the bathroom in a robe.)*
> *（男人旅馆房间。乔伊在那里。钱德勒穿着长袍从浴室出来。）*

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1723.992" duration="1.488" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Hey.
> 嘿。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1725.48" duration="1.44" /> **Chandler Bing:** Hey.
> 嘿。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1726.92" duration="2.232" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** <Note>Have you seen Monica</Note>?
> 你看见莫妮卡了吗？
<GrammarTip points="动词时态">

- **Have you seen Monica?**：现在完成式——"（从刚才到现在）见过她没有"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1729.152" duration="2.328" /> **Chandler Bing:** (Very defensive.) <Note>I’m not seeing Monica</Note>.
> （非常防御性。）我没有见到莫妮卡。
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,双关">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">’m not seeing</span> <span class="o">Monica</span></p>

- **I'm not seeing Monica**：**双关**——see 的进行式既可答"没见着"，更是"没在和某人约会"（I'm seeing someone = 恋爱中）；Chandler 此地无银，用错时态等于招供（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1731.48" duration="1.488" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** (With a confused look on his face.) What?
> （脸上带着困惑的表情）什么？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1732.968" duration="1.464" /> **Chandler Bing:** What?
> 什么？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1734.432" duration="7.584" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Look <Note>we’ve got to find her</Note>. Phoebe just called!! <Note>Rachel’s coming to tell Ross she loves him</Note>!!
> 听着，我们得找到她。菲比刚打电话来！！瑞秋要来告诉罗斯她爱他！！
<GrammarTip points="语气,名词从句">

- **we've got to find her**：have got to = have to"必须"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **tell Ross (that) she loves him**：宾语从句省 that（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1742.016" duration="1.92" /> **Chandler Bing:** Oh my god!
> 哦，我的天啊！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1743.936" duration="6.432" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** I know! <Note>That’s why we got to find Monica</Note>!! <Note>You know where she is</Note>?
> 我知道！这就是为什么我们要找到莫妮卡！！你知道她在哪里吗？
<GrammarTip points="名词从句">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">That</span> <span class="v">’s</span> <span class="c">why we got to find Monica</span></p>

- **That's why we got to find Monica**：why 引导**表语从句**"所以我们才得找到 Monica"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **You know where she is?**：know + where 宾语从句，**陈述语序** she is——口语升调疑问（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1750.368" duration="8.256" /> **Chandler Bing:** No!! Okay!! <Note>What’s with the third degree</Note>?! <Note>Why don’t you just shine a light in my eyes</Note>?! (Joey looks totally confused.)
> 不！！好吧！！第三度是怎么回事？！你为什么不照我的眼睛？！（乔伊看起来很困惑。）
<GrammarTip points="惯用语,疑问句">

- **What's with the third degree?**：What's with...? 习语"……是搞什么名堂"；**the third degree** = 严刑逼供（典故来自警讯）（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **Why don't you just shine a light in my eyes?!**：Why don't you...? 反讽式建议——"你干脆拿台灯照我眼睛审我得了"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）

</GrammarTip>

---

🎬 *The plane. Rachel's telling her story to the passenger on her left. The one on her left is still wearing his headphones.*
> 🎬 *飞机。瑞秋正在向她左边的乘客讲述她的故事。她左边的那个人仍然戴着耳机。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：过去完成进行式 / be about to / 否定词开头与倒装辨析 / it 形式主语

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1758.624" duration="11.568" /> **Rachel Green:** ...And so then I realized. <Note>All this stuff I had been doing</Note>. <Note>proposing to Joshua, lying to Ross about why I couldn’t come to the wedding</Note>. Was all just a way of...
> ……然后我意识到。我一直在做的这些事。向约书亚求婚，向罗斯撒谎说我为什么不能来参加婚礼。这只是一种。。。
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,动词时态,动名词">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1758.624" duration="11.568" />

**原句**：All this stuff I had been doing — proposing to Joshua, lying to Ross about why I couldn't come to the wedding — was all just a way of...

**层次划分**：

- 主干：<p class="svoc"><span class="s">All this stuff</span> <span class="v">was</span> <span class="c">all just a way of...</span></p>
- **(that) I had been doing**：定语从句修饰 stuff，关系代词作宾语省略；**过去完成进行式** had been doing——"（在顿悟之前）一直在做的"（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **proposing to Joshua, lying to Ross...**：两个动名词短语并列，作 stuff 的同位语"具体来说"；propose to sb 向某人求婚（[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md)/[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)）
- **about why I couldn't come to the wedding**：介词 about + **why 名词从句**（陈述语序）（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **a way of (doing sth)**：of + 动名词"一种（……的）方式"（[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md)）

**翻译**：然后我明白了：我之前干的那些事——向 Joshua 求婚、骗 Ross 说我来不了婚礼——统统只是一种……（自我逃避的方式）。

**语法要点**：过去完成进行式（Ch.3）；动名词同位语（Ch.5）；介词 + 名词从句（Ch.12）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1770.192" duration="6.984" /> **Passenger:** (Frustrated he takes his headphones off.) Oh, oh oh!! I’m sorry, <Note>can I interrupt</Note>? You know I just want to say..That <Note>you are a horrible, horrible person</Note>.
> （沮丧的他摘下耳机。）哦，哦！！对不起，我能打断一下吗？你知道我只是想说……你是一个可怕的人。
<GrammarTip points="惯用语,强调">

- **can I interrupt?**：插话的礼貌缓冲句（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **a horrible, horrible person**：形容词**重复**表强调"可怕至极"——邻座乘客憋了半程终于爆发（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1777.176" duration="2.136" /> **Rachel Green:** Ehh, pardon me?
> 呃，对不起？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1779.312" duration="12.0" /> **Passenger:** <Note>You say you love this man</Note>, yet <Note>you’re about to ruin the happiest day of his life</Note>. I’m afraid I have to agree with you friend Pheebs.. This is a..this is a...terrible, terrible plan.
> 你说你爱这个人，但你却要毁掉他一生中最快乐的一天。恐怕我不得不同意你朋友菲比的看法。这是一个……这是一个……可怕的计划。
<GrammarTip points="对等连接词,不定词,形容词比较">

- **You say (that) you love this man, yet...**：yet 对等连接词表转折"然而"（比 but 文雅）；say 后宾语从句省 that（[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)/[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **you're about to ruin...**：be about to + 原形"正要做/眼看就要"（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）
- **the happiest day of his life**：最高级 + of 所有格范围"他一生中最幸福的日子"（[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)）
- ⚠️ 原文 **you friend Pheebs** 应为 **your** friend Pheebs；agree with sb 同意某人（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1791.312" duration="2.52" /> **Rachel Green:** But <Note>he has to know how I feel</Note>!
> 但他必须知道我的感受！
<GrammarTip points="语气,名词从句">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">he</span> <span class="v">has to know</span> <span class="o">how I feel</span></p>

- **has to know how I feel**：have to + 原形"必须"；how 引导宾语从句（陈述语序 I feel）（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1793.832" duration="4.968" /> **Passenger:** But why? He loves this...<Note>this Emily person</Note>. <Note>No good can come of this</Note>.
> 但为什么？他喜欢这个……这个爱米丽的人。这不会有什么好处的。
<GrammarTip points="名词词组,倒装辨析">

- **this Emily person**：this + 人名 + person——轻蔑的指称方式"艾米丽这号人"（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）
- **No good can come of this**：<p class="svoc"><span class="s">No good</span> <span class="v">can come of</span> <span class="o">this</span></p> 注意：否定词 no 在**主语名词前**，全句按正常语序，**不算倒装**（对比 Ch.22 的状语否定提前才倒装）——"这不会有好结果的"（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)/[Ch.11](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter11.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1798.8" duration="2.424" /> **Rachel Green:** (Sighing) Well I-I think <Note>your wrong</Note>.
> （叹气）我觉得你错了。
<GrammarTip points="原文勘误">

- ⚠️ 原文 **your wrong** 应为 **you're** wrong（= you are）——your/you're 同音误写，本集剧本已多次出现，注意区分（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1801.224" duration="4.536" /> **Passenger:** Oh-no.(He bites his fist at her.)And by the way, <Note>it seems to be perfectly clear that you were on a break</Note>. (Rachel gasps and doesn’t know what to say. He puts his headphones back on.)
> 哦，不。（他用拳头咬了她。）顺便说一句，很明显你是在休息。（瑞秋喘息着，不知道说什么。他把耳机重新戴上。）
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,形式主语">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">it</span> <span class="v">seems</span> <span class="c">to be perfectly clear</span> <span class="s">that you were on a break</span></p>

- **it seems... that you were on a break**：**it 形式主语**，that 主语从句后置——"有一点再清楚不过了：你们当时在分手期"；**on a break** 是全剧名梗（Ross 的名言 We were on a break!），路人补刀（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

---

🎬 *The church where Ross and Emily are to be married. Judy, Andrea, and Monica enter together.*
> 🎬 *罗斯和艾米丽结婚的教堂。朱迪、安德里亚和莫妮卡一起进入。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：附加疑问句 / get + 宾语 + 现在分词 / be meant to do / 定语从句省略

Mrs. Geller: (Looking around at the chapel.) Oh my God! <Note>It’s like a fairyland</Note>.
> （环顾教堂）哦，我的天啊！它就像一个仙境。
<GrammarTip points="介词">

- **It's like a fairyland**：介词 like + 名词"像仙境一样"作补语（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

Mrs. Waltham: I know, <Note>it’s horrible isn’t it</Note>?
> 我知道，这很可怕，不是吗？
<GrammarTip points="附加疑问句">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">it</span> <span class="v">’s</span> <span class="c">horrible</span>, <span class="v">isn’t it</span></p>

- **it's horrible, isn't it?**：**附加疑问句**——前肯后否；同一主语同一助动词；Waltham 夫人偏要唱反调（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1805.76" duration="5.088" /> **Monica Geller:** Well, I love it. <Note>I only hope my wedding looks this good</Note>.
> 嗯，我喜欢。我只希望我的婚礼看起来这么好。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,系动词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">hope</span> <span class="o">(that) my wedding looks this good</span></p>

- **hope (that) my wedding looks this good**：hope 接宾语从句省 that；**looks** 系动词（台：连缀动词）+ 形容词 good"看起来这么棒"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

</GrammarTip>

Mrs. Geller: I just hope...
> 我只是希望。。。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1810.848" duration="2.616" /> **Monica Geller:** (Angrily.) <Note>You can let some of them go by</Note>!(Judy and Andrea go to the front of the chapel. Joey approaches Monica.)
> （愤怒地）你可以让他们中的一些人过去！（朱迪和安德里亚走到教堂前面。乔伊走近莫妮卡。）
<GrammarTip points="使役动词,短语动词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">You</span> <span class="v">can let</span> <span class="o">some of them</span> <span class="c">go by</span></p>

- **let some of them go by**：let + 宾语 + 原形；go by"（让这些话）过去算了"——Monica 听腻了我妈的弦外之音（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1813.464" duration="13.032" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** (Whispering.) Pisst, Monica. Alright, we really need to <Note>start looking out for Rachel</Note>. <Note>I’ll cover the front door</Note>. You watch that big hole at the back of the building and <Note>I got Chandler covering Ross</Note>.
> （小声说）小便，莫妮卡。好吧，我们真的需要开始寻找瑞秋。我来掩护前门。你看着大楼后面的那个大洞，我让钱德勒掩护罗斯。
<GrammarTip points="动名词,分词,使役结构">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">got</span> <span class="o">Chandler</span> <span class="c">covering Ross</span></p>

- **I got Chandler covering Ross**：**get/have + 宾语 + 现在分词**——"派/让某人持续做某事"（部署人手）；分词 covering 作宾语补足语（台：补语）（[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)/[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）
- **start looking out for Rachel**：start + 动名词；look out for = 留意、提防（[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **I'll cover the front door**：will + 原形表即时分工；cover 把守（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1826.496" duration="6.816" /> **Monica Geller:** (Awkwardly.) <Note>Why would I care where Chandler is</Note>? You know uhh...You know sometimes I don’t even like Chandler.
> （尴尬地）为什么我会在乎钱德勒在哪里？你知道，嗯……你知道有时候我甚至不喜欢钱德勒。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,语气">

<p class="svoc"><span class="a">Why</span> <span class="v">would</span> <span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">care</span> <span class="o">where Chandler is</span></p>

- **care where Chandler is**：care + where 宾语从句（**陈述语序**）；would 表"（我）干嘛要"——此地无银三百两（刚和 Chandler 过夜）（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1833.312" duration="1.656" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Okay. (They both walk off to watch for Rachel.)
> 好。（他们两个都走开去看瑞秋。）

*(Ross and chandler are standing next to the alter. Ross is practicing for the wedding.)*
> *（罗斯和钱德勒站在圣坛旁边。罗斯正在为婚礼练习。）*

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1834.968" duration="4.488" /> **Ross Geller:** (Using a slightly different inflection for each.) <Note>I do</Note>. I do. I do.
> （对每个词使用稍微不同的屈折变化。）我喜欢。我喜欢。我喜欢。
<GrammarTip points="惯用语,省略">

- **I do**：婚礼誓词"我愿意"——助动词 do 单独承担全部誓言（= I do take this woman...）；Ross 在试三种语调，为后文的口误埋梗（[Ch.16](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter16.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1839.456" duration="4.296" /> **Chandler Bing:** Oh yea, <Note>your right</Note>. It’s the second one.
> 哦，是的，你说得对。这是第二个。
<GrammarTip points="原文勘误">

- ⚠️ 原文 **your right** 应为 **you're** right（= you are）——本集第 N 次 your/you're 混淆（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1843.752" duration="1.464" /> **Ross Geller:** (Very Nervous)Really?
> （非常紧张）真的吗？

[Scene: Joey’s in the front entrance watching for Rachel. The bridesmaid he met at the rehearsal dinner come in.)

🎬 *Joey's in the front entrance watching for Rachel. The bridesmaid he met at the rehearsal dinner comes in.*
> 🎬 *乔伊在前门入口守着瑞秋。他在排练晚宴上认识的那位伴娘走了进来。*

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1845.216" duration="1.224" /> **Felicity:** (In a sexy voice.)Hello Joey.
> （用性感的声音）你好，乔伊。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1846.44" duration="2.208" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Hey, Felicity.
> 嘿，费利西蒂。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1848.648" duration="2.256" /> **Felicity:** Umm, I thought about you all day.
> 嗯，我整天都在想你。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1850.904" duration="1.488" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Yeah.
> 是的。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1852.392" duration="2.256" /> **Felicity:** Um-hum. <Note>Talk New York to me again</Note>.
> 嗯哼。再跟我谈谈纽约。
<GrammarTip points="祈使句,动词用法">

<p class="svoc"><span class="v">Talk</span> <span class="o">New York</span> <span class="a">to me</span></p>

- **Talk New York to me**：talk + （语言/方言） + to sb"用……腔调跟我说话"——talk 的及物活用（通常接 language）；Felicity 迷上了他的纽约腔（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1854.648" duration="3.84" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** (In a New York accent.) <Note>Fuggetaboutit</Note>. (She giggles.) <Note>How you doin</Note>?
> （带着纽约口音）福格塔布特。（她咯咯笑）你好吗？
<GrammarTip points="语音,省略">

- **Fuggetaboutit**：= Forget about it 的纽约口音连读（"得了吧/别想了"）——语音缩合的极端样本
- **How you doin'?**：= How are you doing?——Joey 的招牌搭讪语，助动词 are 全省（[Ch.16](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter16.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1858.488" duration="0.648" /> **Felicity:** Mmm. (She pushes him up against the wall and they begin to kiss.)
> 嗯。（她把他推到墙上，他们开始接吻。）

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1859.136" duration="1.824" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Oh, yeah.
> 哦，是的。

*(Back in the chapel. The parents are still fighting over the bill. Ross is refereeing.)*
> *（回到礼拜堂。父母还在为账单争吵。罗斯是裁判。）*

Mrs. Geller: <Note>There’s nothing to discuss</Note>. We’re not paying for your wine cellar.
> 没什么好讨论的。我们不会为你的酒窖付钱。
<GrammarTip points="不定词,存在句">

<p class="svoc"><span class="a">There</span> <span class="v">’s</span> <span class="s">nothing to discuss</span></p>

- **nothing to discuss**：不定式作 nothing 的后置定语——"没什么好谈的"（不定式主动形式表被动含义：nothing that should be discussed）（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.16](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter16.md)）

</GrammarTip>

Mr. Waltham: (Pleading.) You-you have to <Note>meet me in the middle</Note> here.
> Waltham先生：（恳求）你必须在这里与我会面。
<GrammarTip points="惯用语">

- **meet me in the middle**：习语"各退一步/折中"——谈判用语（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

Mr. Geller: (Forcefully.) Hey, <Note>you keep pushing me on this</Note>, <Note>my foots going to meet the middle of your ass</Note>.
> （用力地）嘿，你一直在推我，我的脚会碰到你的屁股中间。
<GrammarTip points="动名词,幽默">

- **you keep pushing me on this**：keep + 动名词"一个劲地逼我"；口语条件句省 if（你要是再逼……）（[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md)/[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- **my foot's going to meet the middle of your ass**：⚠️ 原文 **foots** 应为 foot's；故意戏仿对方上句的 meet me in the middle——"我的脚就要会一会你屁股的正中间"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1860.96" duration="1.488" /> **Ross Geller:** Dad!! (Emily comes running in.)
> 爸爸！！（艾米丽跑了进来。）

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1862.448" duration="1.2" /> **Emily:** What-what’s going on?!
> 怎么回事？！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1863.648" duration="4.656" /> **Ross Geller:** Nothing, nothing. Everything's under control.
> 没什么，没什么。一切都在控制之中。

Mr. Waltham: You want a piece of me, sir? <Note>Is that what your saying</Note>? (Pointing at Jack and poking him) You want a piece of me?
> 先生，你想要我的一部分吗？你是这么说的吗？（指着杰克，戳了戳他）你想从我身上分一块吗？
<GrammarTip points="惯用语,名词从句,原文勘误">

- **You want a piece of me?**：挑衅习语"想跟我比划比划？"
- **Is that what you're saying?**：⚠️ 原文 **your** 应为 **you're**；what 表语从句"你是这个意思吗"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1868.304" duration="20.112" /> **Ross Geller:** (Stepping in between them.)Okay! Okay! That’s it!! Parents!! Parents!! Back away!! All right, this is our wedding day!   <Note>From now on everyone gets along</Note>, and <Note>if I hear one more word</Note>. <Note>NO GRANDCHILDREN</Note>! (Pointing at his mother.) That’s right!!
> （站在他们中间）好的！可以就这样！！父母父母退后！！好了，今天是我们的婚礼！从现在起，每个人都相处融洽，如果我再听到一个字。没有孙子！（指着他的母亲）没错！！
<GrammarTip points="主谓一致,副词从句,省略">

- **From now on everyone gets along**：from now on"从今往后"；everyone 单数 → gets（[Ch.11](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter11.md)）
- **if I hear one more word**：if 条件句现在式；主句被名词惊叹句 **NO GRANDCHILDREN!** 替代——"再让我听见一句，就别想要孙辈！"（威胁的省略式）（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.16](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter16.md)）
- **Back away!**：祈使句"都给我退后"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

Mr. Geller: Okay, okay.
> 好的，好的。

Mr. Waltham: Sorry old boy, sorry. Sorry. Sorry. (Them all walk away. As he leaves he mutters to Jack.) <Note>I could kill you with my thumb</Note>, you know.
> 对不起，老家伙，对不起。很抱歉很抱歉（他们都走开了。他离开时对杰克喃喃自语。）你知道，我可以用我的拇指杀了你。
<GrammarTip points="语气,介词">

- **I could kill you with my thumb**：could 表（吹嘘的）能力"一根拇指就能收拾你"；with + 工具（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1888.416" duration="1.32" /> **Emily:** What was all that about?
> 那是怎么回事？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1889.736" duration="7.296" /> **Ross Geller:** (Sighing.) It was...This disagreement over...(She sighs. Ross notices her in her wedding dress.) My god. <Note>You...you look beautiful</Note>.
> （叹气）这是……关于……的分歧。（她叹气。罗斯注意到她穿着婚纱。）我的上帝。你…你看起来很漂亮。
<GrammarTip points="系动词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">you</span> <span class="v">look</span> <span class="c">beautiful</span></p>

- **you look beautiful**：look 系动词（台：连缀动词）+ 形容词——"你真美"（注意不用 beautifully，因为形容的是"你"不是"看"）（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1897.032" duration="4.92" /> **Emily:** (Giggles.) Ohh...(She realizes that she’s in her gown.) Oh! <Note>You were not meant to see me before the wedding</Note>. <Note>It’s bad luck</Note>.
> （咯咯笑）哦…（她意识到她穿着她的礼服。）哦！你不该在婚礼前见我。真倒霉。
<GrammarTip points="分词,不定词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">You</span> <span class="v">were not meant</span> <span class="c">to see me before the wedding</span></p>

- **be meant to do**："按理应该/命中注定（不）该"——被动式 be + 过去分词 meant + 不定式；婚礼前新郎见新娘婚纱犯忌讳（英美婚俗）（[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **It's bad luck**：luck 不可数名词，不加 a（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1901.952" duration="4.392" /> **Ross Geller:** You know what, I think <Note>we’ve had all the bad luck we’re going to have</Note>. (He hugs her.)
> 你知道吗，我想我们已经经历了所有的厄运。（他拥抱着她。）
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,关系从句">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">we</span> <span class="v">’ve had</span> <span class="o">all the bad luck (that) we’re going to have</span></p>

- **I think (that) we've had...**：宾语从句省 that；从句内用现在完成式"该倒的霉都倒完了"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **all the bad luck (that) we're going to have**：定语从句修饰 luck，关系词作 have 的宾语省略，从句用 be going to 表"（往后）命里该有的"（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)）

</GrammarTip>

*(The front entrance. Joey and the bridesmaid are up against the wall kissing. Rachel comes in the door and walks by Joey unnoticed. She walks into the chapel and sees Ross and Emily kissing. She looks as though she wants to cry. Emily walks away and Ross turns and sees Rachel standing there.)*
> *（前门。乔伊和伴娘靠墙接吻。瑞秋走进门，无意中从乔伊身边走过。她走进教堂，看到罗斯和艾米莉接吻。她看起来好像想哭。艾米莉走开了，罗斯转过身，看到瑞秋站在那里。）*

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1906.344" duration="9.888" /> **Ross Geller:** My God. Rachel! (He walks towards her, grasps her hands and kisses her on the cheek.) <Note>Your here</Note>. <Note>I can’t believe it</Note>. (She giggles.) <Note>What happen</Note>? Why are you here?
> 我的天啊。瑞秋！（他走向她，握住她的手，亲吻她的脸颊。）你的手在这里。真不敢相信。（她咯咯地笑）发生了什么事？你为什么在这里？
<GrammarTip points="原文勘误,名词从句">

- ⚠️ 原文 **Your here** 应为 **You're** here；**What happen** 应为 What **happened**（过去式）
- **I can't believe it**：believe + 代词 it（从句全省）——惊喜到语塞（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1916.232" duration="6.24" /> **Rachel Green:** Well <Note>I just came</Note>...(She touches him near his heart. She’s almost in tears.) <Note>I just needed to tell you</Note>...(Looking into his eyes. She takes a deep breath.) Congratulations. (He hugs her. She can barely hold back the tears.)
> 我刚来…（她抚摸着他的心。她几乎要哭了。）我只想告诉你…（看着他的眼睛。她深吸了一口气。）祝贺你。（他拥抱着她，她几乎忍不住流泪。）
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,不定词">

- **I just came... / I just needed to tell you...**：just + 过去式"刚……"；needed + 不定式 to tell——"我只是来告诉你……"，话到嘴边咽下，换成 **Congratulations**（全剧最揪心的一幕）（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）

</GrammarTip>

---

🎬 *Camera fades to one of the band members playing guitar at the wedding. The chapel is full of guest. A groomsmen escorts a bridesmaid down the isle. Joey is waiting with Mrs. Waltham to escort he down the isle. A cellular phone rings.*
> 🎬 *在婚礼上，一名乐队成员弹吉他，镜头淡出。小教堂里挤满了客人。一名伴郎护送一名伴娘下岛。乔伊正和沃尔瑟姆夫人一起等着护送他下岛。手机响了。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：get + 过去分词被动 / 古英语 thee / May 祈愿倒装 / shall I 征求意见

Mrs. Waltham: (Answering the phone.) Hello, Waltham Interiors.
> （接电话）你好，沃尔瑟姆室内设计公司。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1922.472" duration="3.816" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Mrs. Waltham. Hi. It’s Phoebe again.
> 沃尔瑟姆太太。你好又是菲比。

Mrs. Waltham: (Throws her head back in disgust.) Why?!
> （厌恶地把头往后仰）为什么？！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1926.288" duration="6.384" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Yea. <Note>Can I please, please, please talk to one of the best men</Note>? <Note>This is going to be the last time I promise</Note>.
> 是的。我能不能请最好的男人谈谈？这将是我最后一次保证。
<GrammarTip points="语气,关系从句">

- **Can I please, please, please talk to...?**：please 三连递进——恳求的修辞性重复；one of the best men"伴郎之一"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）
- **the last time (that) I promise**：the last time 后关系词省略；I promise 插入语"我保证"（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)）

</GrammarTip>

Mrs. Waltham: (Slapping the phone into Joey’s chest.) Joey there’s a girl on the phone for you.
> （把电话打到乔伊的胸前）乔伊，有个女孩在给你打电话。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1932.672" duration="3.192" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** (Smiling.) Ohh great!! (Putting the phone to his ear.) Hello. (He begins to escort her down the aisle.)
> （微笑）哦，太好了！！（把电话放在他耳边）你好。（他开始陪她走下走道。）

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1935.864" duration="1.608" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Did you stop Rachel?
> 你阻止瑞秋了吗？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1937.472" duration="5.592" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** No, but it’s okay. <Note>She just came in and gave him a hug</Note>, that it.
> 没有，但没关系。她只是进来给了他一个拥抱，就这样。
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,双宾语,原文勘误">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">She</span> <span class="v">came in</span> and <span class="v">gave</span> <span class="o">him</span> <span class="o">a hug</span></p>

- **just came in and gave him a hug**：just + 过去式"刚进来"；give him a hug 双宾语"抱了他一下"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）
- ⚠️ 原文 **that it** 应为 **that's** it（"就这样/没事了"）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1943.064" duration="1.68" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** So <Note>nothing got ruined</Note>?
> 所以什么都没有被破坏？
<GrammarTip points="分词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">nothing</span> <span class="v">got</span> <span class="c">ruined</span></p>

- **nothing got ruined**：**get + 过去分词**表被动"没出岔子"——口语里 get 被动比 be 被动更生动；nothing 作主语用单数（[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)/[Ch.11](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter11.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1944.744" duration="1.584" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** No.
> 没有。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1946.328" duration="3.384" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Oh that’s so great! Ohh, so what’s going on now?
> 哦，太棒了！哦，那现在怎么了？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1949.712" duration="19.776" /> **Joey Tribbiani:** Ah, <Note>I’m-I’m walking down the aisle</Note>...Still walking. (Mrs. Waltham takes her place.) <Note>I’m about to pass the bridesmaid I hooked up with last night</Note>. (Looking at the bridesmaid.) Hey! (Talking to Phoebe.) I told her "Hey." And now I’m at the front with Ross. It’s Phoebe. (He shows Ross the phone.) He looks pretty mad. Uh...I’d better go.
> 啊，我，我正在走道上……还在走。（沃尔瑟姆夫人接替了她的位置）我正要从昨晚和我搭讪的伴娘身边经过。（看着伴娘）嘿！（和菲比说话）我告诉她“嘿”，现在我和罗斯站在前面。是菲比。（他把电话给罗斯看）他看起来很生气。嗯……我最好走了。
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,关系从句,惯用语">

- **I'm walking down the aisle**：进行式现场直播"我正走在红毯通道上"；aisle（教堂）走道（注意别与 isle"岛"混淆——本集场景描述就写串了）（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **I'm about to pass the bridesmaid (that) I hooked up with last night**：be about to"正要"；定语从句省关系词（作 hooked up **with** 的宾语，介词留句尾）；hook up with = 搭讪/勾搭（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **He looks pretty mad**：look 系动词 + mad；**I'd better go**：had better + 原形"我该挂了"（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1969.488" duration="4.704" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** No!! wait, wait, wait!! Oh please, <Note>hold it up so I can listen</Note>. (Joey looks at Ross and holds the phone above Ross’s shoulder.)
> 不！！等等，等等，等等！！哦，请拿着它，这样我就可以听了。（乔伊看着罗斯，把电话举过罗斯的肩膀。）
<GrammarTip points="副词从句,短语动词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="v">hold</span> <span class="o">it</span> <span class="a">up</span> <span class="a">so I can listen</span></p>

- **hold it up so (that) I can listen**：口语 **so** 代 so that 引导目的从句"好让我能听见"；hold up 举高（代词 it 夹中间）（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

*(Chandler escorts Monica down the aisle.)*
> *（钱德勒陪莫妮卡走下过道。）*

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1974.192" duration="2.688" /> **Chandler Bing:** <Note>What we did last night</Note> was....
> 我们昨晚做的是。。。。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">What we did last night</span> <span class="v">was</span>...</p>

- **What we did last night was...**：**what 主语从句**——"我们昨晚做的事（真是）……"；what = the thing that，从句内陈述语序（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1976.88" duration="1.68" /> **Monica Geller:** Stupid.
> 愚蠢。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1978.56" duration="2.568" /> **Chandler Bing:** Totally crazy stupid. (He nods his head at the people seated.)
> 完全疯了，笨蛋。（他向坐着的人点头。）

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1981.128" duration="2.016" /> **Monica Geller:** <Note>What were we thinking</Note>?
> 我们在想什么？
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,惯用语">

- **What were we thinking?**：过去进行式的固定反问"我们当时在想什么？！"——= 我们真是昏了头（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1983.144" duration="2.976" /> **Chandler Bing:** <Note>I’m coming over tonight though</Note>, right?
> 不过我今晚要过来，对吗？
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,副词">

- **I'm coming over tonight, though, right?**：进行式表将来（今晚的约定）；**though 挪到句尾**作转折语气词"……不过，对吧？"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1986.12" duration="3.192" /> **Monica Geller:** Oh yeah. Definitely.
> 哦，是的。肯定

*(They quickly take their places and Here Comes the Bride Begins to play. Everyone seated looks back. Emily is being escorted up the aisle by her father. She kisses him on the cheek and takes her place by Ross’s side.)*
> *（他们很快就座，新娘开始玩耍。坐着的每个人都回头看。艾米丽在父亲的陪同下走上过道。她亲吻了他的脸颊，并站在罗斯身边。）*

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1989.312" duration="14.496" /> **Minister:** Friends. Family. <Note>We are gathered to celebrate here today the joyous union of Ross and Emily</Note>. <Note>May the happiness we share with them today be with them always</Note>. Now Emily, repeat after me. I, Emily...
> 朋友们。家庭我们今天在这里欢聚一堂，庆祝罗斯和艾米丽的幸福结合。愿我们今天与他们分享的幸福永远与他们同在。艾米丽，跟我说。一、 艾米丽。。。
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,倒装,正式文体">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="1989.312" duration="14.496" />

**原句**：We are gathered to celebrate here today the joyous union of Ross and Emily. May the happiness we share with them today be with them always.

**层次划分**：

- 主干①：<p class="svoc"><span class="s">We</span> <span class="v">are gathered</span> <span class="a">to celebrate... the joyous union</span></p> 被动式 are gathered——仪式文体的正式开场"我们齐聚一堂"；不定式 to celebrate 表目的
- **celebrate here today the joyous union of Ross and Emily**：宾语 the joyous union... 太长而**后移**到状语 here today 之后（长宾语后置，保持句尾重量）（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.16](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter16.md)）
- 主干②：<p class="svoc"><span class="a">May</span> <span class="s">the happiness</span> <span class="v">be</span> <span class="a">with them always</span></p> **May + 主语 + 原形动词**的祈愿句——"愿……"（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）
- **(that) we share with them today**：定语从句修饰 happiness，关系代词作 share 的宾语省略（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)）

**翻译**：今天我们欢聚于此，共庆 Ross 与 Emily 的美满结合。愿我们今天与他们共享的幸福，永远与他们同在。

**语法要点**：仪式被动式 + 长宾语后移（Ch.1）；May 祈愿倒装（Ch.22）；关系代词作宾语省略（Ch.14）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="2003.808" duration="1.08" /> **Emily:** I, Emily...
> 我，艾米丽。。。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="2004.888" duration="1.272" /> **Minster:** <Note>Take thee Ross</Note>...
> 带你去，罗斯。。。
<GrammarTip points="古英语,祈使句">

<p class="svoc"><span class="v">Take</span> <span class="o">thee Ross</span></p>

- **thee**：古英语"你"的**宾格**（主格 thou / 所有格 thy）——现代英语只剩 you，但婚礼誓词保留古语体；Take thee Ross =（我，Emily）接纳你 Ross（为妻/为夫）（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="2006.16" duration="1.272" /> **Emily:** Take thee Ross...
> 带上你，罗斯。。。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="2007.432" duration="5.64" /> **Minster:** As my lawfully wedded husband, <Note>in sickness and in health</Note>, <Note>till death parts us</Note>.
> 作为我的合法丈夫，无论疾病还是健康，直到死亡将我们分开。
<GrammarTip points="对等连接词,副词从句,主谓一致">

- **in sickness and in health**：and 连接两个对偶介词短语"无论疾病还是健康"——誓词固定对仗（[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)）
- **till death parts us**：till/until 时间从句"直到死亡把我们分开"；death 抽象名词单数 → parts（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.11](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter11.md)）
- **my lawfully wedded husband**：副词 lawfully + 过去分词 wedded 作定语"合法缔结婚姻的"（[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="2013.072" duration="5.952" /> **Emily:** As my lawfully wedded husband, in sickness and in health, until death parts us.
> 作为我的合法丈夫，无论疾病还是健康，直到死亡将我们分开。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="2019.024" duration="3.168" /> **Minster:** Now Ross, repeat after me. I Ross...
> 罗斯，跟我重复一遍。我是罗斯。。。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="2022.192" duration="1.776" /> **Ross Geller:** I Ross...
> 我是罗斯。。。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="2023.968" duration="1.44" /> **Minister:** Take thee, Emily...
> 带上你，艾米丽。。。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="2025.408" duration="5.136" /> **Ross Geller:** <Note>Take thee, Rachel</Note>...(All his friends have looks of shock on their faces. He realizes what he said. Quickly he says.) Emily. (A slight chuckle.) Emily.
> 拿你来说，瑞秋…（他所有的朋友脸上都有震惊的表情。他意识到他说的话。他很快说。）艾米丽。（微微一笑）艾米丽。
<GrammarTip points="口误,剧情炸点">

- **Take thee, Rachel**：誓词跟读中的"弗洛伊德式口误"——心里念着 Rachel，嘴里就把 Emily 顶掉了；语法上只是专名替换，剧情上却是全剧最著名的婚礼事故（语用层面的错配）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S04/S04E23.mp3" start="2030.544" duration="1.416" /> **Minister:** (Looking and feeling awkward. he looks towards Emily.) Uhh...<Note>Shall I go on</Note>?
> （看起来和感觉都很尴尬。他看着艾米丽。）啊……我可以继续吗？
<GrammarTip points="语气,短语动词">

- **Shall I go on?**：**shall I/we...?** 征求意见"要我继续吗"——shall 的本职用法；go on = 继续（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

*(Rachel looks all around as if all the eyes in the chapel were looking at her as the picture fades to black.)*
> *（瑞秋环顾四周，仿佛教堂里所有的眼睛都在看着她，因为照片逐渐变黑。）*

Closing Credits
> 片尾字幕

---

📚 **术语对照**（大陆教材 / 台湾《语法俱乐部》）：定语从句 = 关系从句；宾语从句/主语从句/表语从句 = 名词从句；状语从句 = 副词从句；介词 = 介系词；系动词 = 连缀动词；宾语补足语 = 补语；不定式 = 不定词；主谓一致 = 主语动词一致性

<GrammarTip type="pattern" points="句型总复习,动词时态">

**本集句型：现在进行式表"板上钉钉的将来"**（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）：

- **I'm going to London.** / **Rachel's coming to London.** / **Ross is getting married.**
<p class="svoc"><span class="s">Rachel</span> <span class="v">’s coming</span> <span class="a">to London</span></p>
- **I'm not leaving here.** / **I'm coming over tonight.** / **I'm not going anywhere.**
- **口诀**：来、去、结婚这类"已安排的行程"，英语用进行式说将来——时间状语（today / tonight）常跟在后面
- **对照**：be going to 表"打算/预言"，进行式表"已定行程"，本集两种将来交错出现

</GrammarTip>

<GrammarTip type="pattern" points="句型总复习,语气">

**本集句型：情态动词 + have + 过去分词——对过去的推测与懊悔**（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）：

- **懊悔**：I shouldn't have lied to him. / I shouldn't have said that. / I should never have married her.
- **推测**：You must have been a teenager (when you had him). / Anywhere half-decent would've been booked months ago.
- **口诀**：should have done = 当时该做（没做）；must have done = 当时一定是；would have done = 当时就会（没发生）

</GrammarTip>

<GrammarTip type="pattern" points="句型总复习,委婉请求">

**本集句型：礼貌请求的层层缓冲**（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）：

- **过去进行式缓冲**：I was wondering if... / I was hoping to get to know you better.
- **could/would 缓冲**：Could you bring me the newspaper? / Could we please go home now? / Would you just try to relax?
- **建议与恳求**：Why don't you come? / Shall I go on? / You might as well bring me my book.
- **口诀**：时态往后退一档（过去式/过去进行）、情态动词换委婉档（could/might/would），语气立刻软下来

</GrammarTip>

<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,本集语法地图">

**S04E23 全集语法地图**——把本集知识点放回《语法俱乐部》坐标系：

- **[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md) 基本句型**：ask people to do that（S+V+O+C）/ drive me crazy / give us the lawn ornaments（双宾语）/ slip you one / you look beautiful（S+V+C）/ There's no way in hell（There be）
- **[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md) 名词词组**：six-hour trip / a lot of Monica / one of Ross's best friends / the seven of us / a twenty（数词名词化）/ this Emily person / thousands of miles / twenty seven hundred dollars / bad luck（不可数）
- **[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md) 时态**：The flight leaves in four hours（现在式表时刻表）/ I'm going to London（进行式表将来）/ it's been three days（完成式 + 时段）/ how long have you been planning（完成进行式）/ has already pulled away / I was wondering（过去进行式表委婉）/ All this stuff I had been doing（过去完成进行式）/ just + 过去式（美式）
- **[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md) 不定词**：It could take time to get a taxi（虚主语 it）/ it was stupid to put off the wedding / be about to ruin / want you to know / tell them to swing around / nothing to discuss / nice of you to offer（of 引出逻辑主语）/ how to get to...（疑问词 + 不定词）
- **[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md) 动名词**：think of changing the name / insist on wearing / plan on doing / feel like getting up / the hall being knocked down（复合结构 + 被动）/ my friend getting married / a way of doing / keep pushing me
- **[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md) 分词**：got married / Written by（署名）/ a north-facing tree / self-absorbed / my very moving performance / be meant to see / nothing got ruined（get 被动）/ one seat left（后置定语）/ one leaving in thirty minutes（现在分词后置）
- **[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md) 形容词比较**：more elegant than / as much as the next guy / not as much as I love America / the happiest day of his life / harder than I thought / fewer bricks（可数）/ the best day ever / even dumber than I am
- **[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md) 副词**：the one time / way too much fun / not...anymore / not...yet / as it is / from now on / Out loud? / though 句尾 / horribly 叠词 horrible, horrible
- **[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md) 语气**：could take/be/leave（推测）/ this must be really hard / must have been a teenager（过去推测）/ would be speaking German if it weren't for us（虚拟）/ might as well / had better get going / Shall I go on? / Why don't you...? / don't have to ≠ must not
- **[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md) 介词·短语动词**：say "check" to sb / in the market for / get it from / take advantage of / kiss the gazebo goodbye / on a break / meet me in the middle / a piece of my mind / check this out / look out for / hook up with
- **[Ch.11](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter11.md) 主谓一致**：no one takes / everything's gonna be / the seven of us miss you / somebody...their way / there's no 'or'（引词单数）/ everyone gets along / nothing got ruined
- **[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md) 名词从句**：where the duck food is（陈述语序）/ That's not why you're going（表语从句）/ that's what they call the subway / how expensive weddings can be（感叹式）/ I don't think I have enough（否定前移）/ it seems clear that you were on a break（形式主语）/ What we did last night was...（主语从句）/ tell Ross that I love him
- **[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md) 副词从句**：when we get to London（现在式代未来）/ It's not over until someone says "I do" / if you go... you're gonna / so (that) I can listen / only if you have the hiccups / before we get started / if it weren't for us / because... / Not because...
- **[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md) 关系从句**：the way I left things / somewhere (that) we find in a day / the person with whom one wishes to speak（介词 + whom）/ the best Abbey I've ever seen / the bridesmaid I hooked up with / someone on the subway that looks just like you（分裂式）/ all the bad luck we're going to have / things where you throw it in a bag（关系副词 where）
- **[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md) 对等连接词**：yet（转折）/ now or never / in sickness and in health / came in and gave him a hug / talk, and laugh... 类排比
- **[Ch.16](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter16.md) 简化·省略**：Here you go / me neither / Got 'em / How you doin' / Rachel coming. Do...Something.（电报体）/ NO GRANDCHILDREN! / I hope he did（代动词）/ I know he does
- **[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md) 倒装·疑问**：Here comes my dad（全倒装）/ Here we go（半倒装）/ how come 不倒装 / are we? / isn't it?（附加疑问）/ Who wouldn't want you?（修辞问句）/ what the hell / She said what?（回声问句）/ May the happiness be with them（祈愿倒装）

建议：先通读剧集→按此地图回到《语法俱乐部》对应章节精读→再回剧本找例句巩固。

</GrammarTip>
